  138:         Reconcile today's production quota immediately and continue

  140:         """
  141:

  155:                 ).exception(
  156:                     "Daily autonomous production batch failed."
  157:                 )
  158: 
  159:             # State persistence prevents production above today's quota.
  160:             # Frequent checks also allow recovery after temporary
  161:             # provider/network failures.
  162:             await asyncio.sleep(
  163:                 15 * 60
  164:             )
  165: 
  166:     daily_production_task = None

  170:         and readiness.production_ready
  171:     ):

  175:         )
  176:

  135:     async def _performance_evidence(
  136:         self,
  137:     ) -> tuple[
  138:         dict | None,
  139:         dict,
  140:     ]:
  141:         """
  142:         Refresh channel analytics and build strategy evidence.
  143: 
  144:         Analytics is an optimization signal, not a production
  145:         dependency. Provider or persistence failures therefore
  146:         fail neutral and cannot block topic selection.
  147:         """
  148: 
  149:         try:
  150:             async with self.session_factory() as session:
  151:                 collection = (
  152:                     await self.performance_collector.collect(
  153:                         session,
  154:                         max_items=50,
  155:                     )
  156:                 )
  157: 
  158:                 channel_id = str(
  159:                     collection.get(
  160:                         "channel_id",
  161:                         "",
  162:                     )
  163:                 ).strip()
  164: 
  165:                 if not channel_id:
  166:                     raise ValueError(
  167:                         "Performance collection returned "
  168:                         "an empty channel ID."
  169:                     )
  170: 
  171:                 evidence = (
  172:                     await self.performance_evidence_service.build(
  173:                         session,
  174:                         channel_id,
  175:                     )
  176:                 )
  177: 
  178:             return (
  179:                 evidence,
  180:                 {
  181:                     "status": "success",
  182:                     "channel_id": channel_id,
  183:                     "collected_video_count": int(
  184:                         collection.get(
  185:                             "collected_video_count",
  186:                             0,
  187:                         )
  188:                         or 0
  189:                     ),
  190:                     "snapshot_count": int(
  191:                         collection.get(
  192:                             "snapshot_count",
  193:                             0,
  194:                         )
  195:                         or 0
  196:                     ),
  197:                     "unique_video_count": int(
  198:                         evidence.get(
  199:                             "unique_video_count",
  200:                             0,
  201:                         )
  202:                         or 0
  203:                     ),
  204:                     "eligible_video_count": int(
  205:                         evidence.get(
  206:                             "video_count",
  207:                             0,
  208:                         )
  209:                         or 0
  210:                     ),
  211:                     "total_views": int(
  212:                         evidence.get(
  213:                             "total_views",
  214:                             0,
  215:                         )
  216:                         or 0
  217:                     ),
  218:                     "confidence": float(
  219:                         evidence.get(
  220:                             "confidence",
  221:                             0.0,
  222:                         )
  223:                         or 0.0
  224:                     ),
  225:                     "strategy_ready": (
  226:                         evidence.get(
  227:                             "strategy_ready"
  228:                         )
  229:                         is True
  230:                     ),
  231:                 },
  232:             )
  233: 
  234:         except Exception as exc:
  235:             logger.warning(
  236:                 "YouTube performance analytics "
  237:                 "unavailable; continuing with neutral "
  238:                 "historical evidence: %s",
  239:                 exc,
  240:             )
  241:

  254:     async def _goal_strategy(
  255:         self,
  256:     ) -> tuple[
  257:         dict,
  258:         dict,
  259:     ]:
  260:         """
  261:         Build production strategy from active goals.
  262: 
  263:         Goals are an optimization signal, not a production
  264:         dependency. Goal persistence or evaluation failures
  265:         therefore fail neutral and cannot block topic selection.
  266: 
  267:         Goal strategy does not control scheduler cadence.
  268:         """
  269: 
  270:         try:
  271:             async with self.session_factory() as session:
  272:                 strategy = (
  273:                     await self.goal_strategy_service.build(
  274:                         session
  275:                     )
  276:                 )
  277: 
  278:             if not isinstance(strategy, dict):
  279:                 raise TypeError(
  280:                     "Goal strategy service returned "
  281:                     "a non-dictionary result."
  282:                 )
  283: 
  284:             status = str(
  285:                 strategy.get(
  286:                     "status",
  287:                     "neutral",
  288:                 )
  289:             )
  290: 
  291:             return (
  292:                 strategy,
  293:                 {
  294:                     "status": "success",
  295:                     "strategy_status": status,
  296:                     "goal_guided": (
  297:                         status == "goal_guided"
  298:                     ),
  299:                     "active_goal_count": int(
  300:                         strategy.get(
  301:                             "active_goal_count",
  302:                             0,
  303:                         )
  304:                         or 0
  305:                     ),
  306:                     "target_metric": (
  307:                         strategy.get(
  308:                             "target_metric"
  309:                         )
  310:                     ),
  311:                     "trajectory": (
  312:                         strategy.get(
  313:                             "trajectory"
  314:                         )
  315:                     ),
  316:                     "production_priority": float(
  317:                         strategy.get(
  318:                             "production_priority",
  319:                             50.0,
  320:                         )
  321:                         or 0.0
  322:                     ),
  323:                     "exploration_bias": float(
  324:                         strategy.get(
  325:                             "exploration_bias",
  326:                             0.5,
  327:                         )
  328:                         or 0.0
  329:                     ),
  330:                     "exploitation_bias": float(
  331:                         strategy.get(
  332:                             "exploitation_bias",
  333:                             0.5,
  334:                         )
  335:                         or 0.0
  336:                     ),
  337:                     "scheduler_interval_multiplier": float(
  338:                         strategy.get(
  339:                             "scheduler_interval_multiplier",
  340:                             1.0,
  341:                         )
  342:                         or 1.0
  343:                     ),
  344:                 },
  345:             )
  346: 
  347:         except Exception as exc:
  348:             logger.warning(
  349:                 "Goal production strategy unavailable; "
  350:                 "continuing with neutral goal evidence: %s",
  351:                 exc,
  352:             )
  353: 
  354:             neutral_strategy = {
  355:                 "status": "neutral",
  356:                 "active_goal_count": 0,
  357:                 "primary_goal": None,
  358:                 "target_metric": None,
  359:                 "trajectory": None,
  360:                 "urgency": 0.0,
  361:                 "production_priority": 50.0,
  362:                 "exploration_bias": 0.5,
  363:                 "exploitation_bias": 0.5,
  364:                 "scheduler_interval_multiplier": 1.0,
  365:                 "rationale": (
  366:                     "Goal strategy unavailable; production "
  367:                     "continues with neutral goal influence."
  368:                 ),
  369:             }
  370: 
  371:             return (
  372:                 neutral_strategy,

  374:                     "status": "unavailable",
  375:                     "strategy_status": "neutral",
  376:                     "goal_guided": False,
  377:                     "active_goal_count": 0,
  378:                     "target_metric": None,
  379:                     "trajectory": None,

  549:     async def _upload_video(
  550:         self,
  551:         command_id: str,
  552:         *,
  553:         video_path: str,
  554:         title: str,
  555:         description: str = "",
  556:         tags: list[str] | None = None,
  557:         privacy_status: str = "private",
  558:         category_id: str = "22",
  559:     ) -> dict:
  560:         """Upload a video through the idempotent boundary."""
  561: 
  562:         path = Path(
  563:             video_path
  564:         ).expanduser().resolve()
  565: 
  566:         if not path.is_file():
  567:             raise FileNotFoundError(
  568:                 f"Video not found: {path}"
  569:             )
  570: 
  571:         clean_title = title.strip()
  572: 
  573:         if not clean_title:
  574:             raise ValueError(
  575:                 "YouTube title cannot be empty."

  580:             .strip()

  585:             "private",
  586:             "unlisted",
  587:             "public",
  588:         }
  589: 
  590:         if privacy not in allowed_privacy:
  591:             raise ValueError(
  592:                 "privacy_status must be private, "
  593:                 "unlisted, or public."
  594:             )
  595: 
  596:         # Public publishing stays fail-closed until
  597:         # the autonomous release policy is added.
  598:         if privacy == "public":
  599:             raise ValueError(
  600:                 "Direct public YouTube upload is forbidden. "
  601:                 "Upload privately, then use the protected "
  602:                 "release operation."
  603:             )
  604: 
  605:         clean_tags = []

  609:                 tag
  610:             ).strip()
  611: 
  612:             if clean_tag:
  613:                 clean_tags.append(
  614:                     clean_tag
  615:                 )
  616:

  618:             self.publisher
  619:             .get_authorized_channel

  624:             path,
  625:         )
  626:

  643:             )
  644:         )

  105:                 str(x)
  106:                 for x in research
  107:             )
  108: 
  109:         if not research:
  110:             research = json.dumps(
  111:                 trend,
  112:                 indent=2,
  113:             )
  114: 
  115:         ####################################################
  116:         # Attempt 1
  117:         ####################################################
  118: 
  119:         raw = await self.llm.chat(
  120:             self._build_messages(
  121:                 topic=topic,
  122:                 research=research,
  123:                 format_name=format_name,
  124:                 movie_title=movie_title,
  125:                 reference_profile=reference_profile,
  126:             ),
  127:             json_mode=True,
  128:         )
  129: 
  130:         data = self._extract_json(raw)
  131: 
  132: 
  133:         ####################################################
  134:         # Attempt 2 - controlled regeneration
  135:         ####################################################
  136: 
  137:         if not self._is_valid_content(data):
  138: 
  139:             previous_words = (
  140:                 self._word_count(data)
  141:             )
  142: 
  143:             logger.warning(
  144:                 "Initial content invalid "
  145:                 "(%s words). Regenerating.",
  146:                 previous_words,
  147:             )
  148: 
  149:             retry_raw = await self.llm.chat(
  150:                 self._build_messages(
  151:                     topic=topic,
  152:                     research=research,
  153:                     previous_words=previous_words,
  154:                     retry=True,
  155:                     format_name=format_name,
  156:                     movie_title=movie_title,
  157:                     reference_profile=reference_profile,
  158:             ),
  159:                 json_mode=True,
  160:             )
  161: 
  162:             retry_data = self._extract_json(
  163:                 retry_raw
  164:             )
  165: 
  166: 
  167:             if self._is_valid_content(
  168:                 retry_data
  169:             ):
  170:                 data = retry_data
  171: 
  172:                 logger.info(
  173:                     "Content regeneration succeeded."
  174:                 )
  175: 
  176:             else:
  177:                 retry_words = self._word_count(
  178:                     retry_data
  179:                 )
  180: 
  181:                 logger.warning(
  182:                     "Content regeneration failed "
  183:                     "(%s words). Attempting grounded "
  184:                     "length recovery.",
  185:                     retry_words,
  186:                 )
  187: 
  188:                 if self._is_movie_fact_generation(
  189:                     format_name=format_name,
  190:                     reference_format=reference_format,

  194:                         await self._recover_movie_generation_length(
  195:                             data=retry_data,
  196:                             topic=topic,
  197:                             movie_title=movie_title,
  198:                             research=research,
  199:                         )
  200:                     )
  201: 
  202:                 else:
  203: 
  204:                     recovered = (
  205:                         self._recover_generation_length(
  206:                             data=retry_data,
  207:                             research=research,
  208:                         )
  209:                     )
  210: 
  211:                 if recovered is not None:
  212:                     data = recovered
  213: 
  214:                     logger.info(
  215:                         "Grounded generation length "
  216:                         "recovery succeeded (%s words).",
  217:                         self._word_count(data),
  218:                     )
  219: 
  220:                 else:
  221:                     data = {}
  222: 
  223:         ####################################################
  224:         # Emergency fallback
  225:         ####################################################
  226: 
  227:         if not data:
  228: 
  229:             logger.warning(
  230:                 "Using emergency content fallback."
  231:             )
  232: 
  233:             data = self._fallback(
  234:                 topic=topic,
  235:                 research=research,
  236:             )
  237: 
  238:         ####################################################
  239:         # Normalize
  240:         ####################################################
  241: 
  242:         title = str(
  243:             data.get(
  244:                 "title",
  245:                 topic,
  246:             )
  247:         ).strip()
  248: 
  249:         hashtags = [
  250:             str(tag).strip()
  251:             for tag in data.get(
  252:                 "hashtags",
  253:                 []
  254:             )
  255:             if str(tag).strip()
  256:         ]
  257: 
  258:         lines = [
  259:             str(line).strip()
  260:             for line in data.get(
  261:                 "script_lines",
  262:                 []
  263:             )
  264:             if str(line).strip()
  265:         ]
  266: 
  267:         if len(lines) != 4:
  268:             raise RuntimeError(
  269:                 "Generated content normalization produced "
  270:                 f"{len(lines)} script lines; expected exactly 4."
  271:             )
  272: 
  273:         lines = lines[:4]
  274: 
  275:         if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary":
  276: 
  277:             evidence_ids = data.get(
  278:                 "evidence_ids",
  279:                 [],
  280:             )
  281: 
  282:             evidence_validation = (
  283:                 self.claim_evidence_validator.validate(
  284:                     script_lines=lines,
  285:                     evidence_ids=evidence_ids,
  286:                     research=research,
  287:                 )
  288:             )
  289: 
  290:             if not evidence_validation.valid:
  291:                 raise RuntimeError(
  292:                     "Movie script failed claim-evidence validation: "
  293:                     f"{evidence_validation.issues}"
  294:                 )
  295: 
  296:         if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary":
  297: 
  298:             semantic_validation = (
  299:                 await self.semantic_claim_evidence_validator.validate(
  300:                     script_lines=lines,
  301:                     evidence_ids=data.get(
  302:                         "evidence_ids",
  303:                         [],
  304:                     ),
  305:                     research=research,
  306:                 )
  307:             )
  308: 
  309:             if not semantic_validation.valid:
  310: 
  311:                 repaired_lines = (
  312:                     await self._repair_movie_semantic_failures(
  313:                         script_lines=lines,
  314:                         evidence_ids=data.get(
  315:                             "evidence_ids",
  316:                             [],
  317:                         ),
  318:                         research=research,
  319:                         semantic_validation=semantic_validation,
  320:                         topic=topic,
  321:                         movie_title=movie_title,
  322:                     )
  323:                 )
  324: 
  325:                 if repaired_lines is None:
  326:                     raise RuntimeError(
  327:                         "Movie script failed semantic evidence "
  328:                         "validation and targeted repair failed: "
  329:                         f"{semantic_validation.to_dict()}"
  330:                     )
  331: 
  332:                 lines = repaired_lines
  333: 
  334:                 data["script_lines"] = list(
  335:                     repaired_lines
  336:                 )
  337: 
  338:                 logger.info(
  339:                     "Targeted semantic movie repair succeeded."
  340:                 )
  341: 
  342: 
  343:         ####################################################
  344:         # Final fail-closed script-length guarantee
  345:         ####################################################
  346: 
  347:         final_word_count = sum(
  348:             len(line.split())
  349:             for line in lines
  350:         )
  351: 
  352:         if not (
  353:             self.MIN_WORDS
  354:             <= final_word_count
  355:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
  356:         ):
  357:             raise RuntimeError(
  358:                 "Final generated script failed length "
  359:                 "validation after all generation and "
  360:                 "repair stages: "
  361:                 f"{final_word_count} words "
  362:                 f"(required {self.MIN_WORDS}-"
  363:                 f"{self.MAX_WORDS})."
  364:             )
  365: 
  366:         return GeneratedContent(
  367:             title=title,
  368:             hashtags=hashtags,
  369:             script_lines=lines,
  370:             metadata={
  371:                 "source": topic,
  372:                 "generator": "Jarvis Rich V1",
  373:                 "premise_audit": self.evaluate_premise_strength(topic, reference_format),
  374:                 "content_format": format_name,
  375:                 "movie_title": movie_title,
  376:                 "evidence_ids": (
  377:                     data.get("evidence_ids", [])
  378:                     if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary"
  379:                     else []
  380:                 ),
  381:                 "word_count": sum(
  382:                     len(line.split())
  383:                     for line in lines
  384:                 ),
  385:             },
  386:         )
  387:

  389:     # Content repair
  390:     ########################################################

  388:     ########################################################
  389:     # Content repair
  390:     ########################################################
  391: 
  392:     async def _recover_movie_generation_length(
  393:         self,
  394:         *,
  395:         data: dict,
  396:         topic: str,
  397:         movie_title: str,
  398:         research: str,
  399:     ) -> dict | None:
  400:         """
  401:         Recover short grounded movie-facts scripts.
  402: 
  403:         The research string may contain grounded facts without
  404:         explicit evidence labels. Recovery therefore creates a
  405:         deterministic local evidence contract from those facts.
  406: 
  407:         Validation remains fail-closed.
  408:         """
  409: 
  410:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(
  411:             data
  412:         ):
  413:             return None
  414: 
  415:         current_words = self._word_count(
  416:             data
  417:         )
  418: 
  419:         if (
  420:             self.MIN_WORDS
  421:             <= current_words
  422:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
  423:         ):
  424:             return data
  425: 
  426:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
  427:             return None
  428: 
  429:         # -------------------------------------------------
  430:         # Build deterministic evidence units.
  431:         #
  432:         # Existing labelled evidence is preserved when
  433:         # available. Otherwise grounded research sentences
  434:         # become E1, E2, ... locally for this recovery.
  435:         # -------------------------------------------------
  436: 
  437:         labelled_matches = re.findall(
  438:             r"\[(E\d+)\]\s*([^\n]+)",
  439:             research,
  440:             flags=re.IGNORECASE,
  441:         )
  442: 
  443:         evidence_map = {}
  444: 
  445:         for evidence_id, text in labelled_matches:
  446: 
  447:             normalized = (
  448:                 str(evidence_id)
  449:                 .strip()
  450:                 .upper()
  451:             )
  452: 
  453:             cleaned = " ".join(
  454:                 str(text).split()
  455:             ).strip()
  456: 
  457:             if normalized and cleaned:
  458:                 evidence_map[
  459:                     normalized
  460:                 ] = cleaned
  461: 
  462:         if not evidence_map:
  463: 
  464:             # Research supplied by EvergreenResearch currently
  465:             # arrives as grounded prose rather than E-labelled
  466:             # evidence. Split it conservatively into factual
  467:             # sentence-sized evidence units.
  468: 
  469:             raw_sentences = re.split(
  470:                 r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
  471:                 str(research),
  472:             )
  473: 
  474:             evidence_units = []
  475: 
  476:             banned_fragments = (
  477:                 '"topic"',
  478:                 '"summary"',
  479:                 '"facts"',
  480:                 '"keywords"',
  481:                 '"sources"',
  482:                 '"score"',
  483:                 '"category"',
  484:                 '"url"',
  485:                 '"confidence"',
  486:             )
  487: 
  488:             for sentence in raw_sentences:
  489: 
  490:                 cleaned = " ".join(
  491:                     sentence.split()
  492:                 ).strip()
  493: 
  494:                 if not cleaned:
  495:                     continue
  496: 
  497:                 lower = cleaned.lower()
  498: 
  499:                 if any(
  500:                     fragment in lower
  501:                     for fragment in banned_fragments
  502:                 ):
  503:                     # JSON/key scaffolding is not evidence.
  504:                     # Content following a key can still appear
  505:                     # in later sentence units.
  506:                     continue
  507: 
  508:                 words = cleaned.split()
  509: 
  510:                 if len(words) < 5:
  511:                     continue
  512: 
  513:                 if cleaned in evidence_units:
  514:                     continue
  515: 
  516:                 evidence_units.append(
  517:                     cleaned
  518:                 )
  519: 
  520:                 if len(evidence_units) >= 12:
  521:                     break
  522: 
  523:             # If the serialized research format caused the
  524:             # conservative pass to reject everything, perform
  525:             # one bounded textual extraction rather than
  526:             # inventing evidence.
  527:             if not evidence_units:
  528: 
  529:                 candidate_text = re.sub(
  530:                     r'https?://\S+',
  531:                     ' ',
  532:                     str(research),
  533:                 )
  534: 
  535:                 candidate_text = re.sub(
  536:                     r'["{}\[\],:]',
  537:                     ' ',
  538:                     candidate_text,
  539:                 )
  540: 
  541:                 candidate_text = " ".join(
  542:                     candidate_text.split()
  543:                 )
  544: 
  545:                 raw_sentences = re.split(
  546:                     r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
  547:                     candidate_text,
  548:                 )
  549: 
  550:                 for sentence in raw_sentences:
  551: 
  552:                     cleaned = " ".join(
  553:                         sentence.split()
  554:                     ).strip()
  555: 
  556:                     if len(
  557:                         cleaned.split()
  558:                     ) < 5:
  559:                         continue
  560: 
  561:                     if cleaned in evidence_units:
  562:                         continue
  563: 
  564:                     evidence_units.append(
  565:                         cleaned
  566:                     )
  567: 
  568:                     if len(evidence_units) >= 12:
  569:                         break
  570: 
  571:             for index, text in enumerate(
  572:                 evidence_units,
  573:                 start=1,
  574:             ):
  575:                 evidence_map[
  576:                     f"E{index}"
  577:                 ] = text
  578: 
  579:         if not evidence_map:
  580:             logger.warning(
  581:                 "Movie length recovery blocked: "
  582:                 "no grounded evidence units could be derived."
  583:             )
  584:             return None
  585: 
  586:         allowed_evidence_ids = list(
  587:             evidence_map.keys()
  588:         )
  589: 
  590:         allowed_evidence_set = set(
  591:             allowed_evidence_ids
  592:         )
  593: 
  594:         allowed_evidence_text = ", ".join(
  595:             allowed_evidence_ids
  596:         )
  597: 
  598:         evidence_packet = "\n".join(
  599:             f"[{evidence_id}] {text}"
  600:             for evidence_id, text
  601:             in evidence_map.items()
  602:         )
  603: 
  604:         current_candidate = data
  605: 
  606:         for attempt in range(1, 4):
  607: 
  608:             candidate_words = self._word_count(
  609:                 current_candidate
  610:             )
  611: 
  612:             current_json = json.dumps(
  613:                 current_candidate,
  614:                 indent=2,
  615:             )
  616: 
  617:             missing_words = max(
  618:                 0,
  619:                 85 - candidate_words,
  620:             )
  621: 
  622:             if attempt == 1:
  623:                 correction = f"""
  624: The current narration has {candidate_words} words.
  625: 
  626: Rewrite it to 85-100 narration words total.
  627: 
  628: Approximately {missing_words} additional useful words
  629: are needed if the script is short.
  630: 
  631: Add only explanation directly supported by the
  632: evidence packet below.
  633: """
  634:             else:
  635:                 correction = f"""
  636: THIS IS RECOVERY ATTEMPT {attempt} OF 3.
  637: 
  638: The previous candidate failed validation.
  639: 
  640: It contains {candidate_words} narration words.
  641: 
  642: Return 85-100 narration words.
  643: Use exactly four narration lines.
  644: Aim for 21-25 words per line.
  645: 
  646: Count the words before responding.
  647: Do not add filler.
  648: Do not invent facts.
  649: Use only the allowed evidence IDs.
  650: """
  651: 
  652:             messages = [
  653:                 {
  654:                     "role": "system",
  655:                     "content": f"""
  656: You are repairing a grounded movie-facts
  657: YouTube Short.
  658: 
  659: Return ONLY one JSON object.
  660: 
  661: Required keys:
  662: "title"
  663: "hashtags"
  664: "script_lines"
  665: "evidence_ids"
  666: 
  667: MANDATORY:
  668: 
  669: - exactly four script_lines
  670: - exactly four evidence_ids lists
  671: - 85-100 narration words total
  672: - no narration line over 29 words
  673: - natural spoken vertical-video narration
  674: - line 1 creates the information gap
  675: - line 2 gives only necessary context
  676: - line 3 gives the strongest supported mechanism,
  677:   example, action, contrast, number, or consequence
  678: - line 4 resolves the opening gap
  679: - every line adds new information
  680: - no generic CTA

  682: - no invented facts
  683: - evidence labels never appear inside narration
  684: 
  685: The ONLY legal evidence IDs are:
  686: 
  687: {allowed_evidence_text}
  688:

  691: 
  692: Never create another ID.
  693:

  696:                 },

 1260:     def _recover_generation_length(
 1261:         self,
 1262:         data: dict,
 1263:         research: str,
 1264:     ) -> dict | None:
 1265:         """
 1266:         Expand a structurally valid but short script using only
 1267:         complete sentences already present in supplied research.
 1268:         """
 1269: 
 1270:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(data):
 1271:             return None
 1272: 
 1273:         current_words = self._word_count(data)
 1274: 
 1275:         if self.MIN_WORDS <= current_words <= self.MAX_WORDS:
 1276:             return data
 1277: 
 1278:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1279:             return None
 1280: 
 1281:         research_text = " ".join(
 1282:             str(research).split()
 1283:         ).strip()
 1284: 
 1285:         if not research_text:
 1286:             return None
 1287: 
 1288:         sentences = [
 1289:             sentence.strip()
 1290:             for sentence in re.split(
 1291:                 r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
 1292:                 research_text,
 1293:             )
 1294:             if sentence.strip()
 1295:         ]
 1296: 
 1297:         lines = [
 1298:             str(line).strip()
 1299:             for line in data.get(
 1300:                 "script_lines",
 1301:                 []
 1302:             )
 1303:         ]
 1304: 
 1305:         if len(lines) != 4:
 1306:             return None
 1307: 
 1308:         existing_text = " ".join(lines).lower()
 1309: 
 1310:         safe_sentences = []
 1311: 
 1312:         for sentence in sentences:
 1313: 
 1314:             if len(sentence.split()) < 5:
 1315:                 continue
 1316: 
 1317:             if sentence.lower() in existing_text:
 1318:                 continue
 1319: 
 1320:             safe_sentences.append(sentence)
 1321: 
 1322:         candidate_lines = list(lines)
 1323:         total_words = current_words
 1324:         line_index = 1
 1325: 
 1326:         for sentence in safe_sentences:
 1327: 
 1328:             sentence_words = len(
 1329:                 sentence.split()
 1330:             )
 1331: 
 1332:             if (
 1333:                 total_words + sentence_words
 1334:                 > self.MAX_WORDS
 1335:             ):
 1336:                 continue
 1337: 
 1338:             base = candidate_lines[
 1339:                 line_index
 1340:             ].rstrip()
 1341: 
 1342:             if (
 1343:                 base
 1344:                 and base[-1] not in ".!?"
 1345:             ):
 1346:                 base += "."
 1347: 
 1348:             candidate_lines[
 1349:                 line_index
 1350:             ] = (
 1351:                 base
 1352:                 + " "
 1353:                 + sentence
 1354:             ).strip()
 1355: 
 1356:             total_words += sentence_words
 1357: 
 1358:             line_index += 1
 1359: 
 1360:             if line_index > 3:
 1361:                 line_index = 1
 1362: 
 1363:             if total_words >= self.MIN_WORDS:
 1364:                 break
 1365: 
 1366:         candidate = {
 1367:             **data,
 1368:             "script_lines": candidate_lines,
 1369:         }
 1370: 
 1371:         if not self._is_valid_content(
 1372:             candidate
 1373:         ):
 1374:             return None
 1375: 
 1376:         return candidate

 1644:     def _recover_repair_length(
 1645:         self,
 1646:         *,
 1647:         data: dict,
 1648:         research: str,
 1649:         banned_terms: list[str],
 1650:     ) -> dict | None:
 1651:         """
 1652:         Deterministically recover an undersized repair
 1653:         using only grounded research.
 1654: 
 1655:         Fail closed if structure, length, line limits,
 1656:         or banned-term validation cannot be satisfied.
 1657:         """
 1658: 
 1659:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(data):
 1660:             return None
 1661: 
 1662:         current_words = self._word_count(data)
 1663: 
 1664:         remaining_terms = self._remaining_flagged_terms(
 1665:             data=data,
 1666:             banned_terms=banned_terms,
 1667:         )
 1668: 
 1669:         if (
 1670:             self.MIN_WORDS
 1671:             <= current_words
 1672:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
 1673:             and not remaining_terms
 1674:         ):
 1675:             return data
 1676: 
 1677:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1678:             return None
 1679: 
 1680:         candidate = dict(data)
 1681: 
 1682:         candidate["script_lines"] = [
 1683:             str(line).strip()
 1684:             for line in data.get(
 1685:                 "script_lines",
 1686:                 [],
 1687:             )
 1688:         ]
 1689: 
 1690:         if len(candidate["script_lines"]) != 4:
 1691:             return None
 1692: 
 1693:         import re
 1694: 
 1695:         raw_sentences = re.split(
 1696:             r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
 1697:             str(research or "").strip(),
 1698:         )
 1699: 
 1700:         additions = []
 1701: 
 1702:         existing_text = " ".join(
 1703:             candidate["script_lines"]
 1704:         ).lower()
 1705: 
 1706:         for raw_sentence in raw_sentences:
 1707: 
 1708:             sentence = " ".join(
 1709:                 str(raw_sentence).split()
 1710:             ).strip()
 1711: 
 1712:             words = sentence.split()
 1713: 
 1714:             if len(words) < 5:
 1715:                 continue
 1716: 
 1717:             if len(words) > 18:
 1718:                 continue
 1719: 
 1720:             lowered = sentence.lower()
 1721: 
 1722:             if lowered in existing_text:
 1723:                 continue
 1724: 
 1725:             flagged = False
 1726: 
 1727:             for term in banned_terms:
 1728: 
 1729:                 clean_term = str(term).strip().lower()
 1730: 
 1731:                 if clean_term and clean_term in lowered:
 1732:                     flagged = True
 1733:                     break
 1734: 
 1735:             if flagged:
 1736:                 continue
 1737: 
 1738:             additions.append(sentence)
 1739: 
 1740:         target_lines = [1, 2, 3]
 1741: 
 1742:         for addition in additions:
 1743: 
 1744:             if self._word_count(candidate) >= 85:
 1745:                 break
 1746: 
 1747:             placed = False
 1748: 
 1749:             for line_index in target_lines:
 1750: 
 1751:                 current_line = candidate[
 1752:                     "script_lines"
 1753:                 ][line_index]
 1754: 
 1755:                 combined = (
 1756:                     current_line.rstrip(" .")
 1757:                     + ". "
 1758:                     + addition
 1759:                 ).strip()
 1760: 
 1761:                 if len(combined.split()) > 29:
 1762:                     continue
 1763: 
 1764:                 trial = dict(candidate)
 1765: 
 1766:                 trial["script_lines"] = list(
 1767:                     candidate["script_lines"]
 1768:                 )
 1769: 
 1770:                 trial["script_lines"][
 1771:                     line_index
 1772:                 ] = combined
 1773: 
 1774:                 trial_words = self._word_count(
 1775:                     trial
 1776:                 )
 1777: 
 1778:                 if trial_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1779:                     continue
 1780: 
 1781:                 candidate = trial
 1782:                 placed = True
 1783:                 break
 1784: 
 1785:             if not placed:
 1786:                 continue
 1787: 
 1788:         if not self._is_valid_content(candidate):
 1789:             return None
 1790: 
 1791:         final_words = self._word_count(candidate)
 1792: 
 1793:         if not (
 1794:             self.MIN_WORDS
 1795:             <= final_words
 1796:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
 1797:         ):
 1798:             return None

 1809:         ):

 2517: Never use markdown or code fences.
 2518: Never add text outside the JSON.
 2519: 
 2520: Use exactly this schema:
 2521: 
 2522: {
 2523:   "title": "string",
 2524:   "hashtags": [
 2525:     "#hashtag1",
 2526:     "#hashtag2",
 2527:     "#hashtag3"
 2528:   ],
 2529:   "script_lines": [
 2530:     "line 1",
 2531:     "line 2",
 2532:     "line 3",
 2533:     "line 4"
 2534:   ]
 2535: }
 2536: 
 2537: SCRIPT RULES:
 2538: 
 2539: 1. Exactly four narration lines.
 2540: 2. Total narration MUST be 75-105 words.
 2541: 3. Aim for 19-26 words per narration line.
 2542: 4. No narration line may exceed 29 words.
 2543: 5. Line 1 must immediately open on the exact curiosity-driving premise, recognizable subject, surprising fact, unusual mechanism, dramatic consequence, or price/value question.
 2544: 6. Never begin by defining the broad subject or category.
 2545: 7. Never begin with generic bait such as "Did you know" or "You won't believe".
 2546: 8. Line 1 must create a clear information gap.
 2547: 9. Line 2 gives only the context required to deepen that information gap.
 2548: 10. Line 3 delivers the strongest concrete supported detail, mechanism, example, number, action, contrast, or consequence.
 2549: 11. Line 4 resolves the opening information gap with a specific payoff.
 2550: 12. Every narration line must add genuinely new information.
 2551: 13. Keep the exact topic active throughout the script.
 2552: 14. Prefer concrete nouns, actions, mechanisms, numbers, names, objects, locations, and visible cause-and-effect.
 2553: 15. Remove broad history, definitions, and encyclopedia background unless essential to the exact premise.
 2554: 16. Avoid school-report narration.
 2555: 17. Avoid vague filler when concrete supported information is available.
 2556: 18. Use compact spoken sentences designed for fast vertical video.
 2557: 19. Maintain forward pressure from one line to the next.
 2558: 20. Use only information supported by the research.
 2559: 21. Never invent specificity for drama.
 2560: 22. Do not exaggerate certainty.
 2561: 23. Do not invent facts.
 2562: 24. Do not add generic calls to action.
 2563: 25. Count the narration words before responding.
 2564: 26. Verify every line is 29 words or fewer before responding.
 2565: """
 2566: 
 2567:         retry_instruction = ""
 2568: 
 2569:         if retry:
 2570: 
 2571:             retry_instruction = f"""
 2572: IMPORTANT RETRY:
 2573: 
 2574: Your previous attempt contained approximately
 2575: {previous_words} narration words and FAILED validation.
 2576: 
 2577: Rewrite the entire script.
 2578: 
 2579: The new script must contain 75-105 total narration words.
 2580: 
 2581: Keep exactly four script_lines.
 2582: 
 2583: Do not shorten the previous script.
 2584: 
 2585: Expand only with information supported by the supplied research.
 2586: 
 2587: For movie commentary, remove unsupported comparison or promotional language.
 2588: 
 2589: Do NOT use:
 2590: - groundbreaking
 2591: - cutting-edge
 2592: - Inception comparisons
 2593: - practical effects
 2594: - Eastern mysticism
 2595: 
 2596: unless those exact ideas are explicitly supported by the research.
 2597: """
 2598: 
 2599:         user_prompt = f"""

 2603: 
 2604: CONTENT FORMAT:

 2617: 
 2618: Create the final YouTube Shorts script.

  156:

  158:             candidates

  160:             else self._seed_candidates(

  164:

  201:                 item.suitability_score,
  202:             ),
  203:             reverse=True,
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:         winner = ranked[0]
  207:

   41:                 reason="goal_strategy_not_active",
   42:             )
   43: 
   44:         metric = str(strategy.get("target_metric") or "").strip().lower()
   45: 
   46:         if metric not in self.SUPPORTED_METRICS:
   47:             return self._neutral(
   48:                 reason="unsupported_goal_metric",
   49:                 target_metric=metric or None,
   50:             )
   51: 
   52:         production_priority = self._number(
   53:             strategy.get("production_priority")
   54:         )
   55:         exploitation_bias = self._number(
   56:             strategy.get("exploitation_bias")
   57:         )
   58: 
   59:         if production_priority is None or exploitation_bias is None:
   60:             return self._neutral(
   61:                 reason="invalid_goal_strategy",
   62:                 target_metric=metric,
   63:             )
   64: 
   65:         viral_score = self._candidate_viral_score(candidate)
   66: 
   67:         if viral_score is None:
   68:             return self._neutral(
   69:                 reason="candidate_signal_unavailable",
   70:                 target_metric=metric,
   71:             )
   72: 
   73:         pressure = self._clamp(
   74:             production_priority / 100.0,
   75:             0.0,
   76:             1.0,
   77:         )
   78:         exploitation = self._clamp(
   79:             exploitation_bias,
   80:             0.0,
   81:             1.0,
   82:         )
   83:         candidate_strength = self._clamp(
   84:             viral_score / 100.0,
   85:             0.0,
   86:             1.0,
   87:         )
   88: 
   89:         # Convert candidate strength from [0, 1] into [-1, 1].
   90:         #
   91:         # 100 viral score -> +1
   92:         #  50 viral score ->  0
   93:         #   0 viral score -> -1
   94:         direction = (candidate_strength - 0.5) * 2.0
   95: 
   96:         adjustment = (
   97:             direction
   98:             * self.MAX_ADJUSTMENT
   99:             * pressure
  100:             * exploitation
  101:         )
  102: 
  103:         adjustment = self._clamp(
  104:             adjustment,
  105:             -self.MAX_ADJUSTMENT,
  106:             self.MAX_ADJUSTMENT,
  107:         )
  108: 
  109:         return {
  110:             "status": "applied",
  111:             "reason": "views_goal_candidate_alignment",
  112:             "target_metric": metric,
  113:             "candidate_signal": "viral_score",
  114:             "candidate_signal_value": round(viral_score, 2),
  115:             "production_priority": round(production_priority, 2),
  116:             "exploitation_bias": round(exploitation, 3),
  117:             "adjustment": round(adjustment, 2),
  118:         }
  119:

  122:         cls,
  123:         candidate: dict[str, Any] | None,

   52:     def _provider_strength(self, trend: dict) -> float:
   53:         """
   54:         Normalize provider-specific strength.
   55: 
   56:         Provider APIs use different score scales, so raw values
   57:         must not be compared directly across providers.
   58:         """
   59: 
   60:         source = trend.get("source", "")
   61:         raw_score = max(
   62:             float(trend.get("score", 0) or 0),
   63:             0.0,
   64:         )
   65: 
   66:         if source == "YouTube":
   67:             # YouTube scores can range from single digits
   68:             # into the hundreds or thousands. Log scaling
   69:             # preserves separation without allowing huge
   70:             # values to dominate the final score.
   71:             normalized = (
   72:                 math.log1p(raw_score)
   73:                 / math.log1p(600.0)
   74:             )
   75: 
   76:         elif source == "Google News RSS":
   77:             # Current RSS provider assigns rank scores
   78:             # beginning near 80. Convert that ranking
   79:             # signal into a bounded 0-1 strength.
   80:             normalized = (
   81:                 (raw_score - 60.0)
   82:                 / 20.0
   83:             )
   84: 
   85:         elif source == "Reddit":
   86:             # RedditProvider already produces an
   87:             # approximately 0-100 opportunity score.
   88:             normalized = raw_score / 100.0
   89: 
   90:         else:
   91:             # Conservative generic fallback.
   92:             normalized = raw_score / 100.0
   93: 
   94:         normalized = min(
   95:             max(normalized, 0.0),
   96:             1.0,
   97:         )
   98: 
   99:         return round(
  100:             normalized * self.MAX_PROVIDER_SCORE,
  101:             2,
  102:         )

    1: """Daily autonomous Rich V1 production batch."""
    2: 
    3: from __future__ import annotations
    4: 
    5: import asyncio
    6: import json
    7: import logging
    8: from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
    9: from pathlib import Path
   10: from typing import Any
   11: 
   12: from backend.services.orchestration.production_scheduler import (
   13:     ProductionScheduler,
   14: )
   15: 
   16: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   17: 
   18: 
   19: class DailyProductionBatch:
   20:     """
   21:     Produce a fixed number of successful autonomous videos per day.
   22: 
   23:     State persists across application restarts.
   24: 
   25:     Failed production is bounded per supervisor pass and repeated
   26:     failure enters a cooldown so external providers and local
   27:     generation services are not hammered continuously.
   28:     """
   29: 
   30:     def __init__(
   31:         self,
   32:         scheduler: ProductionScheduler,
   33:         *,
   34:         daily_target: int = 4,
   35:         state_path: str | Path = (
   36:             "generated/state/daily_batch_state.json"
   37:         ),
   38:         attempts_per_pass: int = 2,
   39:         failure_cooldown_seconds: int = 60 * 60,
   40:     ) -> None:
   41: 
   42:         if daily_target < 1:
   43:             raise ValueError(
   44:                 "daily_target must be at least 1."
   45:             )
   46: 
   47:         if attempts_per_pass < 1:
   48:             raise ValueError(
   49:                 "attempts_per_pass must be at least 1."
   50:             )
   51: 
   52:         if failure_cooldown_seconds < 0:
   53:             raise ValueError(
   54:                 "failure_cooldown_seconds cannot be negative."
   55:             )
   56: 
   57:         self.scheduler = scheduler
   58:         self.daily_target = int(daily_target)
   59:         self.state_path = Path(state_path)
   60: 
   61:         self.attempts_per_pass = int(
   62:             attempts_per_pass
   63:         )
   64: 
   65:         self.failure_cooldown_seconds = int(
   66:             failure_cooldown_seconds
   67:         )
   68: 
   69:         self.state_path.parent.mkdir(
   70:             parents=True,
   71:             exist_ok=True,
   72:         )
   73: 
   74:         self._batch_lock = asyncio.Lock()
   75: 
   76:     async def run_daily_batch(
   77:         self,
   78:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   79: 
   80:         async with self._batch_lock:
   81: 
   82:             if not self.scheduler.enabled:
   83:                 return {
   84:                     "status": "autonomy_disabled",
   85:                     "successful": 0,
   86:                     "daily_target": self.daily_target,
   87:                 }
   88: 
   89:             today = date.today().isoformat()
   90:             state = self._load_state()
   91: 
   92:             if state.get("date") != today:
   93: 
   94:                 state = {
   95:                     "date": today,
   96:                     "successful": 0,
   97:                     "attempted": 0,
   98:                     "results": [],
   99:                     "cooldown_until": None,
  100:                 }
  101: 
  102:                 self._save_state(state)
  103: 
  104:             successful = int(
  105:                 state.get("successful", 0)
  106:             )
  107: 
  108:             if successful >= self.daily_target:
  109: 
  110:                 return {

   79:

   87:         )

   90:             cursor=cursor,

   92:         )

  126:             ################################################

   32:     def build(
   33:         self,
   34:         goals: list[dict[str, Any]] | None,
   35:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   36:         """Build one production strategy from evaluated goals."""
   37: 
   38:         source_goals = (
   39:             goals
   40:             if isinstance(goals, list)
   41:             else []
   42:         )
   43: 
   44:         active_goals = [
   45:             goal
   46:             for goal in source_goals
   47:             if (
   48:                 isinstance(goal, dict)
   49:                 and goal.get("status")
   50:                 == self.ACTIVE_STATUS
   51:                 and goal.get("trajectory")
   52:                 in self.TRAJECTORY_PRIORITY
   53:             )
   54:         ]
   55: 
   56:         if not active_goals:
   57:             return self._neutral_strategy()
   58: 
   59:         ranked = sorted(
   60:             active_goals,
   61:             key=self._goal_priority_key,
   62:             reverse=True,
   63:         )
   64: 
   65:         primary = ranked[0]
   66:

   68:             primary["trajectory"]
   69:         )
   70: 
   71:         urgency = self._urgency(
   72:             primary
   73:         )
   74: 
   75:         base_priority = (
   76:             self.TRAJECTORY_PRIORITY[
   77:                 trajectory
   78:             ]
   79:         )
   80: 
   81:         production_priority = self._clamp(
   82:             base_priority
   83:             + urgency * 15.0,
   84:             0.0,
   85:             100.0,
   86:         )
   87: 
   88:         exploitation_bias = self._clamp(

   91:             ]

   93:             0.0,
   94:             0.85,
   95:         )

   98:             1.0
   99:             - exploitation_bias

  104:             "active_goal_count": len(
  105:                 active_goals

   68: 
   69:         if (

   71:             != ProductionOperationStatus
   72:             .RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED
   73:             .value
   74:         ):
   75:             raise ValueError(
   76:                 "Only reconciliation-required operations "
   77:                 "can be reconciled."
   78:             )
   79: 
   80:         selected_reconciler = reconciler
   81: 
   82:         if selected_reconciler is None:
   83:             if (
   84:                 record.operation_type
   85:                 == "upload_video"
   86:                 and record.resource_id.startswith(
   87:                     "youtube:"
   88:                 )

  102:                     self.youtube_publish_reconciler
  103:                 )
  104: 
  105:             else:
  106:                 raise ValueError(
  107:                     "No automatic reconciler is "
  108:                     "registered for operation "
  109:                     f"{record.operation_type!r} "
  110:                     f"resource {record.resource_id!r}."
  111:                 )
  112: 
  113:         reconciliation = (
  114:             await selected_reconciler.reconcile(

  116:             resource_id=record.resource_id,
  117:             idempotency_key=record.idempotency_key,
  118:             )
  119:         )
  120: 
  121:         if (
  122:             reconciliation.status
  123:             == ReconciliationStatus
  124:             .CONFIRMED_COMPLETED
  125:         ):

  127:                 await self.operation_service.complete_reconciled(
  128:                     session,
  129:                     record,
  130:                     result={
  131:                         "reconciled": True,
  132:                         "external_id": (
  133:                             reconciliation.external_id
  134:                         ),
  135:                         "detail": (
  136:                             reconciliation.detail
  137:                         ),
  138:                     },
  139:                 )

  141:

  143:                 "status": "completed",

  149:             }
  150:

  153:             == ReconciliationStatus

  159:                     record,
  160:                     error=(
  161:                         "reconciliation_confirmed_not_found"

  165:             )
  166:

  168:                 "status": "failed",

  170:                 "record_id": failed.id,

   40:             else ProductionOperationReconciliationService()
   41:         )
   42: 
   43:     async def run(
   44:         self,
   45:         session: AsyncSession,
   46:         *,
   47:         stale_after_seconds: float,
   48:     ) -> dict:
   49:         """Recover stale operations and reconcile unresolved provider state.
   50: 
   51:         Recovery is intentionally best-effort. A provider outage must not
   52:         prevent the Jarvis application from starting.
   53:         """
   54: 
   55:         stale_records = (
   56:             await self.recovery_service
   57:             .find_stale_in_progress(
   58:                 session,
   59:                 stale_after_seconds=(
   60:                     stale_after_seconds
   61:                 ),
   62:             )
   63:         )
   64: 
   65:         recovered_count = 0
   66: 
   67:         for record in stale_records:
   68:             try:
   69:                 await (
   70:                     self.recovery_service
   71:                     .mark_reconciliation_required(
   72:                         session,
   73:                         record,
   74:                         reason=(
   75:                             "startup_stale_operation_"
   76:                             "requires_reconciliation"
   77:                         ),
   78:                     )
   79:                 )
   80: 
   81:                 recovered_count += 1
   82: 
   83:             except Exception:
   84:                 logger.exception(
   85:                     "Failed to mark stale production "
   86:                     "operation %s for reconciliation.",
   87:                     getattr(
   88:                         record,
   89:                         "id",
   90:                         "unknown",
   91:                     ),
   92:                 )
   93: 
   94:                 try:
   95:                     await session.rollback()
   96:                 except Exception:
   97:                     logger.exception(
   98:                         "Failed to rollback operation "
   99:                         "recovery transaction."
  100:                     )
  101: 
  102:         unresolved = (
  103:             await self.recovery_service
  104:             .find_reconciliation_required(
  105:                 session
  106:             )
  107:         )
  108: 
  109:         completed_count = 0
  110:         failed_count = 0
  111:         error_count = 0
  112: 
  113:         for record in unresolved:
  114:             try:
  115:                 result = (
  116:                     await self.reconciliation_service
  117:                     .reconcile(
  118:                         session,
  119:                         record,
  120:                     )
  121:                 )
  122: 
  123:                 if (
  124:                     result.get("status")
  125:                     == "completed"
  126:                 ):
  127:                     completed_count += 1
  128: 
  129:                 else:
  130:                     failed_count += 1
  131: 
  132:             except Exception:
  133:                 error_count += 1
  134: 
  135:                 logger.exception(
  136:                     "Startup reconciliation failed for "
  137:                     "production operation %s.",
  138:                     getattr(
  139:                         record,
  140:                         "id",
  141:                         "unknown",
  142:                     ),
  143:                 )
  144: 
  145:                 try:
  146:                     await session.rollback()
  147:                 except Exception:
  148:                     logger.exception(
  149:                         "Failed to rollback startup "
  150:                         "reconciliation transaction."
  151:                     )
  152: 
  153:         return {
  154:             "stale_found": len(
  155:                 stale_records
  156:             ),
  157:             "marked_for_reconciliation": (
  158:                 recovered_count
  159:             ),
  160:             "reconciliation_candidates": len(
  161:                 unresolved
  162:             ),
  163:             "reconciled_completed": (
  164:                 completed_count
  165:             ),
  166:             "reconciled_failed": (
  167:                 failed_count
  168:             ),
  169:             "reconciliation_errors": (
  170:                 error_count
  171:             ),
  172:         }

  101:     async def reclaim_failed(

   99: 
  100:     async def run_cycle(self) -> dict:
  101:         """Run one production cycle or reject if busy."""
  102: 
  103:         if self.lock.locked():
  104:             raise ProductionCycleBusyError(
  105:                 "Another production cycle is already running."
  106:             )
  107: 
  108:         await self.lock.acquire()
  109: 
  110:         cycle_id = self.cycle_id_factory()
  111: 
  112:         self._write_heartbeat(
  113:             cycle_id
  114:         )
  115: 
  116:         heartbeat_task = asyncio.create_task(
  117:             self._heartbeat_loop(
  118:                 cycle_id
  119:             ),
  120:             name=f"production-heartbeat-{cycle_id}",
  121:         )
  122: 
  123:         try:
  124: 
  125:             return await self.orchestrator.run_cycle(
  126:                 cycle_id
  127:             )
  128: 
  129:         finally:
  130: 
  131:             heartbeat_task.cancel()
  132: 
  133:             with suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
  134:                 await heartbeat_task
  135: 
  136:             with suppress(
  137:                 FileNotFoundError,
  138:                 PermissionError,
  139:             ):
  140:                 self.HEARTBEAT_PATH.unlink()
  141: 
  142:             self.lock.release()

  144:     async def run_once(self) -> dict:
  145:         """Attempt one autonomous production cycle with safe retries."""
  146: 
  147:         attempt = 1
  148: 
  149:         while True:
  150:             try:
  151:                 result = await self.runner.run_cycle()
  152: 
  153:                 failure = result.get(
  154:                     "failure"
  155:                 )
  156: 
  157:                 result_retryable = (
  158:                     isinstance(failure, dict)
  159:                     and failure.get("retryable") is True
  160:                 )
  161: 
  162:                 if (
  163:                     result_retryable
  164:                     and attempt
  165:                     < self.retry_policy.max_attempts
  166:                 ):
  167:                     delay = (
  168:                         self.retry_policy.delay_for_retry(
  169:                             attempt
  170:                         )
  171:                     )
  172: 
  173:                     logger.warning(
  174:                         "Retryable production result on "
  175:                         "attempt %s/%s. Retrying in %.2fs. "
  176:                         "Category: %s",
  177:                         attempt,
  178:                         self.retry_policy.max_attempts,
  179:                         delay,
  180:                         failure.get(
  181:                             "category",
  182:                             "unknown",
  183:                         ),
  184:                     )
  185: 
  186:                     await asyncio.sleep(
  187:                         delay
  188:                     )
  189: 
  190:                     attempt += 1
  191:                     continue
  192: 
  193:                 return result
  194: 
  195:             except ProductionCycleBusyError:
  196:                 return {
  197:                     "status": "skipped",
  198:                     "reason": "production_busy",
  199:                     "attempts": attempt,
  200:                 }
  201: 
  202:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
  203:                 raise
  204: 
  205:             except Exception as exc:
  206:                 should_retry = (
  207:                     self.retry_policy.is_retryable_exception(
  208:                         exc
  209:                     )
  210:                     and attempt
  211:                     < self.retry_policy.max_attempts
  212:                 )
  213: 
  214:                 if not should_retry:
  215:                     logger.exception(
  216:                         "Scheduled production cycle failed."
  217:                     )
  218: 
  219:                     return {
  220:                         "status": "scheduler_failed",
  221:                         "reason": "production_cycle_failed",
  222:                         "attempts": attempt,
  223:                         "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
  224:                     }
  225: 
  226:                 delay = (
  227:                     self.retry_policy.delay_for_retry(
  228:                         attempt
  229:                     )
  230:                 )
  231: 
  232:                 logger.warning(
  233:                     "Transient production failure on "
  234:                     "attempt %s/%s. Retrying in %.2fs.",
  235:                     attempt,
  236:                     self.retry_policy.max_attempts,
  237:                     delay,
  238:                 )
  239: 
  240:                 await asyncio.sleep(
  241:                     delay
  242:                 )
  243: 
  244:                 attempt += 1
