   99:         ).strip()
  100: 
  101:         genre = str(
  102:             trend.get(
  103:                 "genre",
  104:                 "",
  105:             )
  106:         ).strip()
  107: 
  108:         format_decision = (
  109:             self.format_classifier.classify(
  110:                 topic=topic_title,
  111:                 genre=genre,
  112:                 authorized_movie_source=False,
  113:             )
  114:         )
  115: 
  116:         trend["content_format"] = {
  117:             "format_name": format_decision.format_name,
  118:             "target_duration_min": format_decision.target_duration_min,
  119:             "target_duration_max": format_decision.target_duration_max,
  120:             "preferred_media": format_decision.preferred_media,
  121:             "narration_mode": format_decision.narration_mode,
  122:             "pacing": format_decision.pacing,
  123:             "rights_gate_required": format_decision.rights_gate_required,
  124:             "reference_profile": format_decision.reference_profile,
  125:         }
  126: 
  127:         ####################################################
  128:         # 3. Research topic
  129:         ####################################################
  130: 
  131:         if (
  132:             format_decision.format_name
  133:             == "famous_movie_commentary"
  134:         ):
  135: 
  136:             movie_title = (
  137:                 MovieTopicParser.extract_movie_title(
  138:                     topic_title
  139:                 )
  140:             )
  141: 
  142:             if not movie_title:
  143:                 raise RuntimeError(
  144:                     "Movie commentary topic has no recognized "
  145:                     f"movie title: {topic_title}"
  146:                 )
  147: 
  148:             trend["movie_title"] = movie_title
  149: 
  150:             knowledge_pack = await asyncio.to_thread(
  151:                     self.movie_research_service.research,
  152:                     movie_title=movie_title,
  153:                     commentary_topic=topic_title,
  154:                 )
  155: 
  156:             research_text = (
  157:                 self.movie_research_service.build_research_text(
  158:                     knowledge_pack
  159:                 )
  160:             )
  161: 
  162:         else:
  163: 
  164:             knowledge_pack = await asyncio.to_thread(
  165:                     self.research_service.research,
  166:                     topic_title,
  167:                 )
  168: 
  169:             research_text = (
  170:                 self.research_service.build_research_text(
  171:                     knowledge_pack
  172:                 )
  173:             )
  174: 
  175:         if not research_text.strip():
  176:             raise RuntimeError(
  177:                 "Research returned no usable grounded "
  178:                 f"information for: {topic_title}"
  179:             )
  180: 
  181:         trend["research"] = research_text
  182:         trend["knowledge"] = knowledge_pack.to_dict()
  183: 
  184:         ####################################################
  185:         # 4. Generate content
  186:         ####################################################
  187:         generated = await self.generator.generate(
  188:             trend
  189:         )
  190: 
  191:         if generated is None:
  192:             raise RuntimeError(
  193:                 "ContentGenerator returned None."
  194:             )
  195: 
  196:         ####################################################
  197:         # 3. Validate generated content
  198:         ####################################################
  199: 
  200:         validation = (
  201:             self.content_validator.validate(
  202:                 generated
  203:             )
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:         ####################################################
  207:         # 4. Repair rejected content
  208:         ####################################################
  209: 
  210:         if not validation["valid"]:
  211: 
  212:             repaired = await self.generator.repair(
  213:                 content=generated,
  214:                 trend=trend,
  215:                 issues=validation["issues"],
  216:             )
  217: 
  218:             if repaired is None:
  219:                 raise RuntimeError(
  220:                     "Generated content failed validation "
  221:                     "and automatic repair failed."
  222:                 )
  223: 
  224:             repaired_validation = (
  225:                 self.content_validator.validate(
  226:                     repaired
  227:                 )
  228:             )
  229: 
  230:             if not repaired_validation["valid"]:
  231:                 raise RuntimeError(
  232:                     "Repaired content still failed "
  233:                     "validation: "
  234:                     f"{repaired_validation['issues']}"
  235:                 )
  236: 
  237:             generated = repaired
  238:             validation = repaired_validation
  239: 
  240:         ####################################################
  241:         # 5. Build narration storyboard
  242:         ####################################################
  243: 
  244:         storyboard = self.storyboard.generate(
  245:             generated
  246:         )
  247: 
  248:         if not storyboard:
  249:             raise RuntimeError(
  250:                 "StoryboardGenerator produced no scenes."
  251:             )
  252: 
  253:         ####################################################
  254:         # 6. Acquire real licensed media
  255:         ####################################################
  256: 
  257:         media_groups = []
  258: 
  259:         topic = str(
  260:             trend.get(
  261:                 "title",
  262:                 "",
  263:             )
  264:         ).strip()
  265: 
  266:         genre = str(
  267:             trend.get(
  268:                 "genre",
  269:                 "",
  270:             )
  271:         ).strip()
  272: 
  273:         ####################################################
  274:         # Stage 2B - semantic visual micro-beats
  275:         #
  276:         # Narration remains four grounded story stages.
  277:         # Visuals are planned independently at a denser cadence.
  278:         ####################################################
  279: 
  280:         visual_beats = self.visual_beat_planner.plan(
  281:             content=generated,
  282:             topic=topic,
  283:             genre=genre,
  284:             format_name=(
  285:                 format_decision.format_name
  286:             ),
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:         if not visual_beats:
  290:             raise RuntimeError(
  291:                 "VisualBeatPlanner produced no visual beats."
  292:             )
  293: 
  294:         visual_beat_dicts = (
  295:             self.visual_beat_planner.to_dicts(
  296:                 visual_beats
  297:             )
  298:         )
  299: 
  300:         for index, scene in enumerate(
  301:             storyboard,
  302:             start=1,
  303:         ):
  304: 
  305:             queries = (
  306:                 self.media_query_builder.build(
  307:                     topic=topic,
  308:                     genre=genre,
  309:                     narration=scene.narration,
  310:                 )
  311:             )[:3]
  312: 
  313:             authorized_scene_assets = []
  314: 
  315:             for query in queries:
  316: 
  317:                 found = await (
  318:                     self.media_provider
  319:                     .search_and_download(
  320:                         query=query,
  321:                         content_id=content_id,
  322:                         limit=1,
  323:                     )
  324:                 )
  325: 
  326:                 if not found:
  327:                     continue
  328: 
  329:                 try:
  330:                     authorized = (
  331:                         self.asset_collector.authorize(
  332:                             found,
  333:                             content_id=content_id,
  334:                         )
  335:                     )
  336:                 except RuntimeError:
  337:                     continue
  338: 
  339:                 image_asset = next(
  340:                     (
  341:                         asset
  342:                         for asset in authorized
  343:                         if asset.asset_type
  344:                         == "image"
  345:                     ),
  346:                     None,
  347:                 )
  348: 
  349:                 if image_asset is None:
  350:                     continue
  351: 
  352:                 authorized_scene_assets.append(
  353:                     image_asset
  354:                 )
  355: 
  356:                 # Current renderer needs only one image
  357:                 # per narration scene. Stop immediately.
  358:                 break
  359: 
  360:             if not authorized_scene_assets:
  361:                 raise RuntimeError(
  362:                     "Scene "
  363:                     f"{index} produced no authorized "
  364:                     "image after limited media search. "
  365:                     f"Queries: {queries}"
  366:                 )
  367: 
  368:             media_groups.append(
  369:                 authorized_scene_assets
  370:             )
  371: 
  372:         if (
  373:             len(media_groups)
  374:             != len(storyboard)
  375:         ):
  376:             raise RuntimeError(
  377:                 "Storyboard/media group mismatch."
  378:             )
  379:         ####################################################
  380:         # Stage 2B - beat-specific authorized media
  381:         #
  382:         # IMPORTANT:
  383:         # This layer is deliberately fail-soft.
  384:         #
  385:         # Every beat has a known-good authorized fallback from
  386:         # its narration stage. A failed provider query therefore
  387:         # reduces visual specificity instead of failing the video.
  388:         ####################################################
  389: 
  390:         beat_render_assets = []
  391:         beat_media_evidence = []
  392: 
  393:         semantic_enrichment_attempts = 0
  394:         semantic_enrichment_successes = 0
  395:         semantic_fallback_count = 0
  396: 
  397:         for beat in visual_beats:
  398: 
  399:             narration_index = int(
  400:                 beat.narration_index
  401:             )
  402: 
  403:             stage_index = max(
  404:                 0,
  405:                 min(
  406:                     narration_index - 1,
  407:                     len(media_groups) - 1,
  408:                 ),
  409:             )
  410: 
  411:             fallback_asset = next(
  412:                 (
  413:                     asset
  414:                     for asset in media_groups[stage_index]
  415:                     if asset.asset_type == "image"
  416:                 ),
  417:                 None,
  418:             )
  419: 
  420:             if fallback_asset is None:
  421:                 raise RuntimeError(
  422:                     "Stage 2B fallback media missing for "
  423:                     f"narration stage {narration_index}."
  424:                 )
  425: 
  426:             selected_asset = fallback_asset
  427:             selected_via = "stage_fallback"
  428: 
  429:             query = str(
  430:                 beat.search_query
  431:             ).strip()
  432: 
  433:             if query and semantic_enrichment_attempts < self.MAX_SEMANTIC_MEDIA_ENRICHMENTS:
  434: 
  435:                 semantic_enrichment_attempts += 1
  436: 
  437:                 try:
  438: 
  439:                     found = await (
  440:                         self.media_provider
  441:                         .search_and_download(
  442:                             query=query,
  443:                             content_id=content_id,
  444:                             limit=1,
  445:                         )
  446:                     )
  447: 
  448:                     if found:
  449: 
  450:                         try:
  451: 
  452:                             authorized = (
  453:                                 self.asset_collector.authorize(
  454:                                     found,
  455:                                     content_id=content_id,
  456:                                 )
  457:                             )
  458: 
  459:                         except RuntimeError:
  460: 
  461:                             authorized = []
  462: 
  463:                         candidate = next(
  464:                             (
  465:                                 asset
  466:                                 for asset in authorized
  467:                                 if asset.asset_type == "image"
  468:                             ),
  469:                             None,
  470:                         )
  471: 
  472:                         if candidate is not None:
  473: 
  474:                             selected_asset = candidate
  475:                             selected_via = "semantic_query"
  476:                             semantic_enrichment_successes += 1
  477: 
  478:                 except Exception:
  479: 
  480:                     # Beat enrichment must never make a valid
  481:                     # production fail. The original authorized
  482:                     # scene asset remains the fallback.
  483:                     selected_asset = fallback_asset
  484:                     selected_via = "stage_fallback"
  485: 
  486:             if selected_via == "stage_fallback":
  487:                 semantic_fallback_count += 1
  488: 
  489:             beat_render_assets.append(
  490:                 selected_asset
  491:             )
  492: 
  493:             beat_media_evidence.append(
  494:                 {
  495:                     "beat_id": beat.beat_id,
  496:                     "narration_index": narration_index,
  497:                     "purpose": beat.purpose,
  498:                     "query": query,
  499:                     "selected_via": selected_via,
  500:                     "asset_id": selected_asset.asset_id,
  501:                     "source_name": selected_asset.source_name,
  502:                     "source_url": selected_asset.source_url,
  503:                     "license_name": selected_asset.license_name,
  504:                     "commercial_use_allowed": (
  505:                         selected_asset.commercial_use_allowed
  506:                     ),
  507:                 }
  508:             )
  509: 
  510:         if (
  511:             len(beat_render_assets)
  512:             != len(visual_beats)
  513:         ):
  514:             raise RuntimeError(
  515:                 "Visual beat/media timeline mismatch."
  516:             )
  517: 
  518:         ####################################################
  519:         # 7. Temporary renderer compatibility
  520:         #
  521:         # The existing renderer still accepts one image
  522:         # object per narration scene. For this integration
  523:         # test we pass the strongest authorized IMAGE from
  524:         # each group.
  525:         #
  526:         # The next renderer patch will consume the complete
  527:         # media_groups structure and produce multiple cuts.
  528:         ####################################################
  529: 
  530:         render_assets = []
  531: 
  532:         for index, group in enumerate(
  533:             media_groups,
  534:             start=1,
  535:         ):
  536: 
  537:             image_asset = next(
  538:                 (
  539:                     asset
  540:                     for asset in group
  541:                     if asset.asset_type
  542:                     == "image"
  543:                 ),
  544:                 None,
  545:             )
  546: 
  547:             if image_asset is None:
  548:                 raise RuntimeError(
  549:                     "Scene "
  550:                     f"{index} has no image-compatible "
  551:                     "asset for the current renderer."
  552:                 )
  553: 
  554:             render_assets.append(
  555:                 image_asset
  556:             )
  557: 
  558:         ####################################################
  559:         # 8. Build production package
  560:         ####################################################
  561: 
  562:         package = await self.package_builder.build(
  563:             content=generated,
  564:             scenes=storyboard,
  565:             images=render_assets,
  566:             trend=trend,
  567:         )
  568: 
  569:         ####################################################
  570:         # 9. Render
  571:         ####################################################
  572: 
  573:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_BEGIN
  574:         #
  575:         # The previous Stage2B implementation could legally
  576:         # resolve many semantic beats to the same authorized
  577:         # fallback image. That produced a technically valid
  578:         # 12-cut video which visually behaved like a slideshow.
  579:         #
  580:         # Rights authorization has already happened upstream.
  581:         # This gate does not authorize media and does not weaken
  582:         # any rights/evidence requirement. It only evaluates the
  583:         # final authorized beat sequence before expensive render.
  584: 
  585:         def _block5_asset_identity(asset):
  586:             if asset is None:
  587:                 return ""
  588: 
  589:             for attribute in (
  590:                 "image_path",
  591:                 "file_path",
  592:                 "local_path",
  593:                 "path",
  594:                 "source_url",
  595:                 "asset_id",
  596:             ):
  597:                 value = getattr(
  598:                     asset,
  599:                     attribute,
  600:                     None,
  601:                 )
  602: 
  603:                 if value:
  604:                     return str(value).strip().lower()
  605: 
  606:             if isinstance(asset, dict):
  607:                 for key in (
  608:                     "image_path",
  609:                     "file_path",
  610:                     "local_path",
  611:                     "path",
  612:                     "source_url",
  613:                     "asset_id",
  614:                 ):
  615:                     value = asset.get(key)
  616: 
  617:                     if value:
  618:                         return str(value).strip().lower()
  619: 
  620:             return str(asset).strip().lower()
  621: 
  622:         block5_identities = [
  623:             _block5_asset_identity(asset)
  624:             for asset in beat_render_assets
  625:         ]
  626: 
  627:         if (
  628:             not block5_identities
  629:             or len(block5_identities)
  630:             != len(visual_beat_dicts)
  631:         ):
  632:             raise RuntimeError(
  633:                 "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  634:                 "beat/image coverage mismatch."
  635:             )
  636: 
  637:         block5_counts = {}
  638: 
  639:         for identity in block5_identities:
  640:             if not identity:
  641:                 raise RuntimeError(
  642:                     "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  643:                     "unidentifiable authorized asset."
  644:                 )
  645: 
  646:             block5_counts[identity] = (
  647:                 block5_counts.get(identity, 0)
  648:                 + 1
  649:             )
  650: 
  651:         block5_distinct_assets = len(
  652:             block5_counts
  653:         )
  654: 
  655:         block5_largest_share = (
  656:             max(block5_counts.values())
  657:             / len(block5_identities)
  658:         )
  659: 
  660:         block5_max_repeat_run = 1
  661:         block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  662: 
  663:         for block5_index in range(
  664:             1,
  665:             len(block5_identities),
  666:         ):
  667:             if (
  668:                 block5_identities[block5_index]
  669:                 == block5_identities[
  670:                     block5_index - 1
  671:                 ]
  672:             ):
  673:                 block5_current_repeat_run += 1
  674:                 block5_max_repeat_run = max(
  675:                     block5_max_repeat_run,
  676:                     block5_current_repeat_run,
  677:                 )
  678:             else:
  679:                 block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  680: 
  681:         block5_minimum_distinct = min(
  682:             4,
  683:             len(block5_identities),
  684:         )
  685: 
  686:         if (
  687:             block5_distinct_assets
  688:             < block5_minimum_distinct
  689:         ):
  690:             raise RuntimeError(
  691:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  692:                 f"{block5_distinct_assets} distinct assets "
  693:                 f"for {len(block5_identities)} beats; "
  694:                 f"minimum is {block5_minimum_distinct}."
  695:             )
  696: 
  697:         if block5_max_repeat_run > 2:
  698:             raise RuntimeError(
  699:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  700:                 f"same asset appears for "
  701:                 f"{block5_max_repeat_run} consecutive beats; "
  702:                 "maximum is 2."
  703:             )
  704: 
  705:         if (
  706:             len(block5_identities) >= 8
  707:             and block5_largest_share > 0.42
  708:         ):
  709:             raise RuntimeError(
  710:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  711:                 "one asset occupies "
  712:                 f"{block5_largest_share:.0%} of semantic beats; "
  713:                 "maximum is 42%."
  714:             )
  715: 
  716:         block5_visual_metrics = {
  717:             "distinct_assets": (
  718:                 block5_distinct_assets
  719:             ),
  720:             "total_beats": (
  721:                 len(block5_identities)
  722:             ),
  723:             "largest_asset_share": round(
  724:                 block5_largest_share,
  725:                 4,
  726:             ),
  727:             "max_consecutive_identical": (
  728:                 block5_max_repeat_run
  729:             ),
  730:             "quality_gate": "passed",
  731:         }
  732: 
  733:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_END
  734: 
  735:         video = await self.renderer.render(
  736:             content=generated,
  737:             scenes=storyboard,
  738:             images=render_assets,
  739:             production_package=package,
  740:             visual_beats=visual_beat_dicts,
  741:             beat_images=beat_render_assets,
  742:         )
  743: 
  744:         ####################################################
  745:         # 10. Return production evidence
  746:         ####################################################
  747: 
  748:         return {
  749:             "generated": generated,
  750:             "content_validation": validation,
  751:             "storyboard": storyboard,
  752:             "licensed_media": [
  753:                 [
  754:                     {
  755:                         "asset_id": asset.asset_id,
  756:                         "asset_type": asset.asset_type,
  757:                         "file_path": asset.file_path,
  758:                         "source_url": asset.source_url,
  759:                         "source_name": asset.source_name,
  760:                         "creator": asset.creator,
  761:                         "license_name": asset.license_name,
  762:                         "license_url": asset.license_url,
  763:                         "attribution_required": (
  764:                             asset.attribution_required
  765:                         ),
  766:                         "commercial_use_allowed": (
  767:                             asset.commercial_use_allowed
  768:                         ),
  769:                         "relevance_score": (
  770:                             asset.relevance_score
  771:                         ),
  772:                         "content_id": asset.content_id,
  773:                     }
  774:                     for asset in group
  775:                 ]
  776:                 for group in media_groups
  777:             ],
  778:             "production_package": package,
  779:             "visual_beats": visual_beat_dicts,
  780:             "beat_media": beat_media_evidence,
  781:             "semantic_media_metrics": {
  782:                 "enrichment_budget": self.MAX_SEMANTIC_MEDIA_ENRICHMENTS,
  783:                 "enrichment_attempts": semantic_enrichment_attempts,
  784:                 "enrichment_successes": semantic_enrichment_successes,
  785:                 "fallback_count": semantic_fallback_count,
  786:                 "total_beats": len(visual_beats),
  787:                 "visual_quality": block5_visual_metrics,
  788:             },
  789:             "video": video,
  790:             "status": "success",
  791:         }
  792:

  795:

   27:         if not cycle_id:

   29:                 "cycle_id cannot be empty."

   32:         existing = await self.get_cycle(

--- ProductionCycleService.recover_stale_cycles @ line 218 ---
  214:         await session.refresh(cycle)

  217: 
  218:     async def recover_stale_cycles(
  219:         self,
  220:         session: AsyncSession,
  221:         *,
  222:         stale_after: timedelta,
  223:         now: datetime | None = None,
  224:     ) -> list[ProductionCycleRecord]:
  225:         """Mark abandoned STARTED cycles as FAILED."""
  226: 
  227:         if stale_after.total_seconds() <= 0:
  228:             raise ValueError(
  229:                 "stale_after must be greater than zero."
  230:             )
  231: 
  232:         recovery_time = now or self._utc_now()
  233:         cutoff = recovery_time - stale_after
  234: 
  235:         statement = (
  236:             select(
  237:                 ProductionCycleRecord
  238:             )
  239:             .where(
  240:                 ProductionCycleRecord.status
  241:                 == ProductionCycleStatus.STARTED
  242:             )
  243:             .where(
  244:                 ProductionCycleRecord.started_at
  245:                 <= cutoff
  246:             )
  247:             .order_by(
  248:                 ProductionCycleRecord.started_at.asc()
  249:             )
  250:         )
  251: 
  252:         query_result = await session.execute(
  253:             statement
  254:         )
  255: 
  256:         stale_cycles = list(
  257:             query_result.scalars().all()
  258:         )
  259: 
  260:         for cycle in stale_cycles:
  261:             cycle.status = (
  262:                 ProductionCycleStatus.FAILED
  263:             )
  264:             cycle.completed_at = recovery_time
  265:             cycle.result = json.dumps(
  266:                 {
  267:                     "cycle_id": cycle.id,
  268:                     "status": "orchestration_failed",
  269:                     "error": "stale_cycle_recovered",
  270:                     "recovery": {
  271:                         "reason": (
  272:                             "Production cycle remained "
  273:                             "STARTED beyond the allowed "
  274:                             "stale interval."
  275:                         ),
  276:                         "stale_after_seconds": (
  277:                             stale_after.total_seconds()
  278:                         ),
  279:                         "recovered_at": (
  280:                             recovery_time.isoformat()
  281:                         ),
  282:                     },
  283:                 },
  284:                 ensure_ascii=False,
  285:             )
  286: 
  287:         if stale_cycles:
  288:             await session.commit()
  289: 
  290:             for cycle in stale_cycles:
  291:                 await session.refresh(
  292:                     cycle
  293:                 )
  294: 
  295:         return stale_cycles
  296: 
  297:     @staticmethod

   36: 
   37:     def score(
   38:         self,
   39:         topic: str,
   40:         sources: list[dict],
   41:     ) -> dict:
   42:         """Return total confidence and component scores."""
   43: 
   44:         if not sources:
   45:             return {
   46:                 "score": 0.0,
   47:                 "relevance": 0.0,
   48:                 "diversity": 0.0,
   49:                 "provider_confidence": 0.0,
   50:                 "evidence": 0.0,
   51:             }
   52: 
   53:         relevance = self._relevance(
   54:             topic,
   55:             sources,
   56:         )
   57: 
   58:         diversity = self._diversity(
   59:             sources
   60:         )
   61: 
   62:         provider_confidence = (
   63:             self._provider_confidence(
   64:                 sources
   65:             )
   66:         )
   67: 
   68:         evidence = self._evidence(
   69:             sources
   70:         )
   71: 
   72:         # Relevance is the confidence gate.
   73:         #
   74:         # Independent publishers and substantial evidence
   75:         # only strengthen confidence when those sources are
   76:         # actually about the researched topic.
   77:         relevance_ratio = min(
   78:             max(
   79:                 relevance / 40.0,
   80:                 0.0,
   81:             ),
   82:             1.0,
   83:         )
   84: 
   85:         supporting_score = (
   86:             diversity
   87:             + provider_confidence
   88:             + evidence
   89:         )
   90: 
   91:         total = (
   92:             relevance
   93:             + (
   94:                 supporting_score
   95:                 * relevance_ratio
   96:             )
   97:         )
   98: 
   99:         return {
  100:             "score": round(
  101:                 min(
  102:                     max(total, 0.0),
  103:                     self.MAX_SCORE,
  104:                 ),
  105:                 2,
  106:             ),
  107:             "relevance": relevance,
  108:             "diversity": diversity,
  109:             "provider_confidence": (
  110:                 provider_confidence
  111:             ),
  112:             "evidence": evidence,
  113:             "relevance_ratio": round(
  114:                 relevance_ratio,
  115:                 3,
  116:             ),
  117:         }
  118:

  120:         self,
  121:         topic: str,
  122:         sources: list[dict],


--- ResearchConfidenceScorer._relevance @ line 119 ---
  115:                 3,
  116:             ),
  117:         }
  118: 
  119:     def _relevance(
  120:         self,
  121:         topic: str,
  122:         sources: list[dict],
  123:     ) -> float:
  124:         """
  125:         Measure how strongly result headlines overlap
  126:         with meaningful terms from the research topic.
  127:         """
  128: 
  129:         topic_terms = self._terms(topic)
  130: 
  131:         if not topic_terms:
  132:             return 0.0
  133: 
  134:         similarities = []
  135: 
  136:         for source in sources:
  137:             title_terms = self._terms(
  138:                 str(
  139:                     source.get(
  140:                         "title",
  141:                         "",
  142:                     )
  143:                     or ""
  144:                 )
  145:             )
  146: 
  147:             if not title_terms:
  148:                 similarities.append(0.0)
  149:                 continue
  150: 
  151:             overlap = (
  152:                 topic_terms
  153:                 & title_terms
  154:             )
  155: 
  156:             similarities.append(
  157:                 len(overlap)
  158:                 / len(topic_terms)
  159:             )
  160: 
  161:         average = (
  162:             sum(similarities)
  163:             / len(similarities)
  164:         )
  165: 
  166:         return round(
  167:             min(
  168:                 average * 40.0,
  169:                 40.0,
  170:             ),
  171:             2,
  172:         )
  173: 
  174:     @staticmethod
  175:     def _diversity(
  176:         sources: list[dict],
  177:     ) -> float:

  247:         )

  249:     @staticmethod
  250:     def _evidence(
  251:         sources: list[dict],
  252:     ) -> float:
  253:         """
  254:         Reward sources containing usable research text,
  255:         rather than empty search-result shells.
  256:         """
  257: 
  258:         usable = 0
  259: 
  260:         for source in sources:
  261:             content = str(
  262:                 source.get(
  263:                     "content",
  264:                     "",
  265:                 )
  266:                 or ""
  267:             ).strip()
  268: 
  269:             if len(content) >= 40:
  270:                 usable += 1
  271: 
  272:         return round(
  273:             min(
  274:                 usable / 4.0,
  275:                 1.0,
  276:             )
  277:             * 20.0,
  278:             2,
  279:         )

  186:                     continue
  187: 
  188:                 if url:
  189:                     seen_urls.add(url)
  190: 
  191:                 pack.add_source(
  192:                     result
  193:                 )
  194: 
  195:                 content = str(
  196:                     result.get(
  197:                         "content",
  198:                         "",
  199:                     )
  200:                 ).strip()
  201: 
  202:                 if content:
  203:                     pack.add_fact(
  204:                         content
  205:                     )
  206:                     all_content.append(
  207:                         content
  208:                     )
  209: 
  210:             # Two good background sources are enough
  211:             # for the first Rich V1 integration test.
  212:             if len(pack.sources) >= 2:
  213:                 break
  214: 
  215:         if all_content:
  216:             pack.set_summary(
  217:                 " ".join(
  218:                     all_content
  219:                 )[:6000]
  220:             )
  221: 
  222:         pack.category = "Evergreen"
  223: 
  224:         confidence = (
  225:             self.confidence.score(
  226:                 clean_query,
  227:                 pack.sources,
  228:             )
  229:         )
  230: 
  231:         pack.score = float(
  232:             confidence.get(
  233:                 "score",
  234:                 0.0,
  235:             )
  236:             or 0.0
  237:         )
  238: 
  239:         self.memory.save(
  240:             clean_query,
  241:             pack,
  242:         )
  243: 
  244:         return pack
  245:

  247:     def _research_queries(
  248:         self,
  249:         topic: str,
  250:     ) -> list[str]:
  251:         clean = " ".join(
  252:             str(topic).split()
  253:         ).strip()
  254: 
  255:         if not clean:
  256:             return []
  257: 
  258:         lower = clean.lower()
  259: 
  260:         exact_queries: list[str] = [clean]
  261:         concept_queries: list[str] = []
  262:         alias_queries: list[str] = []
  263: 
  264:         try:
  265:             concept_queries = list(
  266:                 self._expand_concept_queries(clean)
  267:                 or []
  268:             )
  269:         except Exception:
  270:             concept_queries = []
  271: 
  272:         for trigger, aliases in self.RESEARCH_ALIASES.items():
  273:             if trigger in lower:
  274:                 alias_queries.extend(
  275:                     str(alias).strip()
  276:                     for alias in aliases
  277:                     if str(alias).strip()
  278:                 )
  279: 
  280:         # The exact premise must remain the highest-priority
  281:         # research request. Previously aliases could replace it
  282:         # completely, which encouraged broad background evidence.
  283:         ordered = (
  284:             exact_queries
  285:             + concept_queries
  286:             + alias_queries
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:         generic_terms = {
  290:             "film",
  291:             "filmmaking",
  292:             "cinema",
  293:             "technology",
  294:             "science",
  295:             "engineering",
  296:             "business",
  297:             "history",
  298:             "movie",
  299:         }
  300: 
  301:         topic_tokens = {
  302:             token.strip(
  303:                 ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  304:             ).lower()
  305:             for token in clean.split()
  306:             if len(
  307:                 token.strip(
  308:                     ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  309:                 )
  310:             ) >= 4
  311:         }
  312: 
  313:         def query_quality(query: str) -> tuple:
  314:             normalized = " ".join(
  315:                 str(query).split()
  316:             ).strip()
  317: 
  318:             tokens = {
  319:                 token.strip(
  320:                     ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  321:                 ).lower()
  322:                 for token in normalized.split()
  323:                 if len(
  324:                     token.strip(
  325:                         ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  326:                     )
  327:                 ) >= 4
  328:             }
  329: 
  330:             overlap = len(
  331:                 tokens & topic_tokens
  332:             )
  333: 
  334:             exact = int(
  335:                 normalized.lower()
  336:                 == clean.lower()
  337:             )
  338: 
  339:             generic_only = int(
  340:                 bool(tokens)
  341:                 and tokens.issubset(generic_terms)
  342:             )
  343: 
  344:             return (
  345:                 exact,
  346:                 overlap,
  347:                 -generic_only,
  348:                 min(len(tokens), 12),
  349:             )
  350: 
  351:         seen: set[str] = set()
  352:         unique: list[str] = []
  353: 
  354:         for query in ordered:
  355:             normalized = " ".join(
  356:                 str(query).split()
  357:             ).strip()
  358: 
  359:             if not normalized:
  360:                 continue
  361: 
  362:             key = normalized.casefold()
  363: 
  364:             if key in seen:
  365:                 continue
  366: 
  367:             seen.add(key)
  368:             unique.append(normalized)
  369: 
  370:         if not unique:
  371:             return [clean]
  372: 
  373:         exact = unique[0]
  374: 
  375:         remainder = sorted(
  376:             unique[1:],
  377:             key=query_quality,
  378:             reverse=True,
  379:         )
  380: 
  381:         # Keep research bounded. Exact premise + up to four
  382:         # supporting concept/alias queries.
  383:         return [exact] + remainder[:4]

  385:     @staticmethod
  386:     def _expand_concept_queries(
  387:         topic: str,
  388:     ) -> list[str]:
  389:         """
  390:         Convert a storytelling hook into a small ordered set
  391:         of encyclopedia-style research concepts.
  392: 
  393:         Ordering matters:
  394:         specific explanatory concepts come before broad
  395:         background concepts.
  396:         """
  397: 
  398:         clean = " ".join(
  399:             str(topic).split()
  400:         ).strip()
  401: 
  402:         if not clean:
  403:             return []
  404: 
  405:         lower = clean.lower()
  406: 
  407:         queries: list[str] = []
  408: 
  409:         def add(value: str) -> None:
  410:             value = " ".join(
  411:                 str(value).split()
  412:             ).strip()
  413: 
  414:             if not value:
  415:                 return
  416: 
  417:             existing = {
  418:                 item.casefold()
  419:                 for item in queries
  420:             }
  421: 
  422:             if value.casefold() not in existing:
  423:                 queries.append(value)
  424: 
  425:         # ------------------------------------------
  426:         # High-value explanatory concepts.
  427:         #
  428:         # These are domain concepts, not mappings
  429:         # for one exact YouTube title.
  430:         # ------------------------------------------
  431: 
  432:         if (
  433:             "time zone" in lower
  434:             or "time zones" in lower
  435:         ):
  436:             add("Time zone")
  437:             add("Standard time")
  438:             add("Railway time")
  439: 
  440:         if (
  441:             "sell" in lower
  442:             and "loss" in lower
  443:         ):
  444:             add("Loss leader")
  445:             add("Pricing")
  446:             add("Business model")
  447:

  449:             "silence" in lower

  662:         words = re.findall(
  663:             r"[A-Za-z0-9'-]+",

  667:         keywords = [
  668:             word
  669:             for word in words

  671:                 len(word) >= 4
  672:                 and word.casefold()

  674:             )
  675:         ]

  677:         for word in keywords:
  678:             add(word.title())

  683:         # Original hook remains the final fallback only.
  684:         add(clean)
  685: 
  686:         return queries[:6]

  688:     @staticmethod
  689:     def build_research_text(
  690:         pack: KnowledgePack,
  691:     ) -> str:
  692: 
  693:         parts: list[str] = []
  694: 
  695:         if pack.summary:
  696:             parts.append(
  697:                 pack.summary.strip()
  698:             )
  699: 
  700:         for fact in pack.facts:

  702:             clean = str(
  703:                 fact
  704:             ).strip()
  705: 
  706:             if (
  707:                 clean
  708:                 and clean not in parts
  709:             ):
  710:                 parts.append(clean)
  711: 
  712:         return "\n\n".join(parts)

   67:         self,

   72:

   74:             str(topic).split()
   75:         ).strip()
   76: 
   77:         if not clean_topic:
   78:             return []
   79: 
   80:         queries = self._queries(
   81:             clean_topic

   83: 
   84:         positive_prompts = [
   85:             f"{clean_topic} product close up",
   86:             f"{clean_topic} luxury product shot",
   87:             f"{clean_topic} macro detail",

  106:             str,
  107:             LuxurySourceLead,
  108:         ] = {}
  109: 
  110:         for query in queries:
  111: 
  112:             candidates = self.youtube.search(
  113:                 query=query,
  114:                 limit=8,
  115:             )
  116: 
  117:             for candidate in candidates:
  118:

  120:                     self.verifier.verify(
  121:                         thumbnail_url=(
  122:                             candidate.thumbnail_url
  123:                         ),
  124:                         positive_prompts=(
  125:                             positive_prompts
  126:                         ),
  127:                         negative_prompts=(
  128:                             negative_prompts
  129:                         ),
  130:                     )
  131:                 )
  132: 
  133:                 if not verification.valid:
  134:                     continue
  135: 
  136:                 if (
  137:                     verification.positive_score
  138:                     < self.MIN_VISUAL_POSITIVE
  139:                 ):
  140:                     continue
  141: 
  142:                 if (
  143:                     verification.margin
  144:                     < self.MIN_VISUAL_MARGIN
  145:                 ):
  146:                     continue
  147: 
  148:                 source_type = (
  149:                     self._source_type(
  150:                         candidate.authority_score
  151:                     )

  154:                 visual_score = min(
  155:                     100.0,

  159:                     )
  160:                     + (
  161:                         max(

  163:                             0.0,
  164:                         )
  165:                         * 100.0
  166:                     ),
  167:                 )
  168: 
  169:                 final_score = round(
  170:                     candidate.discovery_score
  171:                     if hasattr(
  172:                         candidate,
  173:                         "discovery_score",
  174:                     )

  179:                 final_score = round(

  181:                     + candidate.authority_score * 0.30

  226:                     lead.video_id

  230:                     previous is None

--- MovieResearchService._extract_relevant_sentences @ line 248 ---

  247:     @staticmethod
  248:     def _extract_relevant_sentences(
  249:         text: str,
  250:         terms: set[str],
  251:     ) -> list[str]:
  252: 
  253:         if not text.strip():
  254:             return []
  255: 
  256:         sentences = [
  257:             sentence.strip()
  258:             for sentence in re.split(
  259:                 r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
  260:                 " ".join(
  261:                     text.split()
  262:                 ),
  263:             )
  264:             if sentence.strip()
  265:         ]
  266: 
  267:         scored = []
  268: 
  269:         for sentence in sentences:
  270: 
  271:             lower = sentence.lower()
  272: 
  273:             matches = sum(
  274:                 1
  275:                 for term in terms
  276:                 if term in lower
  277:             )
  278: 
  279:             if matches <= 0:
  280:                 continue
  281: 
  282:             scored.append(
  283:                 (
  284:                     matches,
  285:                     len(sentence),
  286:                     sentence,
  287:                 )
  288:             )
  289: 
  290:         scored.sort(
  291:             key=lambda item: (
  292:                 item[0],
  293:                 item[1],
  294:             ),
  295:             reverse=True,
  296:         )
  297: 
  298:         output = []
  299:         seen = set()
  300: 
  301:         for _, _, sentence in scored:
  302: 
  303:             key = sentence.lower()
  304: 
  305:             if key in seen:
  306:                 continue
  307: 
  308:             seen.add(key)
  309:             output.append(sentence)
  310: 
  311:         return output

  160:

  188:             )

  191:                 "www."
  192:             ):

  198:                     title=title,
  199:                     snippet=snippet,
  200:                 )
  201:             )

  207:                     snippet=snippet,
  208:                 )
  209:             )
  210:

  212:                 authority * 0.55
  213:                 + relevance * 0.45,
  214:                 2,
  215:             )
  216:

  218:                 GoogleWebCandidate(
  219:                     title=title,

--- GoogleDiscoveryProvider._relevance_score @ line 298 ---
  294:             2,
  295:         )
  296: 
  297:     @staticmethod
  298:     def _relevance_score(
  299:         *,
  300:         query: str,
  301:         title: str,
  302:         snippet: str,
  303:     ) -> float:
  304: 
  305:         query_words = {
  306:             word
  307:             for word in query.lower().split()
  308:             if len(word) >= 3
  309:         }
  310: 
  311:         if not query_words:
  312:             return 0.0
  313: 
  314:         title_words = set(
  315:             title.lower().split()
  316:         )
  317: 
  318:         snippet_words = set(
  319:             snippet.lower().split()
  320:         )
  321: 
  322:         title_overlap = len(
  323:             query_words.intersection(
  324:                 title_words
  325:             )
  326:         )
  327: 
  328:         snippet_overlap = len(
  329:             query_words.intersection(
  330:                 snippet_words
  331:             )
  332:         )
  333: 
  334:         score = (
  335:             (
  336:                 title_overlap
  337:                 / len(query_words)
  338:             )
  339:             * 70.0
  340:             +
  341:             (
  342:                 snippet_overlap
  343:                 / len(query_words)
  344:             )
  345:             * 30.0
  346:         )
  347: 
  348:         return round(
  349:             min(
  350:                 score,
  351:                 100.0,
  352:             ),
  353:             2,
  354:         )

   28:             return []
   29:

   32:             f"?q={self._encode_query(topic)}"

   48:             },

   59:                 xml_data
   60:             )
   61:

   66:             )

   76:                 item.find("link")
   77:             )

   81:                 )
   82:             )

   84:                 item.find("pubDate")
   85:             )

   87:             if not title:
   88:                 continue
   89: 
   90:             publisher = self._extract_publisher(
   91:                 title
   92:             )
   93: 
   94:             headline = self._extract_headline(
   95:                 title
   96:             )
   97: 
   98:             content_parts = []

  101:                 content_parts.append(description)
  102: 
  103:             if published:
  104:                 content_parts.append(
  105:                     f"Published: {published}"
  106:                 )
  107: 
  108:             results.append(
  109:                 {

  112:                         content_parts

  409:     @classmethod
  410:     def _authority_score(
  411:         cls,
  412:         *,
  413:         title: str,
  414:         channel_title: str,
  415:     ) -> float:
  416: 
  417:         channel = " ".join(
  418:             str(
  419:                 channel_title
  420:             ).lower().split()
  421:         )
  422: 
  423:         title_lower = (
  424:             str(title).lower()
  425:         )
  426: 
  427:         score = 35.0
  428: 
  429:         ####################################################
  430:         # Authority must come primarily from the CHANNEL,
  431:         # not from a brand name appearing in the title.
  432:         ####################################################
  433: 
  434:         exact_high_authority = {
  435:             "rolex",
  436:             "nasa",
  437:             "boeing",
  438:             "airbus",
  439:             "apple",
  440:             "nvidia",
  441:             "microsoft",
  442:             "ferrari",
  443:             "porsche",
  444:             "marvel entertainment",
  445:             "warner bros. pictures",
  446:             "warner bros. entertainment",
  447:             "universal pictures",
  448:             "paramount pictures",
  449:             "sony pictures entertainment",
  450:             "national geographic",
  451:             "smithsonian channel",
  452:         }
  453: 
  454:         if channel in exact_high_authority:
  455:             score = 100.0
  456: 
  457:         elif any(
  458:             term == channel
  459:             for term in cls.OFFICIAL_TERMS
  460:         ):
  461:             score = 95.0
  462: 
  463:         ####################################################
  464:         # Explicit official-channel naming helps, but does
  465:         # not automatically equal first-party authority.
  466:         ####################################################
  467: 
  468:         elif (
  469:             "official" in channel
  470:             or channel.endswith(
  471:                 " official"
  472:             )
  473:         ):
  474:             score = 80.0
  475: 
  476:         ####################################################
  477:         # Established specialist channels still have some
  478:         # source value, but remain below first-party media.
  479:         ####################################################
  480: 
  481:         elif any(
  482:             term in channel
  483:             for term in (
  484:                 "watchfinder",
  485:                 "crown & caliber",
  486:                 "bob's watches",
  487:                 "wristcheck",
  488:                 "hodinkee",
  489:                 "watchbox",
  490:             )
  491:         ):
  492:             score = 65.0
  493: 
  494:         ####################################################
  495:         # Penalize low-value derivative/reaction content.
  496:         ####################################################
  497: 
  498:         combined = (
  499:             channel
  500:             + " "
  501:             + title_lower
  502:         )
  503: 
  504:         if any(
  505:             term in combined
  506:             for term in cls.LOW_VALUE_TERMS
  507:         ):
  508:             score -= 30.0
  509: 
  510:         return round(
  511:             max(
  512:                 0.0,
  513:                 min(
  514:                     score,
  515:                     100.0,
  516:                 ),
  517:             ),
  518:             2,
  519:         )

  521:     @staticmethod
  522:     def _relevance_score(
  523:         *,
  524:         query: str,
  525:         title: str,
  526:         description: str,
  527:     ) -> float:
  528: 
  529:         query_words = {
  530:             word
  531:             for word in (
  532:                 query.lower().split()
  533:             )
  534:             if len(word) >= 3
  535:         }
  536: 
  537:         title_words = set(
  538:             title.lower().split()
  539:         )
  540: 
  541:         body_words = set(
  542:             description.lower().split()
  543:         )
  544: 
  545:         if not query_words:
  546:             return 0.0
  547: 
  548:         title_overlap = len(
  549:             query_words
  550:             .intersection(
  551:                 title_words
  552:             )
  553:         )
  554: 
  555:         body_overlap = len(
  556:             query_words
  557:             .intersection(
  558:                 body_words
  559:             )
  560:         )
  561: 
  562:         title_ratio = (
  563:             title_overlap
  564:             / len(query_words)
  565:         )
  566: 
  567:         body_ratio = (
  568:             body_overlap
  569:             / len(query_words)
  570:         )
  571: 
  572:         score = (
  573:             title_ratio * 75.0
  574:             + body_ratio * 25.0
  575:         )
  576: 
  577:         return round(
  578:             min(
  579:                 score,
  580:                 100.0,
  581:             ),
  582:             2,
  583:         )
  584:

  587:         *,
  588:         definition: str,

    1: """Persistent research memory."""
    2: 
    3: from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    4: 
    5: from backend.services.research.knowledge_pack import KnowledgePack
    6: 
    7: 
    8: class ResearchMemory:
    9:     """
   10:     Temporary implementation.
   11: 
   12:     Will later store/retrieve KnowledgePacks
   13:     from SQLite.
   14:     """
   15: 
   16:     def __init__(self):
   17:         self.memory = {}
   18: 
   19:     def get(
   20:         self,
   21:         topic: str,
   22:         max_age_hours: int = 24,
   23:     ) -> KnowledgePack | None:
   24: 
   25:         key = topic.lower()
   26: 
   27:         if key not in self.memory:
   28:             return None
   29: 
   30:         timestamp, pack = self.memory[key]
   31: 
   32:         if (
   33:             datetime.utcnow() - timestamp
   34:             > timedelta(hours=max_age_hours)
   35:         ):
   36:             del self.memory[key]
   37:             return None
   38: 
   39:         return pack
   40: 
   41:     def save(
   42:         self,
   43:         topic: str,
   44:         pack: KnowledgePack,
   45:     ) -> None:
   46: 
   47:         self.memory[topic.lower()] = (
   48:             datetime.utcnow(),
   49:             pack,
   50:         )
   51: 
   52:     def clear(self):
   53: 
   54:         self.memory.clear()

    9: from backend.services.research.providers.wikipedia import WikipediaProvider
   10: from backend.services.research.research_memory import ResearchMemory

   12: 
   13: def sanitize_topic(raw_title: str) -> str:
   14:     """
   15:     Extract the core topic from noisy titles for better research quality.
   16:     """
   17: 
   18:     if not raw_title:
   19:         return ""
   20: 
   21:     segments = re.split(r"[|\-–—]", raw_title)
   22:     clean = segments[0] if segments else raw_title
   23: 
   24:     noise_patterns = [
   25:         r"\b(official|teaser|trailer|hd|video|full movie|breaking news|live)\b",
   26:         r"\b(ndtv|live law|the hindu|times of india|hindustan times)\b",
   27:         r"\b(\d{1,2}(st|nd|rd|th)?\s+(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|sept|oct|nov|dec))\b",
   28:         r"\b202\d\b",
   29:     ]
   30: 
   31:     for pattern in noise_patterns:
   32:         clean = re.sub(
   33:             pattern,
   34:             "",
   35:             clean,
   36:             flags=re.IGNORECASE,
   37:         )
   38: 
   39:     clean = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", clean).strip()
   40: 
   41:     return clean if len(clean) >= 3 else raw_title.strip()
   42:

   69:     def research(self, topic: str) -> KnowledgePack:
   70:         """
   71:         Research a topic and return a populated KnowledgePack.
   72:         """
   73: 
   74:         clean_query = sanitize_topic(topic)
   75: 
   76:         cached = self.memory.get(clean_query)
   77: 
   78:         if cached is not None:
   79:             print(f"[ResearchMemory] HIT -> {clean_query}")
   80:             return cached
   81: 
   82:         print(f"[ResearchMemory] MISS -> {clean_query}")
   83: 
   84:         pack = KnowledgePack(topic=topic)
   85: 
   86:         all_content = []
   87: 
   88:         for provider in self.registry.get_all():
   89: 
   90:             try:
   91:                 results = provider.research(clean_query)
   92: 
   93:                 for result in results:
   94: 
   95:                     pack.add_source(result)
   96: 
   97:                     content = result.get("content", "").strip()
   98: 
   99:                     if content:
  100:                         pack.add_fact(content)
  101:                         all_content.append(content)
  102: 
  103:             except Exception as exc:
  104: 
  105:                 print(
  106:                     f"[ResearchService] "
  107:                     f"{provider.name} failed for "
  108:                     f"'{clean_query}': {exc}"
  109:                 )
  110: 
  111:         # Summary
  112:         if all_content:
  113:             pack.set_summary(
  114:                 " ".join(all_content[:3])[:4000]
  115:             )
  116: 
  117:         # Category
  118:         pack.category = self._classify_topic(clean_query)
  119: 
  120:         # Deterministic research confidence.
  121:         confidence = self.confidence.score(
  122:             clean_query,
  123:             pack.sources,
  124:         )
  125: 
  126:         pack.score = confidence["score"]
  127: 
  128:         # Save for future requests
  129:         self.memory.save(
  130:             clean_query,
  131:             pack,
  132:         )
  133: 
  134:         return pack

  144:             )

  147:             positive_score
  148:             - negative_score

  250:     def _download(
  251:         self,
  252:         url: str,
  253:     ) -> Path | None:
  254: 
  255:         if not url:
  256:             return None
  257: 
  258:         digest = hashlib.sha1(
  259:             url.encode(
  260:                 "utf-8"
  261:             )
  262:         ).hexdigest()[:16]
  263: 
  264:         output = (
  265:             self.cache_dir
  266:             / f"{digest}.jpg"
  267:         )
  268: 
  269:         if (
  270:             output.exists()
  271:             and output.stat().st_size > 0
  272:         ):
  273:             return output
  274: 
  275:         try:
  276: 
  277:             response = requests.get(
  278:                 url,
  279:                 timeout=20,
  280:             )
  281: 
  282:             response.raise_for_status()
  283: 
  284:             output.write_bytes(
  285:                 response.content
  286:             )
  287: 
  288:         except Exception:
  289:             return None
  290: 
  291:         return output

  290:     def _clip_embedding(
  291:         self,
  292:         clip: IndexedSourceClip,

  294:

  365:     def _text_embedding(
  366:         self,
  367:         query: str,
  368:     ) -> torch.Tensor:

  371:             self.tokenizer(

  375:                         "cinematic visual of "
  376:                         + query
  377:                     ),
  378:                     (
  379:                         "close relevant footage of "
  380:                         + query
  381:                     ),
  382:                 ]

  385:                 self.device
  386:             )
  387:         )

  389:         with torch.no_grad():

  393:                     tokens

  407:             ################################################

  412:                     keepdim=True,
  413:                 )

  419:                     dim=-1,
  420:                     keepdim=True,
  421:                 )

  407:     def _best_video_file(
  408:         self,
  409:         metadata: dict,
  410:     ) -> dict | None:
  411: 
  412:         candidates = []
  413: 
  414:         for item in metadata.get(
  415:             "files",
  416:             [],
  417:         ):
  418: 
  419:             name = str(
  420:                 item.get(
  421:                     "name",
  422:                     "",
  423:                 )
  424:             )
  425: 
  426:             suffix = Path(
  427:                 name
  428:             ).suffix.lower()
  429: 
  430:             if (
  431:                 suffix
  432:                 not in self.VIDEO_EXTENSIONS
  433:             ):
  434:                 continue
  435: 
  436:             try:
  437:                 size = int(
  438:                     item.get(
  439:                         "size",
  440:                         0,
  441:                     )
  442:                     or 0
  443:                 )
  444:             except (
  445:                 TypeError,
  446:                 ValueError,
  447:             ):
  448:                 size = 0
  449: 
  450:             if (
  451:                 size <= 0
  452:                 or size
  453:                 > self.MAX_DOWNLOAD_BYTES
  454:             ):
  455:                 continue
  456: 
  457:             lower_name = name.lower()
  458: 
  459:             score = 0
  460: 
  461:             if suffix == ".mp4":
  462:                 score += 30
  463: 
  464:             if "512kb" in lower_name:
  465:                 score += 20
  466: 
  467:             if "h.264" in lower_name:
  468:                 score += 15
  469: 
  470:             if "derivative" in str(
  471:                 item.get(
  472:                     "source",
  473:                     "",
  474:                 )
  475:             ).lower():
  476:                 score += 10
  477: 
  478:             candidates.append(
  479:                 (
  480:                     score,
  481:                     -size,
  482:                     item,
  483:                 )
  484:             )
  485: 
  486:         if not candidates:
  487:             return None
  488: 
  489:         candidates.sort(
  490:             reverse=True,
  491:             key=lambda row: (
  492:                 row[0],
  493:                 row[1],
  494:             ),
  495:         )
  496: 
  497:         return candidates[0][2]

  499:     def _download(
  500:         self,
  501:         *,
  502:         url: str,
  503:         content_id: str,
  504:         identifier: str,
  505:         filename: str,
  506:     ) -> Path | None:
  507: 
  508:         digest = hashlib.sha1(
  509:             (
  510:                 identifier
  511:                 + "|"
  512:                 + filename
  513:             ).encode(
  514:                 "utf-8"
  515:             )
  516:         ).hexdigest()[:16]
  517: 
  518:         suffix = (
  519:             Path(
  520:                 filename
  521:             ).suffix.lower()
  522:             or ".mp4"
  523:         )
  524: 
  525:         output = (
  526:             self.download_root
  527:             / (
  528:                 f"{content_id}_"
  529:                 f"{digest}"
  530:                 f"{suffix}"
  531:             )
  532:         )
  533: 
  534:         if (
  535:             output.exists()
  536:             and output.stat().st_size > 0
  537:         ):
  538:             return output
  539: 
  540:         temp = output.with_suffix(
  541:             output.suffix + ".part"
  542:         )
  543: 
  544:         try:
  545: 
  546:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  547:                 url,
  548:                 headers={
  549:                     "User-Agent": (
  550:                         "JarvisRichV1/1.0"
  551:                     ),
  552:                 },
  553:             )
  554: 
  555:             with urllib.request.urlopen(
  556:                 request,
  557:                 timeout=60,
  558:             ) as response:
  559: 
  560:                 with temp.open(
  561:                     "wb"
  562:                 ) as handle:
  563: 
  564:                     total = 0
  565: 
  566:                     while True:
  567: 
  568:                         chunk = response.read(
  569:                             1024 * 1024
  570:                         )
  571: 
  572:                         if not chunk:
  573:                             break
  574: 
  575:                         total += len(
  576:                             chunk
  577:                         )
  578: 
  579:                         if (
  580:                             total
  581:                             > self.MAX_DOWNLOAD_BYTES
  582:                         ):
  583:                             raise RuntimeError(
  584:                                 "Archive video exceeds "
  585:                                 "download limit."
  586:                             )
  587: 
  588:                         handle.write(
  589:                             chunk
  590:                         )
  591: 
  592:             temp.replace(
  593:                 output
  594:             )
  595: 
  596:         except Exception:
  597: 
  598:             try:
  599:                 temp.unlink(
  600:                     missing_ok=True
  601:                 )
  602:             except OSError:
  603:                 pass
  604: 
  605:             return None
  606: 
  607:         return output

  130: 
  131:             asset_id = (
  132:                 "nasa-"
  133:                 + hashlib.sha1(
  134:                     (
  135:                         nasa_id
  136:                         + "|"
  137:                         + asset_url
  138:                     ).encode(
  139:                         "utf-8"
  140:                     )
  141:                 ).hexdigest()[:14]
  142:             )
  143: 
  144:             output.append(
  145:                 MediaAsset(
  146:                     asset_id=asset_id,
  147:                     asset_type="video",
  148:                     file_path=str(
  149:                         local_path
  150:                     ),
  151:                     source_url=asset_url,
  152:                     source_name=(
  153:                         self.SOURCE_NAME
  154:                     ),
  155:                     creator=str(
  156:                         metadata.get(
  157:                             "center",
  158:                             "NASA",
  159:                         )
  160:                         or "NASA"
  161:                     ),
  162:                     license_name=(
  163:                         "NASA Media"
  164:                     ),
  165:                     license_url=(
  166:                         "https://www.nasa.gov/nasa-brand-center/images-and-media/"
  167:                     ),
  168:                     attribution_required=False,
  169:                     commercial_use_allowed=True,
  170:                     relevance_score=80.0,
  171:                     content_id=content_id,
  172:                 )

  224:     def _best_video_url(
  225:         self,
  226:         nasa_id: str,
  227:     ) -> str | None:
  228: 
  229:         url = (
  230:             self.ASSET_URL.format(
  231:                 nasa_id=(
  232:                     urllib.parse.quote(
  233:                         nasa_id,
  234:                         safe="",
  235:                     )
  236:                 )
  237:             )
  238:         )
  239: 
  240:         try:
  241: 
  242:             with urllib.request.urlopen(
  243:                 url,
  244:                 timeout=30,
  245:             ) as response:
  246: 
  247:                 data = json.loads(
  248:                     response.read().decode(
  249:                         "utf-8",
  250:                         errors="replace",
  251:                     )
  252:                 )
  253: 
  254:         except Exception:
  255:             return None
  256: 
  257:         items = (
  258:             data.get(
  259:                 "collection",
  260:                 {},
  261:             ).get(
  262:                 "items",
  263:                 [],
  264:             )
  265:             or []
  266:         )
  267: 
  268:         candidates = []
  269: 
  270:         for item in items:
  271: 
  272:             href = str(
  273:                 item.get(
  274:                     "href",
  275:                     "",
  276:                 )
  277:             ).strip()
  278: 
  279:             if not href:
  280:                 continue
  281: 
  282:             parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(
  283:                 href
  284:             )
  285: 
  286:             suffix = Path(
  287:                 parsed.path
  288:             ).suffix.lower()
  289: 
  290:             if (
  291:                 suffix
  292:                 not in self.VIDEO_EXTENSIONS
  293:             ):
  294:                 continue
  295: 
  296:             lower = href.lower()
  297: 
  298:             score = 0
  299: 
  300:             if suffix == ".mp4":
  301:                 score += 30
  302: 
  303:             if "orig" in lower:
  304:                 score += 10
  305: 
  306:             if "large" in lower:
  307:                 score += 8
  308: 
  309:             if "medium" in lower:
  310:                 score += 5
  311: 
  312:             candidates.append(
  313:                 (
  314:                     score,
  315:                     href,
  316:                 )
  317:             )
  318: 
  319:         if not candidates:
  320:             return None
  321: 
  322:         candidates.sort(
  323:             reverse=True,
  324:             key=lambda row: row[0],
  325:         )
  326: 
  327:         return candidates[0][1]

  332:         url: str,


--- NasaVideoProvider._download @ line 329 ---

  326: 
  327:         return candidates[0][1]
  328: 
  329:     def _download(
  330:         self,
  331:         *,
  332:         url: str,
  333:         content_id: str,
  334:         nasa_id: str,
  335:     ) -> Path | None:
  336: 
  337:         parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(
  338:             url
  339:         )
  340: 
  341:         suffix = (
  342:             Path(
  343:                 parsed.path
  344:             ).suffix.lower()
  345:             or ".mp4"
  346:         )
  347: 
  348:         digest = hashlib.sha1(
  349:             url.encode(
  350:                 "utf-8"
  351:             )
  352:         ).hexdigest()[:16]
  353: 
  354:         safe_id = "".join(
  355:             character
  356:             if character.isalnum()
  357:             else "_"
  358:             for character in nasa_id
  359:         )[:40]
  360: 
  361:         output = (
  362:             self.download_root
  363:             / (
  364:                 f"{content_id}_"
  365:                 f"{safe_id}_"
  366:                 f"{digest}"
  367:                 f"{suffix}"
  368:             )
  369:         )
  370: 
  371:         if (
  372:             output.exists()
  373:             and output.stat().st_size > 0
  374:         ):
  375:             return output
  376: 
  377:         temp = output.with_suffix(
  378:             output.suffix + ".part"
  379:         )
  380: 
  381:         try:
  382: 
  383:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  384:                 url,
  385:                 headers={
  386:                     "User-Agent": (
  387:                         "JarvisRichV1/1.0"
  388:                     ),
  389:                 },
  390:             )
  391: 
  392:             with urllib.request.urlopen(
  393:                 request,
  394:                 timeout=90,
  395:             ) as response:
  396: 
  397:                 with temp.open(
  398:                     "wb"
  399:                 ) as handle:
  400: 
  401:                     total = 0
  402: 
  403:                     while True:
  404: 
  405:                         chunk = response.read(
  406:                             1024 * 1024
  407:                         )
  408: 
  409:                         if not chunk:
  410:                             break
  411: 
  412:                         total += len(
  413:                             chunk
  414:                         )
  415: 
  416:                         if (
  417:                             total
  418:                             > self.MAX_DOWNLOAD_BYTES
  419:                         ):
  420:                             raise RuntimeError(
  421:                                 "NASA video exceeds "
  422:                                 "download limit."
  423:                             )
  424: 
  425:                         handle.write(
  426:                             chunk
  427:                         )
  428: 
  429:             temp.replace(
  430:                 output
  431:             )
  432: 
  433:         except Exception:
  434: 
  435:             try:
  436:                 temp.unlink(
  437:                     missing_ok=True
  438:                 )
  439:             except OSError:
  440:                 pass
  441: 
  442:             return None
  443: 
  444:         return output

   61:     async def search_and_download(
   62:         self,
   63:         *,
   64:         query: str,
   65:         content_id: str,
   66:         limit: int = 2,
   67:     ) -> list[MediaAsset]:
   68: 
   69:         clean_query = " ".join(
   70:             str(query).split()
   71:         ).strip()
   72: 
   73:         if not clean_query:
   74:             return []
   75: 
   76:         clean_content_id = str(
   77:             content_id
   78:         ).strip()
   79: 
   80:         if not clean_content_id:
   81:             raise RuntimeError(
   82:                 "Pexels search requires content_id."
   83:             )
   84: 
   85:         safe_limit = max(
   86:             1,
   87:             min(
   88:                 int(limit),
   89:                 5,
   90:             ),
   91:         )
   92: 
   93:         ####################################################
   94:         # Prefer video because movie commentary benefits
   95:         # from motion. Fall back to photos.
   96:         ####################################################
   97: 
   98:         video_results = await asyncio.to_thread(
   99:             self._search_videos,
  100:             clean_query,
  101:             safe_limit,
  102:         )
  103: 
  104:         assets = []
  105: 
  106:         for item in video_results:
  107: 
  108:             asset = await asyncio.to_thread(
  109:                 self._download_video,
  110:                 item,
  111:                 clean_query,
  112:                 clean_content_id,
  113:             )
  114: 
  115:             if asset is not None:
  116:                 assets.append(asset)
  117: 
  118:             if len(assets) >= safe_limit:
  119:                 return assets
  120: 
  121:         remaining = (
  122:             safe_limit - len(assets)
  123:         )
  124: 
  125:         if remaining <= 0:
  126:             return assets
  127: 
  128:         photo_results = await asyncio.to_thread(
  129:             self._search_photos,
  130:             clean_query,
  131:             remaining,
  132:         )
  133: 
  134:         for item in photo_results:
  135: 
  136:             asset = await asyncio.to_thread(
  137:                 self._download_photo,
  138:                 item,
  139:                 clean_query,
  140:                 clean_content_id,
  141:             )
  142: 
  143:             if asset is not None:
  144:                 assets.append(asset)
  145: 
  146:             if len(assets) >= safe_limit:
  147:                 break
  148: 
  149:         return assets
