  254:     def _download_video(
  255:         self,
  256:         item: dict,
  257:         query: str,
  258:         content_id: str,
  259:     ) -> MediaAsset | None:
  260: 
  261:         video_id = str(
  262:             item.get(
  263:                 "id",
  264:                 "",
  265:             )
  266:         ).strip()
  267: 
  268:         source_url = str(
  269:             item.get(
  270:                 "url",
  271:                 "",
  272:             )
  273:         ).strip()
  274: 
  275:         creator = ""
  276: 
  277:         user = item.get(
  278:             "user",
  279:             {},
  280:         )
  281: 
  282:         if isinstance(user, dict):
  283:             creator = str(
  284:                 user.get(
  285:                     "name",
  286:                     "",
  287:                 )
  288:             ).strip()
  289: 
  290:         files = item.get(
  291:             "video_files",
  292:             [],
  293:         )
  294: 
  295:         if not isinstance(files, list):
  296:             return None
  297: 
  298:         usable = []
  299: 
  300:         for file_info in files:
  301: 
  302:             if not isinstance(
  303:                 file_info,
  304:                 dict,
  305:             ):
  306:                 continue
  307: 
  308:             link = str(
  309:                 file_info.get(
  310:                     "link",
  311:                     "",
  312:                 )
  313:             ).strip()
  314: 
  315:             file_type = str(
  316:                 file_info.get(
  317:                     "file_type",
  318:                     "",
  319:                 )
  320:             ).lower()
  321: 
  322:             width = self._int_or_none(
  323:                 file_info.get(
  324:                     "width"
  325:                 )
  326:             )
  327: 
  328:             height = self._int_or_none(
  329:                 file_info.get(
  330:                     "height"
  331:                 )
  332:             )
  333: 
  334:             if not link:
  335:                 continue
  336: 
  337:             if "mp4" not in file_type:
  338:                 continue
  339: 
  340:             usable.append(
  341:                 (
  342:                     width or 0,
  343:                     height or 0,
  344:                     link,
  345:                     width,
  346:                     height,
  347:                 )
  348:             )
  349: 
  350:         if not usable:
  351:             return None
  352: 
  353:         ####################################################
  354:         # Prefer useful resolution without blindly taking
  355:         # the largest available file.
  356:         ####################################################
  357: 
  358:         usable.sort(
  359:             key=lambda item: (
  360:                 item[1] >= 1080,
  361:                 item[0] >= 720,
  362:                 item[0] * item[1],
  363:             ),
  364:             reverse=True,
  365:         )
  366: 
  367:         (
  368:             _,
  369:             _,
  370:             download_url,
  371:             width,
  372:             height,
  373:         ) = usable[0]
  374: 
  375:         filename = self._filename(
  376:             content_id=content_id,
  377:             media_id=video_id,
  378:             extension=".mp4",
  379:         )
  380: 
  381:         destination = (
  382:             self.download_root
  383:             / filename
  384:         )
  385: 
  386:         if not destination.exists():
  387: 
  388:             if not self._download(
  389:                 download_url,
  390:                 destination,
  391:             ):
  392:                 return None
  393: 
  394:         relevance = self._relevance_score(
  395:             query=query,
  396:             item_text=" ".join(
  397:                 [
  398:                     source_url,
  399:                     creator,
  400:                 ]
  401:             ),
  402:         )
  403: 
  404:         return MediaAsset(
  405:             asset_id=(
  406:                 f"pexels-video-{video_id}"
  407:             ),
  408:             asset_type="video",
  409:             file_path=str(destination),
  410:             source_url=source_url,
  411:             source_name=self.SOURCE_NAME,
  412:             creator=creator or None,
  413:             license_name=self.LICENSE_NAME,
  414:             license_url=self.LICENSE_URL,
  415:             attribution_required=False,
  416:             commercial_use_allowed=True,
  417:             relevance_score=relevance,
  418:             duration=self._float_or_none(
  419:                 item.get(
  420:                     "duration"
  421:                 )
  422:             ),
  423:             width=width,
  424:             height=height,
  425:             content_id=content_id,
  426:         )
  427:

  430:     ########################################################

  561:

  563: 
  564:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  565:                 url,
  566:                 headers={
  567:                     "User-Agent": (
  568:                         "Jarvis-Rich-V1/1.0"
  569:                     ),
  570:                 },
  571:             )
  572: 
  573:             with urllib.request.urlopen(
  574:                 request,
  575:                 timeout=30,
  576:             ) as response:
  577: 
  578:                 data = response.read()
  579: 
  580:             if not data:
  581:                 return False
  582:

  584:                 data
  585:             )

  588:

  593:     def _filename(
  594:         *,
  595:         content_id: str,
  596:         media_id: str,
  597:         extension: str,
  598:     ) -> str:
  599:

  601:             r"[^A-Za-z0-9_-]+",
  602:             "_",

  605: 
  606:         digest = hashlib.sha1(
  607:             str(media_id).encode(
  608:                 "utf-8"

  610:         ).hexdigest()[:12]

  613:             f"{safe_content}_"
  614:             f"{digest}"

--- PexelsProvider._relevance_score @ line 619 ---

  617: 
  618:     @staticmethod
  619:     def _relevance_score(
  620:         *,
  621:         query: str,
  622:         item_text: str,
  623:     ) -> float:
  624:         """
  625:         Conservative lexical relevance estimate.
  626: 
  627:         This deliberately does not award high relevance merely
  628:         because Pexels returned an item.
  629:         """
  630: 
  631:         query_terms = {
  632:             word
  633:             for word in re.findall(
  634:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  635:                 query.lower(),
  636:             )
  637:             if len(word) >= 4
  638:         }
  639: 
  640:         item_terms = {
  641:             word
  642:             for word in re.findall(
  643:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  644:                 item_text.lower(),
  645:             )
  646:             if len(word) >= 4
  647:         }
  648: 
  649:         if not query_terms:
  650:             return 0.0
  651: 
  652:         shared = (
  653:             query_terms
  654:             .intersection(
  655:                 item_terms
  656:             )
  657:         )
  658: 
  659:         ratio = (
  660:             len(shared)
  661:             / len(query_terms)
  662:         )
  663: 
  664:         return round(
  665:             min(
  666:                 95.0,
  667:                 60.0
  668:                 + ratio * 35.0,
  669:             ),
  670:             1,
  671:         )
  672: 
  673:     @staticmethod
  674:     def _int_or_none(
  675:         value,
  676:     ) -> int | None:

  276:     def _search_and_download_sync(
  277:         self,
  278:         query: str,
  279:         content_id: str,
  280:         limit: int,
  281:     ) -> list[MediaAsset]:
  282: 
  283:         search_results = self._api(
  284:             {
  285:                 "action": "query",
  286:                 "generator": "search",
  287:                 "gsrsearch": query,
  288:                 "gsrnamespace": "6",
  289:                 "gsrlimit": str(
  290:                     max(limit * 5, 10)
  291:                 ),
  292:                 "prop": "imageinfo",
  293:                 "iiprop": (
  294:                     "url|mime|extmetadata"
  295:                 ),
  296:                 "iiurlwidth": "1600",
  297:                 "format": "json",
  298:                 "formatversion": "2",
  299:             }
  300:         )
  301: 
  302:         pages = (
  303:             search_results
  304:             .get("query", {})
  305:             .get("pages", [])
  306:         )
  307: 
  308:         output_dir = (
  309:             self.base_dir
  310:             / self._safe_name(content_id)
  311:         )
  312: 
  313:         output_dir.mkdir(
  314:             parents=True,
  315:             exist_ok=True,
  316:         )
  317: 
  318:         assets: list[MediaAsset] = []
  319: 
  320:         for rank, page in enumerate(
  321:             pages,
  322:             start=1,
  323:         ):
  324: 
  325:             if len(assets) >= limit:
  326:                 break
  327: 
  328:             imageinfo = page.get(
  329:                 "imageinfo",
  330:                 [],
  331:             )
  332: 
  333:             if not imageinfo:
  334:                 continue
  335: 
  336:             info = imageinfo[0]
  337: 
  338:             mime = str(
  339:                 info.get("mime", "")
  340:             ).lower()
  341: 
  342:             if mime not in self.ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES:
  343:                 continue
  344: 
  345:             metadata = info.get(
  346:                 "extmetadata",
  347:                 {},
  348:             )
  349: 
  350:             license_name = self._meta(
  351:                 metadata,
  352:                 "LicenseShortName",
  353:             )
  354: 
  355:             usage_terms = self._meta(
  356:                 metadata,
  357:                 "UsageTerms",
  358:             )
  359: 
  360:             license_text = (
  361:                 f"{license_name} {usage_terms}"
  362:             ).lower()
  363: 
  364:             if not self._license_allowed(
  365:                 license_text
  366:             ):
  367:                 continue
  368: 
  369:             source_url = str(
  370:                 info.get(
  371:                     "descriptionurl",
  372:                     "",
  373:                 )
  374:                 or info.get(
  375:                     "url",
  376:                     "",
  377:                 )
  378:             ).strip()
  379: 
  380:             if mime.startswith("video/"):
  381:                 # Video assets must download the actual video,
  382:                 # never the image thumbnail.
  383:                 media_url = str(
  384:                     info.get(
  385:                         "url",
  386:                         "",
  387:                     )
  388:                 ).strip()
  389:             else:
  390:                 # Images use a smaller Wikimedia thumbnail
  391:                 # when available to reduce bandwidth/rate limits.
  392:                 media_url = str(
  393:                     info.get(
  394:                         "thumburl",
  395:                         "",
  396:                     )
  397:                     or info.get(
  398:                         "url",
  399:                         "",
  400:                     )
  401:                 ).strip()
  402: 
  403:             if not media_url:
  404:                 continue
  405: 
  406:             title = str(
  407:                 page.get(
  408:                     "title",
  409:                     "media",
  410:                 )
  411:             )
  412: 
  413:             relevance = self._relevance_score(
  414:                 query=query,
  415:                 title=title,
  416:                 rank=rank,
  417:             )
  418: 
  419:             if relevance < 65.0:
  420:                 continue
  421: 
  422:             suffix = self._suffix_from_url(
  423:                 media_url,
  424:                 mime,
  425:             )
  426: 
  427:             filename = (
  428:                 f"{uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  429:                 f"{suffix}"
  430:             )
  431: 
  432:             destination = (
  433:                 output_dir / filename
  434:             )
  435: 
  436:             try:
  437:                 self._download(
  438:                     media_url,
  439:                     destination,
  440:                 )
  441: 
  442:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
  443: 
  444:                 if exc.code == 429:
  445:                     # One throttled asset must not abort the
  446:                     # entire production cycle. Continue searching
  447:                     # for another explicitly licensed candidate.
  448:                     continue
  449: 
  450:                 raise
  451: 
  452:             except Exception:
  453:                 # Media-provider failure is isolated to this
  454:                 # candidate. Other search results may still work.
  455:                 continue
  456: 
  457:             creator = self._clean_html(
  458:                 self._meta(
  459:                     metadata,
  460:                     "Artist",
  461:                 )
  462:             )
  463: 
  464:             license_url = self._meta(
  465:                 metadata,
  466:                 "LicenseUrl",
  467:             )
  468: 
  469:             attribution_required = not (
  470:                 "public domain"
  471:                 in license_text
  472:                 or "cc0"
  473:                 in license_text
  474:             )
  475: 
  476:             asset_type = (
  477:                 "video"
  478:                 if mime.startswith("video/")
  479:                 else "image"
  480:             )
  481: 
  482:             assets.append(
  483:                 MediaAsset(
  484:                     asset_id=uuid4().hex,
  485:                     asset_type=asset_type,
  486:                     file_path=str(
  487:                         destination
  488:                     ),
  489:                     source_url=source_url,
  490:                     source_name=(
  491:                         "Wikimedia Commons"
  492:                     ),
  493:                     creator=creator or None,
  494:                     license_name=(
  495:                         license_name
  496:                         or usage_terms
  497:                         or None
  498:                     ),
  499:                     license_url=(
  500:                         license_url or None
  501:                     ),
  502:                     attribution_required=(
  503:                         attribution_required
  504:                     ),
  505:                     commercial_use_allowed=True,
  506:                     relevance_score=relevance,
  507:                     content_id=content_id,
  508:                 )
  509:             )
  510: 
  511:         return assets

  516:     ) -> dict:

  510:

  512: 
  513:     def _api(
  514:         self,
  515:         params: dict[str, str],
  516:     ) -> dict:
  517:         """
  518:         Wikimedia API request with bounded handling for
  519:         HTTP 429 and temporary transport failures.
  520: 
  521:         Persistent provider throttling becomes an empty
  522:         provider result instead of terminating the full
  523:         video pipeline.
  524: 
  525:         Licensing, relevance and authorization logic
  526:         remain unchanged elsewhere.
  527:         """
  528: 
  529:         url = (
  530:             self.API_URL
  531:             + "?"
  532:             + urllib.parse.urlencode(
  533:                 params
  534:             )
  535:         )
  536: 
  537:         attempts = 3
  538: 
  539:         for attempt in range(
  540:             1,
  541:             attempts + 1,
  542:         ):
  543: 
  544:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  545:                 url,
  546:                 headers={
  547:                     "User-Agent": self.USER_AGENT,
  548:                 },
  549:             )
  550: 
  551:             try:
  552: 
  553:                 with urllib.request.urlopen(
  554:                     request,
  555:                     timeout=30,
  556:                 ) as response:
  557: 
  558:                     return json.loads(
  559:                         response.read().decode(
  560:                             "utf-8"
  561:                         )
  562:                     )
  563: 
  564:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
  565: 
  566:                 if exc.code != 429:
  567:                     raise
  568: 
  569:                 if attempt >= attempts:
  570:                     return {}
  571: 
  572:                 retry_after = None
  573: 
  574:                 if exc.headers is not None:
  575: 
  576:                     try:
  577:                         retry_after = (
  578:                             exc.headers.get(
  579:                                 "Retry-After"
  580:                             )
  581:                         )
  582:                     except Exception:
  583:                         retry_after = None
  584: 
  585:                 wait_seconds = None
  586: 
  587:                 if retry_after:
  588: 
  589:                     try:
  590:                         wait_seconds = float(
  591:                             str(
  592:                                 retry_after
  593:                             ).strip()
  594:                         )
  595:                     except (
  596:                         TypeError,
  597:                         ValueError,
  598:                     ):
  599:                         wait_seconds = None
  600: 
  601:                 if wait_seconds is None:
  602: 
  603:                     wait_seconds = min(
  604:                         2.0 ** attempt,
  605:                         8.0,
  606:                     )
  607: 
  608:                 wait_seconds = max(
  609:                     1.0,
  610:                     min(
  611:                         float(
  612:                             wait_seconds
  613:                         ),
  614:                         15.0,
  615:                     ),
  616:                 )
  617: 
  618:                 time.sleep(
  619:                     wait_seconds
  620:                 )
  621: 
  622:             except (
  623:                 urllib.error.URLError,
  624:                 TimeoutError,
  625:             ):
  626: 
  627:                 if attempt >= attempts:
  628:                     return {}
  629: 
  630:                 time.sleep(
  631:                     min(
  632:                         1.5 * attempt,
  633:                         4.5,
  634:                     )
  635:                 )
  636: 
  637:         return {}
  638: 
  639:     def _download(
  640:         self,
  641:         url: str,
  642:         destination: Path,


--- WikimediaCommonsProvider._download @ line 639 ---
  635:                 )
  636: 
  637:         return {}
  638: 
  639:     def _download(
  640:         self,
  641:         url: str,
  642:         destination: Path,
  643:     ) -> None:
  644: 
  645:         if destination.exists():
  646:             return
  647: 
  648:         attempts = 3
  649: 
  650:         for attempt in range(
  651:             1,
  652:             attempts + 1,
  653:         ):
  654: 
  655:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  656:                 url,
  657:                 headers={
  658:                     "User-Agent": self.USER_AGENT,
  659:                 },
  660:             )
  661: 
  662:             try:
  663: 
  664:                 with urllib.request.urlopen(
  665:                     request,
  666:                     timeout=60,
  667:                 ) as response:
  668: 
  669:                     destination.write_bytes(
  670:                         response.read()
  671:                     )
  672: 
  673:                 # Small courtesy delay between successful
  674:                 # Wikimedia media requests.
  675:                 time.sleep(1.25)
  676: 
  677:                 return
  678: 
  679:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
  680: 
  681:                 if (
  682:                     exc.code == 429
  683:                     and attempt < attempts
  684:                 ):
  685: 
  686:                     retry_after = (
  687:                         exc.headers.get(
  688:                             "Retry-After"
  689:                         )
  690:                         if exc.headers
  691:                         else None
  692:                     )
  693: 
  694:                     try:
  695:                         delay = float(
  696:                             retry_after
  697:                         )
  698:                     except (
  699:                         TypeError,
  700:                         ValueError,
  701:                     ):
  702:                         delay = min(
  703:                             3.0 * attempt,
  704:                             10.0,
  705:                         )
  706: 
  707:                     time.sleep(
  708:                         max(delay, 2.0)
  709:                     )
  710: 
  711:                     continue
  712: 
  713:                 raise
  714: 
  715:         raise RuntimeError(
  716:             "Wikimedia media download failed "
  717:             "after retry attempts."
  718:         )
  719:     def _license_allowed(
  720:         self,
  721:         text: str,
  722:     ) -> bool:
  723:

  803:             "image/webp": ".webp",
  804:             "video/webm": ".webm",
  805:             "video/mp4": ".mp4",
  806:             "video/ogg": ".ogv",

  817:         query: str,


--- WikimediaCommonsProvider._relevance_score @ line 815 ---
  811:             ".bin",
  812:         )
  813: 
  814:     @staticmethod
  815:     def _relevance_score(
  816:         *,
  817:         query: str,
  818:         title: str,
  819:         rank: int,
  820:     ) -> float:
  821: 
  822:         stopwords = {
  823:             "the",
  824:             "a",
  825:             "an",
  826:             "and",
  827:             "or",
  828:             "of",
  829:             "to",
  830:             "in",
  831:             "on",
  832:             "for",
  833:             "how",
  834:             "why",
  835:             "what",
  836:             "is",
  837:             "are",
  838:         }
  839: 
  840:         query_words = {
  841:             word
  842:             for word in re.findall(
  843:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  844:                 query.lower(),
  845:             )
  846:             if (
  847:                 len(word) >= 3
  848:                 and word not in stopwords
  849:             )
  850:         }
  851: 
  852:         title_words = set(
  853:             re.findall(
  854:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  855:                 title.lower(),
  856:             )
  857:         )
  858: 
  859:         overlap = len(
  860:             query_words & title_words
  861:         )
  862: 
  863:         if not query_words:
  864:             semantic = 0.0
  865:         else:
  866:             semantic = (
  867:                 overlap
  868:                 / len(query_words)
  869:             )
  870: 
  871:         rank_bonus = max(
  872:             0.0,
  873:             18.0 - (rank - 1) * 1.5,
  874:         )
  875: 
  876:         return min(
  877:             100.0,
  878:             60.0
  879:             + semantic * 30.0
  880:             + rank_bonus,
  881:         )
  882: 
  883: 
  884: 
  885: 
  886:

--- MovieContextualQueryBuilder.build @ line 114 ---
  110:         ),

  113:     @classmethod
  114:     def build(
  115:         cls,
  116:         *,
  117:         visual_query: str,
  118:         purpose: str = "",
  119:     ) -> list[str]:
  120: 
  121:         text = (
  122:             f"{visual_query} {purpose}"
  123:             .lower()
  124:         )
  125: 
  126:         queries: list[str] = []
  127: 
  128:         for triggers, fallbacks in cls.RULES:
  129: 
  130:             if any(
  131:                 trigger in text
  132:                 for trigger in triggers
  133:             ):
  134:                 queries.extend(
  135:                     fallbacks
  136:                 )
  137: 
  138:         ####################################################
  139:         # Purpose-aware fallbacks
  140:         ####################################################
  141: 
  142:         if purpose == "filmmaker_context":
  143:             queries.extend(
  144:                 [
  145:                     "film director on set",
  146:                     "filmmaking camera crew",
  147:                     "movie production set",
  148:                 ]
  149:             )
  150: 
  151:         elif purpose == "technical_detail":
  152:             queries.extend(
  153:                 [
  154:                     "visual effects workstation",
  155:                     "digital compositing film",
  156:                     "computer graphics production",
  157:                 ]
  158:             )
  159: 
  160:         elif purpose == "supporting_visual":
  161:             queries.extend(
  162:                 [
  163:                     "cinematic abstract geometry",
  164:                     "surreal architecture",
  165:                     "film visual effects production",
  166:                 ]
  167:             )
  168: 
  169:         elif purpose in {
  170:             "hook_subject",
  171:             "hook_detail",
  172:         }:
  173:             queries.extend(
  174:                 [
  175:                     "surreal cinematic architecture",
  176:                     "dramatic geometric city",
  177:                 ]
  178:             )
  179: 
  180:         elif purpose in {
  181:             "payoff",
  182:             "closing_visual",
  183:         }:
  184:             queries.extend(
  185:                 [
  186:                     "cinematic city perspective",
  187:                     "abstract film visual effects",
  188:                 ]
  189:             )
  190: 
  191:         ####################################################
  192:         # Normalize + deduplicate
  193:         ####################################################
  194: 
  195:         output: list[str] = []
  196:         seen: set[str] = set()
  197: 
  198:         for query in queries:
  199: 
  200:             clean = re.sub(
  201:                 r"\s+",
  202:                 " ",
  203:                 str(query),
  204:             ).strip()
  205: 
  206:             key = clean.lower()
  207: 
  208:             if (
  209:                 not clean
  210:                 or key in seen
  211:             ):
  212:                 continue
  213: 
  214:             seen.add(key)
  215:             output.append(clean)
  216: 
  217:             if len(output) >= cls.MAX_QUERIES:
  218:                 break
  219: 
  220:         return output

  258:                                 asset=asset,
  259:                                 query=query,
  260:                                 beat=beat,
  261:                                 movie_title=movie_title,
  262:                             )
  263: 
  264:                             valid, issues = (
  265:                                 self.asset_collector.validate_asset(
  266:                                     asset,
  267:                                     content_id=clean_content_id,
  268:                                 )
  269:                             )
  270: 
  271:                             if not valid:
  272:                                 continue
  273: 
  274:                             if not self._passes_identity_policy(
  275:                                 asset=asset,
  276:                                 beat=beat,
  277:                                 query=query,
  278:                                 movie_title=movie_title,
  279:                             ):
  280:                                 continue
  281: 
  282:                             visual_validation = await asyncio.to_thread(
  283:                                 self.visual_semantic_validator.validate,
  284:                                 asset=asset,
  285:                                 beat=beat,
  286:                                 movie_title=movie_title,
  287:                             )
  288: 
  289:                             if not visual_validation["valid"]:
  290:                                 continue
  291: 
  292:                             quality_validation = await asyncio.to_thread(
  293:                                 self.visual_quality_validator.validate,
  294:                                 asset=asset,
  295:                             )
  296: 
  297:                             if not quality_validation["valid"]:
  298:                                 continue
  299: 
  300: 
  301:                             fallback_candidates.append(
  302:                                 asset
  303:                             )
  304: 
  305:                     # Stop after the first fallback query that
  306:                     # yields authorized candidates. Unlike the
  307:                     # primary pool, this stage exists only to
  308:                     # recover an otherwise unresolved beat.
  309:                     if fallback_candidates:
  310:                         break
  311: 
  312:                 winner = self._select_best(
  313:                     fallback_candidates
  314:                 )
  315: 
  316: 
  317:             if winner is None:
  318: 
  319:                 resolved.append(
  320:                     ResolvedMovieBeat(
  321:                         beat_id=beat.beat_id,
  322:                         narration_index=(
  323:                             beat.narration_index
  324:                         ),
  325:                         purpose=beat.purpose,
  326:                         visual_query=beat.visual_query,
  327:                         target_duration=(
  328:                             beat.target_duration
  329:                         ),
  330:                         evidence_ids=list(
  331:                             beat.evidence_ids
  332:                         ),
  333:                         asset=None,
  334:                         status="unresolved",
  335:                         attempted_queries=queries,
  336:                     )
  337:                 )
  338: 
  339:                 continue
  340: 
  341:             used_source_urls.add(
  342:                 winner.source_url
  343:             )
  344: 
  345:             resolved.append(
  346:                 ResolvedMovieBeat(
  347:                     beat_id=beat.beat_id,
  348:                     narration_index=(
  349:                         beat.narration_index
  350:                     ),
  351:                     purpose=beat.purpose,
  352:                     visual_query=beat.visual_query,
  353:                     target_duration=(
  354:                         beat.target_duration
  355:                     ),
  356:                     evidence_ids=list(
  357:                         beat.evidence_ids
  358:                     ),
  359:                     asset=winner,
  360:                     status="resolved",
  361:                     attempted_queries=queries,
  362:                 )
  363:             )
  364: 
  365:         return resolved

  367:     @staticmethod
  368:     def _passes_identity_policy(
  369:         *,
  370:         asset: MediaAsset,
  371:         beat: MovieVisualBeat,
  372:         query: str,
  373:         movie_title: str,
  374:     ) -> bool:
  375:         """
  376:         Prevent generic stock media from impersonating a specific
  377:         filmmaker or named movie identity.
  378: 
  379:         Pexels may still satisfy these beats through contextual
  380:         fallback queries such as "film director on set".
  381:         """
  382: 
  383:         if (
  384:             str(
  385:                 asset.source_name
  386:             ).strip().lower()
  387:             != "pexels"
  388:         ):
  389:             return True
  390: 
  391:         clean_query = " ".join(
  392:             str(query).lower().split()
  393:         )
  394: 
  395:         clean_movie = " ".join(
  396:             str(movie_title).lower().split()
  397:         )
  398: 
  399:         visual_text = " ".join(
  400:             str(
  401:                 beat.visual_query
  402:             ).lower().split()
  403:         )
  404: 
  405:         identity_words = {
  406:             "director",
  407:             "directing",
  408:             "filmmaker",
  409:         }
  410: 
  411:         has_identity_intent = any(
  412:             word in visual_text
  413:             for word in identity_words
  414:         )
  415: 
  416:         if (
  417:             has_identity_intent
  418:             and clean_movie
  419:             and clean_movie in clean_query
  420:         ):
  421:             return False
  422: 
  423:         return True
  424:

--- MovieMediaResolver._apply_query_relevance @ line 426 ---

  423:         return True
  424: 
  425:     @staticmethod
  426:     def _apply_query_relevance(
  427:         *,
  428:         asset: MediaAsset,
  429:         query: str,
  430:         beat: MovieVisualBeat,
  431:         movie_title: str,
  432:     ) -> None:
  433:         """
  434:         Apply resolver-level retrieval relevance.
  435: 
  436:         Direct movie-specific matches receive the strongest
  437:         retrieval confidence.
  438: 
  439:         Contextual Pexels results may enter the candidate pool,
  440:         but do not outrank stronger direct matches merely because
  441:         they are video.
  442:         """
  443: 
  444:         current = float(
  445:             asset.relevance_score
  446:             or 0.0
  447:         )
  448: 
  449:         clean_query = " ".join(
  450:             str(query).lower().split()
  451:         )
  452: 
  453:         clean_visual = " ".join(
  454:             str(
  455:                 beat.visual_query
  456:             ).lower().split()
  457:         )
  458: 
  459:         clean_movie = " ".join(
  460:             str(movie_title).lower().split()
  461:         )
  462: 
  463:         score = current
  464: 
  465:         ####################################################
  466:         # Exact planned visual request
  467:         ####################################################
  468: 
  469:         if (
  470:             clean_visual
  471:             and clean_query == clean_visual
  472:         ):
  473:             score = max(
  474:                 score,
  475:                 78.0,
  476:             )
  477: 
  478:         ####################################################
  479:         # Movie-specific retrieval
  480:         ####################################################
  481: 
  482:         elif (
  483:             clean_movie
  484:             and clean_movie in clean_query
  485:         ):
  486:             score = max(
  487:                 score,
  488:                 72.0,
  489:             )
  490: 
  491:         ####################################################
  492:         # Contextual provider retrieval
  493:         ####################################################
  494: 
  495:         if (
  496:             str(
  497:                 asset.source_name
  498:             ).strip().lower()
  499:             == "pexels"
  500:         ):
  501:             score = max(
  502:                 score,
  503:                 68.0,
  504:             )
  505: 
  506:         ####################################################
  507:         # Purpose/query alignment
  508:         ####################################################
  509: 
  510:         purpose_terms = {
  511:             "hook_subject": {
  512:                 "film",
  513:                 "mirror",
  514:                 "dimension",
  515:                 "cinematic",
  516:                 "surreal",
  517:             },
  518:             "hook_detail": {
  519:                 "visual",
  520:                 "effects",
  521:                 "geometry",
  522:                 "surreal",
  523:                 "cinematic",
  524:             },
  525:             "filmmaker_context": {
  526:                 "director",
  527:                 "film",
  528:                 "production",
  529:                 "camera",
  530:                 "filmmaking",
  531:             },
  532:             "explanation": {
  533:                 "production",
  534:                 "dimension",
  535:                 "action",
  536:                 "film",
  537:                 "behind",
  538:             },
  539:             "technical_detail": {
  540:                 "visual",
  541:                 "effects",
  542:                 "vfx",
  543:                 "compositing",
  544:                 "graphics",
  545:             },
  546:             "supporting_visual": {
  547:                 "visual",
  548:                 "effects",
  549:                 "production",
  550:                 "surreal",
  551:                 "geometry",
  552:             },
  553:             "payoff": {
  554:                 "cinematic",
  555:                 "action",
  556:                 "film",
  557:                 "city",
  558:                 "visual",
  559:             },
  560:             "closing_visual": {
  561:                 "visual",
  562:                 "effects",
  563:                 "cinematic",
  564:                 "dimension",
  565:                 "geometry",
  566:             },
  567:         }
  568: 
  569:         expected = purpose_terms.get(
  570:             beat.purpose,
  571:             set(),
  572:         )
  573: 
  574:         query_words = set(
  575:             clean_query.split()
  576:         )
  577: 
  578:         overlap = len(
  579:             expected.intersection(
  580:                 query_words
  581:             )
  582:         )
  583: 
  584:         if overlap >= 2:
  585:             score += 4.0
  586: 
  587:         elif overlap == 1:
  588:             score += 2.0
  589: 
  590:         asset.relevance_score = round(
  591:             min(
  592:                 score,
  593:                 95.0,
  594:             ),
  595:             1,
  596:         )

  598:     @staticmethod
  599:     def _select_best(
  600:         candidates: list[MediaAsset],
  601:     ) -> MediaAsset | None:


--- MovieMediaResolver._select_best @ line 599 ---
  595:             1,
  596:         )
  597: 
  598:     @staticmethod
  599:     def _select_best(
  600:         candidates: list[MediaAsset],
  601:     ) -> MediaAsset | None:
  602: 
  603:         if not candidates:
  604:             return None
  605: 
  606:         ranked = sorted(
  607:             candidates,
  608:             key=lambda asset: (
  609:                 ################################################
  610:                 # Relevance is always the primary criterion.
  611:                 ################################################
  612:                 float(
  613:                     asset.relevance_score
  614:                     or 0.0
  615:                 ),
  616: 
  617:                 ################################################
  618:                 # Prefer motion only after relevance.
  619:                 ################################################
  620:                 asset.asset_type == "video",
  621: 
  622:                 ################################################
  623:                 # Portrait assets fit Shorts better.
  624:                 ################################################
  625:                 (
  626:                     (asset.height or 0)
  627:                     >
  628:                     (asset.width or 0)
  629:                 ),
  630: 
  631:                 ################################################
  632:                 # Resolution is only a final tie-breaker.
  633:                 ################################################
  634:                 (
  635:                     (asset.width or 0)
  636:                     *
  637:                     (asset.height or 0)
  638:                 ),
  639:             ),
  640:             reverse=True,
  641:         )
  642: 
  643:         return ranked[0]
  644:

  646:     def _query_variants(
  647:         *,
  648:         beat: MovieVisualBeat,


--- MovieMediaResolver._query_variants @ line 646 ---
  642: 
  643:         return ranked[0]

  645:     @staticmethod
  646:     def _query_variants(
  647:         *,
  648:         beat: MovieVisualBeat,
  649:         movie_title: str,
  650:     ) -> list[str]:
  651: 
  652:         ####################################################
  653:         # Tier 1: direct movie/context searches
  654:         ####################################################
  655: 
  656:         primary = " ".join(
  657:             beat.visual_query.split()
  658:         ).strip()
  659: 
  660:         direct_queries = [
  661:             primary,
  662:         ]
  663: 
  664:         purpose_queries = {
  665:             "hook_subject": [
  666:                 f"{movie_title} film",
  667:                 f"{movie_title} promotional image",
  668:             ],
  669:             "hook_detail": [
  670:                 f"{movie_title} visual effects",
  671:                 f"{movie_title} production still",
  672:             ],
  673:             "filmmaker_context": [
  674:                 f"{movie_title} director",
  675:                 f"{movie_title} production",
  676:             ],
  677:             "explanation": [
  678:                 f"{movie_title} behind the scenes",
  679:                 f"{movie_title} production still",
  680:             ],
  681:             "technical_detail": [
  682:                 f"{movie_title} visual effects",
  683:                 f"{movie_title} VFX production",
  684:             ],
  685:             "supporting_visual": [
  686:                 f"{movie_title} production image",
  687:                 f"{movie_title} film still",
  688:             ],
  689:             "payoff": [
  690:                 f"{movie_title} film",
  691:                 f"{movie_title} promotional image",
  692:             ],
  693:             "closing_visual": [
  694:                 f"{movie_title} visual effects",
  695:                 f"{movie_title} production still",
  696:             ],
  697:         }
  698: 
  699:         direct_queries.extend(
  700:             purpose_queries.get(
  701:                 beat.purpose,
  702:                 [],
  703:             )
  704:         )
  705: 
  706:         ####################################################
  707:         # Tier 2: contextual licensed B-roll
  708:         ####################################################
  709: 
  710:         contextual_queries = (
  711:             MovieContextualQueryBuilder.build(
  712:                 visual_query=beat.visual_query,
  713:                 purpose=beat.purpose,
  714:             )
  715:         )
  716: 
  717:         ####################################################
  718:         # Preserve order:
  719:         #
  720:         # direct movie relevance first,
  721:         # broader contextual fallback second.
  722:         ####################################################
  723: 
  724:         queries = (
  725:             direct_queries
  726:             + contextual_queries
  727:         )
  728: 
  729:         output = []
  730:         seen = set()
  731: 
  732:         for query in queries:
  733: 
  734:             clean = " ".join(
  735:                 str(query).split()
  736:             ).strip()
  737: 
  738:             key = clean.lower()
  739: 
  740:             if (
  741:                 not clean
  742:                 or key in seen
  743:             ):
  744:                 continue
  745: 
  746:             seen.add(key)
  747:             output.append(clean)
  748: 
  749:         return output
  750:

   33: 
   34: class SourceClipIndexer:
   35:     """
   36:     Convert source videos into short selectable visual moments.
   37: 
   38:     The indexer does not render the final Short.
   39:     It identifies candidate shot windows that ClipMatcher can later
   40:     rank against narration/visual beats.
   41:     """
   42: 
   43:     MIN_CLIP_DURATION = 1.0
   44:     TARGET_CLIP_DURATION = 2.0

  107:     def index_asset(
  108:         self,
  109:         *,
  110:         asset: MediaAsset,
  111:         content_id: str,
  112:     ) -> list[IndexedSourceClip]:
  113: 
  114:         source = Path(
  115:             asset.file_path
  116:         )
  117: 
  118:         if not source.exists():
  119:             return []
  120: 
  121:         duration = self._duration(
  122:             source
  123:         )
  124: 
  125:         if duration <= 0:
  126:             return []
  127: 
  128:         boundaries = self._scene_boundaries(
  129:             source
  130:         )
  131: 
  132:         windows = self._build_windows(
  133:             duration=duration,
  134:             boundaries=boundaries,
  135:         )
  136: 
  137:         output_dir = (
  138:             self.output_root
  139:             / content_id
  140:             / asset.asset_id
  141:         )
  142: 
  143:         output_dir.mkdir(
  144:             parents=True,
  145:             exist_ok=True,
  146:         )
  147: 
  148:         clips: list[
  149:             IndexedSourceClip
  150:         ] = []
  151: 
  152:         for index, (
  153:             start,
  154:             end,
  155:         ) in enumerate(
  156:             windows[
  157:                 :self.MAX_CLIPS_PER_ASSET
  158:             ],
  159:             start=1,
  160:         ):
  161: 
  162:             preview = (
  163:                 output_dir
  164:                 / f"clip_{index:03d}.jpg"
  165:             )
  166: 
  167:             midpoint = (
  168:                 start
  169:                 + (
  170:                     end - start
  171:                 )
  172:                 / 2.0
  173:             )
  174: 
  175:             if not self._extract_frame(
  176:                 source=source,
  177:                 timestamp=midpoint,
  178:                 output=preview,
  179:             ):
  180:                 continue
  181: 
  182:             clip_id = (
  183:                 f"{asset.asset_id}_"
  184:                 f"{index:03d}"
  185:             )
  186: 
  187:             clips.append(
  188:                 IndexedSourceClip(
  189:                     clip_id=clip_id,
  190:                     asset_id=asset.asset_id,
  191:                     source_path=str(source),
  192:                     source_name=(
  193:                         asset.source_name
  194:                     ),
  195:                     start_time=round(
  196:                         start,
  197:                         3,
  198:                     ),
  199:                     end_time=round(
  200:                         end,
  201:                         3,
  202:                     ),
  203:                     duration=round(
  204:                         end - start,
  205:                         3,
  206:                     ),
  207:                     preview_path=str(
  208:                         preview
  209:                     ),
  210:                     width=asset.width,
  211:                     height=asset.height,
  212:                 )
  213:             )
  214: 
  215:         return clips

--- SourceClipIndexer._scene_boundaries @ line 257 ---
  253: 
  254:         except Exception:
  255:             return 0.0
  256: 
  257:     def _scene_boundaries(
  258:         self,
  259:         source: Path,
  260:     ) -> list[float]:
  261: 
  262:         ####################################################
  263:         # FFmpeg prints scene-change timestamps through
  264:         # showinfo. We only use these as candidate boundaries.
  265:         ####################################################
  266: 
  267:         filter_value = (
  268:             "select="
  269:             f"'gt(scene,{self.SCENE_THRESHOLD})',"
  270:             "showinfo"
  271:         )
  272: 
  273:         command = [
  274:             self.ffmpeg,
  275:             "-hide_banner",
  276:             "-i",
  277:             str(source),
  278:             "-vf",
  279:             filter_value,
  280:             "-an",
  281:             "-f",
  282:             "null",
  283:             "-",
  284:         ]
  285: 
  286:         try:
  287: 
  288:             completed = subprocess.run(
  289:                 command,
  290:                 stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
  291:                 stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  292:                 text=True,
  293:                 check=False,
  294:                 timeout=180,
  295:             )
  296: 
  297:         except Exception:
  298:             return []
  299: 
  300:         matches = re.findall(
  301:             r"pts_time:([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)",
  302:             completed.stderr,
  303:         )
  304: 
  305:         values = []
  306: 
  307:         for item in matches:
  308: 
  309:             try:
  310:                 value = float(
  311:                     item
  312:                 )
  313:             except ValueError:
  314:                 continue
  315: 
  316:             if value > 0:
  317:                 values.append(
  318:                     value
  319:                 )
  320: 
  321:         return sorted(
  322:             set(values)
  323:         )
  324: 
  325:     def _build_windows(
  326:         self,
  327:         *,
  328:         duration: float,


--- SourceClipIndexer._build_windows @ line 325 ---
  321:         return sorted(
  322:             set(values)
  323:         )
  324: 
  325:     def _build_windows(
  326:         self,
  327:         *,
  328:         duration: float,
  329:         boundaries: list[float],
  330:     ) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
  331: 
  332:         ####################################################
  333:         # Prefer real scene boundaries.
  334:         ####################################################
  335: 
  336:         points = [
  337:             0.0,
  338:             *[
  339:                 value
  340:                 for value in boundaries
  341:                 if (
  342:                     0.0 < value < duration
  343:                 )
  344:             ],
  345:             duration,
  346:         ]
  347: 
  348:         windows: list[
  349:             tuple[float, float]
  350:         ] = []
  351: 
  352:         for index in range(
  353:             len(points) - 1
  354:         ):
  355: 
  356:             start = points[index]
  357:             stop = points[index + 1]
  358: 
  359:             scene_duration = (
  360:                 stop - start
  361:             )
  362: 
  363:             if (
  364:                 scene_duration
  365:                 < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  366:             ):
  367:                 continue
  368: 
  369:             current = start
  370: 
  371:             while current < stop:
  372: 
  373:                 remaining = (
  374:                     stop - current
  375:                 )
  376: 
  377:                 if (
  378:                     remaining
  379:                     < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  380:                 ):
  381:                     break
  382: 
  383:                 clip_duration = min(
  384:                     self.TARGET_CLIP_DURATION,
  385:                     remaining,
  386:                     self.MAX_CLIP_DURATION,
  387:                 )
  388: 
  389:                 end = min(
  390:                     current
  391:                     + clip_duration,
  392:                     stop,
  393:                 )
  394: 
  395:                 if (
  396:                     end - current
  397:                     >= self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  398:                 ):
  399:                     windows.append(
  400:                         (
  401:                             current,
  402:                             end,
  403:                         )
  404:                     )
  405: 
  406:                 current = end
  407: 
  408:         ####################################################
  409:         # Some videos contain very few detectable cuts.
  410:         # Fall back to uniform windows rather than producing
  411:         # an empty index.
  412:         ####################################################
  413: 
  414:         if len(windows) < 4:
  415: 
  416:             windows = []
  417: 
  418:             current = 0.0
  419: 
  420:             while (
  421:                 current
  422:                 < duration
  423:                 and len(windows)
  424:                 < self.MAX_CLIPS_PER_ASSET
  425:             ):
  426: 
  427:                 end = min(
  428:                     current
  429:                     + self.TARGET_CLIP_DURATION,
  430:                     duration,
  431:                 )
  432: 
  433:                 if (
  434:                     end - current
  435:                     < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  436:                 ):
  437:                     break
  438: 
  439:                 windows.append(
  440:                     (
  441:                         current,
  442:                         end,
  443:                     )
  444:                 )
  445: 
  446:                 current = end
  447: 
  448:         return windows
  449: 
  450:     def _extract_frame(
  451:         self,
  452:         *,
  453:         source: Path,

   43: class SourceFootageResolver:
   44:     """
   45:     Resolve real/topic-specific reusable footage before generic B-roll.
   46: 
   47:     Priority:
   48:         1. Wikimedia Commons
   49:         2. Internet Archive
   50:         3. Pexels contextual fallback
   51: 
   52:     Unauthorized scraping/downloading is deliberately outside this
   53:     resolver.
   54:     """
   55: 
   56:     PRIMARY_LIMIT_PER_QUERY = 2
   57:     FALLBACK_LIMIT_PER_QUERY = 2
   58: 
   59:     def __init__(
   60:         self,
   61:         *,
   62:         primary_providers=None,
   63:         fallback_providers=None,
   64:         asset_collector=None,
   65:     ) -> None:
   66: 
   67:         self.primary_providers = (
   68:             primary_providers
   69:             if primary_providers is not None
   70:             else [
   71:                 WikimediaCommonsProvider(),
   72:                 InternetArchiveProvider(),
   73:             ]
   74:         )
   75: 
   76:         if fallback_providers is not None:
   77: 
   78:             self.fallback_providers = (

   80:             )

   83:

   85: 
   86:             try:
   87: 
   88:                 self.fallback_providers.append(
   89:                     PexelsProvider()
   90:                 )
   91: 
   92:             except RuntimeError as exc:
   93: 
   94:                 print(
   95:                     "Pexels fallback unavailable: "
   96:                     f"{exc}"
   97:                 )
   98: 
   99:         self.asset_collector = (
  100:             asset_collector
  101:             if asset_collector is not None

   85:     def match_many(
   86:         self,
   87:         *,
   88:         beats: list[SourceVisualBeat],
   89:         clips: list[IndexedSourceClip],
   90:         max_reuse_per_source: int = 10,
   91:     ) -> list[StrictMatchedClip]:
   92: 
   93:         if not beats or not clips:
   94:             return []
   95: 
   96:         self._ensure_model()
   97: 
   98:         valid_clips = []
   99:         embeddings = []
  100: 
  101:         for clip in clips:
  102: 
  103:             embedding = (
  104:                 self._image_embedding(
  105:                     clip.preview_path
  106:                 )
  107:             )
  108: 
  109:             if embedding is None:
  110:                 continue
  111: 
  112:             valid_clips.append(
  113:                 clip
  114:             )
  115: 
  116:             embeddings.append(
  117:                 embedding
  118:             )
  119: 
  120:         if not valid_clips:
  121:             return []
  122: 
  123:         image_matrix = torch.cat(
  124:             embeddings,
  125:             dim=0,
  126:         )
  127: 
  128:         used_clip_ids: set[str] = set()
  129: 
  130:         source_usage: dict[
  131:             str,
  132:             int,
  133:         ] = {}
  134: 
  135:         output = []
  136: 
  137:         for beat in beats:
  138: 
  139:             positive_prompts = [
  140:                 beat.visual_goal,
  141:                 *beat.search_queries,
  142:             ]
  143: 
  144:             positive_embedding = (
  145:                 self._text_embedding(
  146:                     positive_prompts
  147:                 )
  148:             )
  149: 
  150:             positive_scores = (
  151:                 positive_embedding
  152:                 @ image_matrix.T
  153:             ).squeeze(0)
  154: 
  155:             negative_scores = torch.zeros_like(
  156:                 positive_scores
  157:             )
  158: 
  159:             if beat.negative_visuals:
  160: 
  161:                 negative_embedding = (
  162:                     self._text_embedding(
  163:                         beat.negative_visuals
  164:                     )
  165:                 )
  166: 
  167:                 negative_scores = (
  168:                     negative_embedding
  169:                     @ image_matrix.T
  170:                 ).squeeze(0)
  171: 
  172:             final_scores = (
  173:                 positive_scores
  174:                 - (
  175:                     negative_scores
  176:                     * self.NEGATIVE_WEIGHT
  177:                 )
  178:             )
  179: 
  180:             ranked = torch.argsort(
  181:                 final_scores,
  182:                 descending=True,
  183:             ).tolist()
  184: 
  185:             winner = None
  186: 
  187:             for index in ranked:
  188: 
  189:                 clip = valid_clips[
  190:                     index
  191:                 ]
  192: 
  193:                 positive = float(
  194:                     positive_scores[
  195:                         index
  196:                     ].item()
  197:                 )
  198: 
  199:                 negative = float(
  200:                     negative_scores[
  201:                         index
  202:                     ].item()
  203:                 )
  204: 
  205:                 final = float(
  206:                     final_scores[
  207:                         index
  208:                     ].item()
  209:                 )
  210: 
  211:                 if (
  212:                     positive
  213:                     < self.MIN_POSITIVE_SCORE
  214:                 ):
  215:                     continue
  216: 
  217:                 if (
  218:                     final
  219:                     < self.MIN_FINAL_SCORE
  220:                 ):
  221:                     continue
  222: 
  223:                 if (
  224:                     clip.clip_id
  225:                     in used_clip_ids
  226:                 ):
  227:                     continue
  228: 
  229:                 if (
  230:                     source_usage.get(
  231:                         clip.source_path,
  232:                         0,
  233:                     )
  234:                     >= max_reuse_per_source
  235:                 ):
  236:                     continue
  237: 
  238:                 winner = (
  239:                     clip,
  240:                     positive,
  241:                     negative,
  242:                     final,
  243:                 )
  244: 
  245:                 break
  246: 
  247:             if winner is None:
  248:                 continue
  249: 
  250:             clip, positive, negative, final = (
  251:                 winner
  252:             )
  253: 
  254:             used_clip_ids.add(
  255:                 clip.clip_id
  256:             )
  257: 
  258:             source_usage[
  259:                 clip.source_path
  260:             ] = (
  261:                 source_usage.get(
  262:                     clip.source_path,
  263:                     0,
  264:                 )
  265:                 + 1
  266:             )
  267: 
  268:             output.append(
  269:                 StrictMatchedClip(
  270:                     beat_id=beat.beat_id,
  271:                     visual_goal=(
  272:                         beat.visual_goal
  273:                     ),
  274:                     clip_id=(
  275:                         clip.clip_id
  276:                     ),
  277:                     source_path=(
  278:                         clip.source_path
  279:                     ),
  280:                     source_name=(
  281:                         clip.source_name
  282:                     ),
  283:                     start_time=(
  284:                         clip.start_time
  285:                     ),
  286:                     end_time=(
  287:                         clip.end_time
  288:                     ),
  289:                     duration=(
  290:                         clip.duration
  291:                     ),
  292:                     preview_path=(
  293:                         clip.preview_path
  294:                     ),
  295:                     positive_score=round(
  296:                         positive,
  297:                         4,
  298:                     ),
  299:                     negative_score=round(
  300:                         negative,
  301:                         4,
  302:                     ),
  303:                     final_score=round(
  304:                         final,
  305:                         4,
  306:                     ),
  307:                 )
  308:             )
  309: 
  310:         return output

  339:     def _image_embedding(
  340:         self,
  341:         path_value: str,
  342:     ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
  343: 
  344:         cached = self.image_cache.get(
  345:             path_value
  346:         )
  347: 
  348:         if cached is not None:
  349:             return cached
  350: 
  351:         path = Path(
  352:             path_value
  353:         )
  354: 
  355:         if not path.exists():
  356:             return None
  357: 
  358:         try:
  359: 
  360:             image = Image.open(
  361:                 path
  362:             ).convert(
  363:                 "RGB"
  364:             )
  365: 
  366:             tensor = (
  367:                 self.preprocess(
  368:                     image
  369:                 )
  370:                 .unsqueeze(0)
  371:                 .to(
  372:                     self.device
  373:                 )
  374:             )
  375: 
  376:             with torch.no_grad():
  377: 
  378:                 features = (
  379:                     self.model.encode_image(
  380:                         tensor
  381:                     )
  382:                 )
  383: 
  384:                 features = (
  385:                     features
  386:                     / features.norm(
  387:                         dim=-1,
  388:                         keepdim=True,
  389:                     )
  390:                 )
  391: 
  392:             features = (
  393:                 features.cpu()
  394:             )
  395: 
  396:             self.image_cache[
  397:                 path_value
  398:             ] = features
  399: 
  400:             return features
  401: 
  402:         except Exception:
  403:             return None
  404:

  406:         self,
  407:         prompts: list[str],
  408:     ) -> torch.Tensor:

   88:             if verifier is not None

   92:     async def resolve(
   93:         self,
   94:         *,
   95:         beat: SourceVisualBeat,
   96:         content_id: str,
   97:     ) -> BeatResolutionResult:
   98: 
   99:         attempted_queries = []
  100:         rejection_reasons = []
  101: 
  102:         for query_index, query in enumerate(
  103:             beat.search_queries[
  104:                 :self.MAX_QUERIES
  105:             ],
  106:             start=1,
  107:         ):
  108: 
  109:             attempted_queries.append(
  110:                 query
  111:             )
  112: 
  113:             assets: list[
  114:                 MediaAsset
  115:             ] = []
  116: 
  117:             ################################################
  118:             # Retrieve real subject-specific footage.
  119:             ################################################
  120: 
  121:             for provider in self.providers:
  122: 
  123:                 try:
  124: 
  125:                     found = await provider.search_and_download(
  126:                         query=query,
  127:                         content_id=(
  128:                             f"{content_id}-"
  129:                             f"{beat.beat_id}-"
  130:                             f"q{query_index}"
  131:                         ),
  132:                         limit=(
  133:                             self.RESULTS_PER_QUERY
  134:                         ),
  135:                     )
  136: 
  137:                 except Exception as exc:
  138: 
  139:                     rejection_reasons.append(
  140:                         (
  141:                             f"{provider.__class__.__name__}:"
  142:                             f"provider_error:"
  143:                             f"{type(exc).__name__}"
  144:                         )
  145:                     )
  146: 
  147:                     continue
  148: 
  149:                 for asset in found:
  150: 
  151:                     if asset.asset_type != "video":
  152:                         continue
  153: 
  154:                     if (
  155:                         asset.commercial_use_allowed
  156:                         is not True
  157:                     ):
  158:                         continue
  159: 
  160:                     assets.append(
  161:                         asset
  162:                     )
  163: 
  164:             if not assets:
  165:                 rejection_reasons.append(
  166:                     f"{query}:no_video_assets"
  167:                 )
  168:                 continue
  169: 
  170:             ################################################
  171:             # Index every candidate video into short shots.
  172:             ################################################
  173: 
  174:             clips: list[
  175:                 IndexedSourceClip
  176:             ] = []
  177: 
  178:             for asset in assets:
  179: 
  180:                 clips.extend(
  181:                     self.indexer.index_asset(
  182:                         asset=asset,
  183:                         content_id=(
  184:                             f"{content_id}-"
  185:                             f"{beat.beat_id}-"
  186:                             f"q{query_index}"
  187:                         ),
  188:                     )
  189:                 )
  190: 
  191:             if not clips:
  192:                 rejection_reasons.append(
  193:                     f"{query}:no_indexed_clips"
  194:                 )
  195:                 continue
  196: 
  197:             ################################################
  198:             # Rank clips against this exact beat.
  199:             ################################################
  200: 
  201:             matches = (
  202:                 self.matcher.match_many(
  203:                     beats=[
  204:                         beat
  205:                     ],
  206:                     clips=clips,
  207:                     max_reuse_per_source=20,
  208:                 )
  209:             )
  210: 
  211:             if not matches:
  212:                 rejection_reasons.append(
  213:                     f"{query}:no_semantic_match"
  214:                 )
  215:                 continue
  216: 
  217:             ################################################
  218:             # Do not trust the top CLIP match yet.
  219:             # Hard-check visual identity.
  220:             ################################################
  221: 
  222:             for match in matches:
  223: 
  224:                 required_prompts = [
  225:                     beat.visual_goal,
  226:                     *beat.search_queries,
  227:                 ]
  228: 
  229:                 reject_prompts = [
  230:                     *beat.negative_visuals,
  231:                     "generic unrelated machinery",
  232:                     "unrelated household object",
  233:                     "generic stock footage",
  234:                 ]
  235: 
  236:                 verification = (
  237:                     self.verifier.verify(
  238:                         image_path=(
  239:                             match.preview_path
  240:                         ),
  241:                         required_prompts=(
  242:                             required_prompts
  243:                         ),
  244:                         reject_prompts=(
  245:                             reject_prompts
  246:                         ),
  247:                     )
  248:                 )
  249: 
  250:                 if verification.valid:
  251: 
  252:                     return BeatResolutionResult(
  253:                         beat_id=(
  254:                             beat.beat_id
  255:                         ),
  256:                         status="resolved",
  257:                         match=match,
  258:                         attempted_queries=(
  259:                             attempted_queries
  260:                         ),
  261:                         rejection_reasons=(
  262:                             rejection_reasons
  263:                         ),
  264:                     )
  265: 
  266:                 rejection_reasons.append(
  267:                     (
  268:                         f"{query}:"
  269:                         f"{match.clip_id}:"
  270:                         f"{verification.reason}:"
  271:                         f"pos={verification.positive_score}:"
  272:                         f"neg={verification.negative_score}:"
  273:                         f"margin={verification.margin}"
  274:                     )
  275:                 )
  276: 
  277:         ####################################################
  278:         # Fail honestly. Renderer must never fill this beat
  279:         # with an unrelated visual.
  280:         ####################################################
  281: 
  282:         return BeatResolutionResult(
  283:             beat_id=beat.beat_id,
  284:             status=(
  285:                 "required_visual_not_found"
  286:             ),
  287:             match=None,
  288:             attempted_queries=(
  289:                 attempted_queries
  290:             ),
  291:             rejection_reasons=(
  292:                 rejection_reasons
  293:             ),
  294:         )

   89: def final_master_qa_v18(

   94:     """
   95:     Final benchmark gate.
   96: 
   97:     V18 deliberately does NOT destroy the chronology-safe V14/V15
   98:     architecture. It audits the final sequence as a viewer would:
   99: 
  100:         hook
  101:         visual quality
  102:         semantic relevance
  103:         pacing / motion
  104:         chronology
  105:         story progression
  106:         payoff
  107:         narration grounding
  108:         captions
  109:     """
  110: 
  111:     print()

  114:     )
  115: 
  116:     if not moments:
  117: 
  118:         raise RuntimeError(
  119:             "V18: no optimized moments."
  120:         )
  121: 
  122:     render_scores = []
  123:     semantic_scores = []
  124:     motion_scores = []
  125:     timestamps = []
  126: 
  127:     weak_render = []
  128:     weak_semantic = []
  129: 
  130:     for index, moment in enumerate(moments):

  134:             "render_score_v10",
  135:             "render_score",
  136:             "render",

  144:             "semantic_v9",

  149:             moment,
  150:             "motion",
  151:             "motion_score",
  152:             default=0.40,
  153:         )
  154: 
  155:         timestamp = _v18_number(
  156:             moment,
  157:             "timestamp",
  158:             "center",
  159:             "time",
  160:             default=0.0,
  161:         )
  162: 
  163:         render_scores.append(render)
  164:         semantic_scores.append(semantic)
  165:         motion_scores.append(motion)
  166:         timestamps.append(timestamp)
  167: 
  168:         if render < V18_MIN_RENDER:
  169:             weak_render.append(index + 1)
  170: 
  171:         if semantic < V18_MIN_SEMANTIC:
  172:             weak_semantic.append(index + 1)
  173: 
  174:     avg_render = _v18_mean(render_scores)
  175:     avg_semantic = _v18_mean(semantic_scores)
  176:     avg_motion = _v18_mean(motion_scores)
  177: 
  178:     phases = _v18_phase_ranges(
  179:         len(moments)
  180:     )
  181: 
  182:     phase_metrics = {}
  183: 
  184:     for phase, (start, end) in phases.items():
  185: 
  186:         phase_metrics[phase] = {
  187:             "semantic": _v18_mean(
  188:                 semantic_scores[start:end]
  189:             ),
  190:             "render": _v18_mean(
  191:                 render_scores[start:end]
  192:             ),
  193:             "motion": _v18_mean(
  194:                 motion_scores[start:end]
  195:             ),
  196:         }
  197: 
  198:     hook = phase_metrics["HOOK"]
  199:     payoff = phase_metrics["PAYOFF"]
  200: 
  201:     backward_jumps = []
  202: 
  203:     for i in range(
  204:         1,
  205:         len(timestamps),
  206:     ):
  207: 
  208:         delta = (
  209:             timestamps[i]
  210:             - timestamps[i - 1]
  211:         )
  212: 
  213:         if delta < -18.0:
  214:

  222: 
  223:     narration_words = re.findall(

  226:     )

  229:         0.0,

  293:     claim_support = 0.0

  295: 
  296:     if isinstance(

  299:     ):

--- VisualBeatPlanner.plan @ line 35 ---
   31: 
   32:     def __init__(self) -> None:
   33:         self.query_builder = MediaQueryBuilder()
