   35:     def plan(
   36:         self,
   37:         *,
   38:         content: GeneratedContent,
   39:         topic: str,
   40:         genre: str,
   41:         format_name: str,
   42:     ) -> list[VisualBeat]:
   43: 
   44:         lines = list(
   45:             content.script_lines
   46:         )
   47: 
   48:         if not lines:
   49:             raise RuntimeError(
   50:                 "Cannot build visual beats from empty script."
   51:             )
   52: 
   53:         beats: list[VisualBeat] = []
   54:         used_queries: set[str] = set()
   55: 
   56:         for narration_index, line in enumerate(
   57:             lines,
   58:             start=1,
   59:         ):
   60: 
   61:             queries = self._queries_for_line(
   62:                 topic=topic,
   63:                 genre=genre,
   64:                 line=line,
   65:             )
   66: 
   67:             # Reference-style story stages:
   68:             # hook receives the densest visual treatment,
   69:             # followed by context, escalation, and payoff.
   70:             beat_allocation = {
   71:                 1: 4,
   72:                 2: 3,
   73:                 3: 3,
   74:                 4: 2,
   75:             }
   76: 
   77:             per_line = beat_allocation.get(
   78:                 narration_index,
   79:                 2,
   80:             )
   81: 
   82:             selected_queries = []
   83: 
   84:             for query in queries:
   85: 
   86:                 key = query.lower().strip()
   87: 
   88:                 if (
   89:                     not key
   90:                     or key in used_queries
   91:                 ):
   92:                     continue
   93: 
   94:                 used_queries.add(key)
   95:                 selected_queries.append(
   96:                     query
   97:                 )
   98: 
   99:                 if len(
  100:                     selected_queries
  101:                 ) >= per_line:
  102:                     break
  103: 
  104:             for local_index, query in enumerate(
  105:                 selected_queries,
  106:                 start=1,
  107:             ):
  108: 
  109:                 beats.append(
  110:                     VisualBeat(
  111:                         beat_id=(
  112:                             f"n{narration_index}"
  113:                             f"_b{local_index}"
  114:                         ),
  115:                         narration_index=narration_index,
  116:                         purpose=self._purpose(
  117:                             narration_index=narration_index,
  118:                             local_index=local_index,
  119:                         ),
  120:                         search_query=query,
  121:                         preferred_media=(
  122:                             self._preferred_media(
  123:                                 format_name
  124:                             )
  125:                         ),
  126:                         target_duration=(
  127:                             self._target_duration(
  128:                                 format_name
  129:                             )
  130:                         ),
  131:                         emphasis=(
  132:                             "high"
  133:                             if narration_index == 1
  134:                             else "normal"
  135:                         ),
  136:                     )
  137:                 )
  138: 
  139:         beats = beats[
  140:             :self.MAX_BEATS
  141:         ]
  142: 
  143:         if len(beats) < self.MIN_BEATS:
  144:             beats = self._pad_beats(

  154:     def _queries_for_line(
  155:         self,
  156:         *,
  157:         topic: str,
  158:         genre: str,
  159:         line: str,
  160:     ) -> list[str]:
  161: 
  162:         queries = (
  163:             self.query_builder.build(
  164:                 topic=topic,
  165:                 genre=genre,
  166:                 narration=line,
  167:             )
  168:         )
  169: 
  170:         ####################################################
  171:         # Add narration-specific concrete noun phrases.
  172:         ####################################################
  173: 
  174:         words = re.findall(
  175:             r"[A-Za-z0-9]+",
  176:             line.lower(),
  177:         )
  178: 
  179:         stopwords = {
  180:             "this",
  181:             "that",
  182:             "with",
  183:             "from",
  184:             "their",
  185:             "they",
  186:             "have",
  187:             "more",
  188:             "into",
  189:             "because",
  190:             "about",
  191:             "which",
  192:             "while",
  193:             "were",
  194:             "used",
  195:             "using",
  196:             "designed",
  197:             "helps",
  198:             "help",
  199:             "proper",
  200:             "ensure",
  201:             "extremely",
  202:         }
  203: 
  204:         useful = [
  205:             word
  206:             for word in words
  207:             if (
  208:                 len(word) >= 4
  209:                 and word not in stopwords
  210:             )
  211:         ]
  212: 
  213:         phrase_candidates = []
  214: 
  215:         if len(useful) >= 2:
  216:             phrase_candidates.append(
  217:                 " ".join(
  218:                     useful[:2]
  219:                 )
  220:             )
  221: 
  222:         if len(useful) >= 3:
  223:             phrase_candidates.append(
  224:                 " ".join(
  225:                     useful[:3]
  226:                 )
  227:             )
  228: 
  229:         if len(useful) >= 4:
  230:             phrase_candidates.append(
  231:                 " ".join(
  232:                     useful[-3:]
  233:                 )
  234:             )
  235: 
  236:         output = []
  237: 
  238:         ####################################################
  239:         # Prefer concrete alias/media queries over full topic.
  240:         ####################################################
  241: 
  242:         for query in [
  243:             *queries,
  244:             *phrase_candidates,
  245:         ]:
  246: 
  247:             clean = " ".join(
  248:                 str(query).split()
  249:             ).strip()
  250: 
  251:             if not clean:
  252:                 continue
  253: 
  254:             if clean.lower() == topic.lower():
  255:                 continue
  256: 
  257:             if clean not in output:
  258:                 output.append(clean)
  259: 
  260:         return output
  261:

  379:     def _pad_beats(
  380:         self,
  381:         *,
  382:         beats: list[VisualBeat],
  383:         topic: str,
  384:         genre: str,
  385:         format_name: str,
  386:         used_queries: set[str],
  387:     ) -> list[VisualBeat]:
  388: 
  389:         result = list(beats)
  390: 
  391:         fallback_queries = (
  392:             self.query_builder.build(
  393:                 topic=topic,
  394:                 genre=genre,
  395:                 narration=topic,
  396:             )
  397:         )
  398: 
  399:         for query in fallback_queries:
  400: 
  401:             if len(result) >= self.MIN_BEATS:
  402:                 break
  403: 
  404:             clean = query.strip()
  405: 
  406:             if (
  407:                 not clean
  408:                 or clean.lower()
  409:                 in used_queries
  410:                 or clean.lower()
  411:                 == topic.lower()
  412:             ):
  413:                 continue
  414: 
  415:             used_queries.add(
  416:                 clean.lower()
  417:             )
  418: 
  419:             result.append(
  420:                 VisualBeat(
  421:                     beat_id=(
  422:                         f"extra_b"
  423:                         f"{len(result)+1}"
  424:                     ),
  425:                     narration_index=(
  426:                         (len(result) % 4) + 1
  427:                     ),
  428:                     purpose="supporting_detail",
  429:                     search_query=clean,
  430:                     preferred_media=(
  431:                         self._preferred_media(
  432:                             format_name
  433:                         )
  434:                     ),
  435:                     target_duration=(
  436:                         self._target_duration(
  437:                             format_name
  438:                         )
  439:                     ),
  440:                     emphasis="normal",
  441:                 )
  442:             )
  443: 
  444:         return result

  142:             )
  143:

  188:     def _image_embedding(
  189:         self,
  190:         image_path: str,
  191:     ):
  192: 
  193:         cached = self._image_cache.get(
  194:             image_path
  195:         )
  196: 
  197:         if cached is not None:
  198:             return cached
  199: 
  200:         path = Path(
  201:             image_path
  202:         )
  203: 
  204:         if not path.exists():
  205:             return None
  206: 
  207:         try:
  208: 
  209:             image = Image.open(
  210:                 path
  211:             ).convert(
  212:                 "RGB"
  213:             )
  214: 
  215:             tensor = (
  216:                 self.preprocess(
  217:                     image
  218:                 )
  219:                 .unsqueeze(0)
  220:                 .to(
  221:                     self.device
  222:                 )
  223:             )
  224: 
  225:             with torch.no_grad():
  226: 
  227:                 features = (
  228:                     self.model.encode_image(
  229:                         tensor
  230:                     )
  231:                 )
  232: 
  233:                 features = (
  234:                     features
  235:                     / features.norm(
  236:                         dim=-1,
  237:                         keepdim=True,
  238:                     )
  239:                 )
  240: 
  241:             features = features.cpu()
  242: 
  243:             self._image_cache[
  244:                 image_path
  245:             ] = features
  246: 
  247:             return features
  248: 
  249:         except Exception:
  250:             return None
  251:

   52:     def validate(
   53:         self,
   54:         *,
   55:         asset: MediaAsset,
   56:     ) -> dict:
   57: 
   58:         frame_paths = self._sample_frames(
   59:             asset
   60:         )
   61: 
   62:         if not frame_paths:
   63:             return {
   64:                 "valid": False,
   65:                 "reason": "no_quality_frames",
   66:                 "frames": [],
   67:             }
   68: 
   69:         frame_results = []
   70: 
   71:         try:
   72: 
   73:             for frame_path in frame_paths:
   74: 
   75:                 result = self._analyze_frame(
   76:                     frame_path
   77:                 )

   80:                     result
   81:                 )

   83:         finally:
   84:

   87:                 for frame_path in frame_paths:
   88: 
   89:                     try:
   90:                         frame_path.unlink(
   91:                             missing_ok=True
   92:                         )
   93:                     except OSError:
   94:                         pass
   95: 
   96:         usable_count = sum(
   97:             1
   98:             for item in frame_results
   99:             if item["valid"]
  100:         )
  101: 
  102:         ####################################################
  103:         # Images require their only frame to pass.
  104:         # Videos pass if at least 2 of 3 sampled frames pass.
  105:         ####################################################
  106: 
  107:         required = (
  108:             1
  109:             if asset.asset_type == "image"
  110:             else min(
  111:                 2,
  112:                 len(frame_results),
  113:             )
  114:         )
  115: 
  116:         valid = (
  117:             usable_count >= required
  118:         )
  119: 
  120:         averages = self._averages(
  121:             frame_results
  122:         )
  123: 
  124:         return {
  125:             "valid": valid,
  126:             "reason": (
  127:                 "visual_quality_ok"
  128:                 if valid
  129:                 else "visual_quality_failed"
  130:             ),
  131:             "usable_frames": usable_count,
  132:             "required_frames": required,
  133:             "averages": averages,
  134:             "frames": frame_results,

--- VisualQualityValidator._analyze_frame @ line 137 ---
  133:             "averages": averages,
  134:             "frames": frame_results,
  135:         }
  136: 
  137:     def _analyze_frame(
  138:         self,
  139:         path: Path,
  140:     ) -> dict:
  141: 
  142:         try:
  143: 
  144:             with Image.open(
  145:                 path
  146:             ) as image:
  147: 
  148:                 image = image.convert(
  149:                     "RGB"
  150:                 )
  151: 
  152:                 # Downsample for predictable QA cost.
  153:                 image.thumbnail(
  154:                     (720, 720),
  155:                     Image.Resampling.LANCZOS,
  156:                 )
  157: 
  158:                 gray = image.convert(
  159:                     "L"
  160:                 )
  161: 
  162:                 array = np.asarray(
  163:                     gray,
  164:                     dtype=np.float32,
  165:                 )
  166: 
  167:                 if array.size == 0:
  168:                     return self._failed_frame(
  169:                         "empty_frame"
  170:                     )
  171: 
  172:                 brightness = float(
  173:                     array.mean()
  174:                 )
  175: 
  176:                 contrast = float(
  177:                     array.std()
  178:                 )
  179: 
  180:                 dark_ratio = float(
  181:                     np.mean(
  182:                         array <= 18.0
  183:                     )
  184:                 )
  185: 
  186:                 bright_ratio = float(
  187:                     np.mean(
  188:                         array >= 245.0
  189:                     )
  190:                 )
  191: 
  192:                 ################################################
  193:                 # Edge activity:
  194:                 # low value usually means blur, blankness,
  195:                 # extreme darkness, or low-information imagery.
  196:                 ################################################
  197: 
  198:                 edges = gray.filter(
  199:                     ImageFilter.FIND_EDGES
  200:                 )
  201: 
  202:                 edge_array = np.asarray(
  203:                     edges,
  204:                     dtype=np.float32,
  205:                 )
  206: 
  207:                 edge_activity = float(
  208:                     edge_array.std()
  209:                 )
  210: 
  211:                 issues = []
  212: 
  213:                 ################################################
  214:                 # Dark imagery may still be visually usable.
  215:                 #
  216:                 # Title cards, logos, night shots, and high-
  217:                 # contrast cinematic frames can be intentionally
  218:                 # dark while still containing strong structure.
  219:                 ################################################
  220: 
  221:                 dark_but_informative = (
  222:                     contrast >= 25.0
  223:                     and edge_activity >= 25.0
  224:                 )
  225: 
  226:                 if (
  227:                     brightness
  228:                     < self.MIN_BRIGHTNESS
  229:                     and not dark_but_informative
  230:                 ):
  231:                     issues.append(
  232:                         "too_dark"
  233:                     )
  234: 
  235:                 if (
  236:                     brightness
  237:                     > self.MAX_BRIGHTNESS
  238:                 ):
  239:                     issues.append(
  240:                         "too_bright"
  241:                     )
  242: 
  243:                 if (
  244:                     dark_ratio
  245:                     > self.MAX_DARK_RATIO
  246:                     and not dark_but_informative
  247:                 ):
  248:                     issues.append(
  249:                         "mostly_black"
  250:                     )
  251: 
  252:                 if (
  253:                     bright_ratio
  254:                     > self.MAX_BRIGHT_RATIO
  255:                 ):
  256:                     issues.append(
  257:                         "mostly_white"
  258:                     )
  259: 
  260:                 if (
  261:                     contrast
  262:                     < self.MIN_CONTRAST
  263:                 ):
  264:                     issues.append(
  265:                         "low_contrast"
  266:                     )
  267: 
  268:                 if (
  269:                     edge_activity
  270:                     < self.MIN_EDGE_ACTIVITY
  271:                 ):
  272:                     issues.append(
  273:                         "low_visual_information"
  274:                     )
  275: 
  276:                 return {
  277:                     "valid": not issues,
  278:                     "brightness": round(
  279:                         brightness,
  280:                         2,
  281:                     ),
  282:                     "contrast": round(
  283:                         contrast,
  284:                         2,
  285:                     ),
  286:                     "dark_ratio": round(
  287:                         dark_ratio,
  288:                         4,
  289:                     ),
  290:                     "bright_ratio": round(
  291:                         bright_ratio,
  292:                         4,
  293:                     ),
  294:                     "edge_activity": round(
  295:                         edge_activity,
  296:                         2,
  297:                     ),
  298:                     "issues": issues,
  299:                 }
  300: 
  301:         except Exception as exc:
  302: 
  303:             return self._failed_frame(
  304:                 "frame_analysis_error",
  305:                 detail=str(exc),
  306:             )

  314:             asset.file_path
  315:         )

  318:             return []
  319: 
  320:         if asset.asset_type == "image":
  321:             return [
  322:                 source
  323:             ]
  324: 
  325:         if asset.asset_type != "video":
  326:             return []
  327: 
  328:         duration = float(
  329:             asset.duration
  330:             or 0.0
  331:         )

  333:         ####################################################

  335:         # early/middle-ish timestamps.
  336:         ####################################################

  348:

  352:                 0.25,
  353:                 0.75,
  354:                 1.25,
  355:             ]
  356: 
  357:         output_paths = []
  358: 
  359:         for index, timestamp in enumerate(
  360:             timestamps,
  361:             start=1,
  362:         ):
  363: 
  364:             output = (
  365:                 Path(
  366:                     tempfile.gettempdir()
  367:                 )
  368:                 / (
  369:                     "jarvis_quality_"

  371:                     f"{index}.jpg"
  372:                 )
  373:             )

  376:                 self.ffmpeg_binary,
  377:                 "-y",
  378:                 "-loglevel",
  379:                 "error",

  381:                 str(
  382:                     round(
  383:                         timestamp,
  384:                         3,
  385:                     )

  390:                 "1",
  391:                 "-q:v",
  392:                 "3",
  393:                 str(output),
  394:             ]
  395: 
  396:             try:
  397: 
  398:                 completed = subprocess.run(
  399:                     command,
  400:                     stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
  401:                     stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
  402:                     check=False,
  403:                     timeout=45,
  404:                 )

  406:             except (
  407:                 OSError,
  408:                 subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
  409:             ):

  411:

  413:                 completed.returncode == 0
  414:                 and output.exists()

  417:                     output

--- VisualSemanticValidator._similarity @ line 127 ---
  123:                 )
  124:             ),

  126: 
  127:     def _similarity(
  128:         self,
  129:         *,
  130:         image_path: Path,
  131:         beat: MovieVisualBeat,
  132:         movie_title: str,
  133:     ) -> float:
  134: 
  135:         self._ensure_model()
  136: 
  137:         image = Image.open(
  138:             image_path
  139:         ).convert(
  140:             "RGB"
  141:         )
  142: 
  143:         image_tensor = (
  144:             self.preprocess(
  145:                 image
  146:             )
  147:             .unsqueeze(0)
  148:             .to(self.device)
  149:         )
  150: 
  151:         prompts = self._text_prompts(
  152:             beat=beat,
  153:             movie_title=movie_title,
  154:         )
  155: 
  156:         text_tokens = (
  157:             self.tokenizer(
  158:                 prompts
  159:             )
  160:             .to(self.device)
  161:         )
  162: 
  163:         with torch.no_grad():
  164: 
  165:             image_features = (
  166:                 self.model.encode_image(
  167:                     image_tensor
  168:                 )
  169:             )
  170: 
  171:             text_features = (
  172:                 self.model.encode_text(
  173:                     text_tokens
  174:                 )
  175:             )
  176: 
  177:             image_features = (
  178:                 image_features
  179:                 / image_features.norm(
  180:                     dim=-1,
  181:                     keepdim=True,
  182:                 )
  183:             )
  184: 
  185:             text_features = (
  186:                 text_features
  187:                 / text_features.norm(
  188:                     dim=-1,
  189:                     keepdim=True,
  190:                 )
  191:             )
  192: 
  193:             similarities = (
  194:                 image_features
  195:                 @ text_features.T
  196:             )
  197: 
  198:         best = float(
  199:             similarities.max().item()
  200:         )
  201: 
  202:         if math.isnan(best):
  203:             return 0.0
  204: 
  205:         return best
  206:

   63:     async def render(
   64:         self,
   65:         *,
   66:         title: str,
   67:         indexed_clips: list[IndexedSourceClip],
   68:     ) -> dict:
   69: 
   70:         timeline = (
   71:             LuxuryReferenceTimeline.shots()
   72:         )
   73: 
   74:         if len(indexed_clips) < len(timeline):
   75:             raise RuntimeError(
   76:                 "Luxury benchmark needs at least "
   77:                 f"{len(timeline)} indexed clips; "
   78:                 f"received {len(indexed_clips)}."
   79:             )
   80: 
   81:         ####################################################
   82:         # Spread selections across the source instead of
   83:         # consuming only the first 30 clips.
   84:         ####################################################
   85: 
   86:         selected = self._spread_select(
   87:             indexed_clips,
   88:             count=len(timeline),
   89:         )
   90: 
   91:         rendered_clips = []
   92:         opened_sources = []
   93: 
   94:         try:
   95: 
   96:             for shot, indexed in zip(
   97:                 timeline,
   98:                 selected,
   99:             ):
  100: 
  101:                 source_path = Path(
  102:                     indexed.source_path
  103:                 )
  104: 
  105:                 if not source_path.exists():
  106:                     raise FileNotFoundError(
  107:                         source_path
  108:                     )
  109: 
  110:                 source = VideoFileClip(
  111:                     str(source_path)
  112:                 )
  113: 
  114:                 opened_sources.append(
  115:                     source
  116:                 )
  117: 
  118:                 target_duration = float(
  119:                     shot.duration
  120:                 )
  121: 
  122:                 ################################################
  123:                 # Start from the indexed semantically useful
  124:                 # moment but allow longer benchmark shots to
  125:                 # continue through the original source.
  126:                 ################################################
  127: 
  128:                 start = max(
  129:                     0.0,
  130:                     float(
  131:                         indexed.start_time
  132:                     ),
  133:                 )
  134: 
  135:                 if (
  136:                     start + target_duration
  137:                     > source.duration
  138:                 ):
  139:                     start = max(
  140:                         0.0,
  141:                         source.duration
  142:                         - target_duration
  143:                     )
  144: 
  145:                 end = min(
  146:                     source.duration,
  147:                     start + target_duration,
  148:                 )
  149: 
  150:                 actual_duration = (
  151:                     end - start
  152:                 )
  153: 
  154:                 if actual_duration <= 0:
  155:                     continue
  156: 
  157:                 clip = source.subclipped(
  158:                     start,
  159:                     end,
  160:                 )
  161: 
  162:                 ################################################
  163:                 # Fill the 1080x2400 reference canvas.
  164:                 ################################################
  165: 
  166:                 scale = max(
  167:                     LuxuryReferenceTimeline.WIDTH
  168:                     / clip.w,
  169:                     LuxuryReferenceTimeline.HEIGHT
  170:                     / clip.h,
  171:                 )
  172: 
  173:                 clip = clip.resized(
  174:                     scale
  175:                 )
  176: 
  177:                 ################################################
  178:                 # Controlled punch-in rhythm.
  179:                 #
  180:                 # This is deliberately subtle for premium
  181:                 # product footage.
  182:                 ################################################
  183: 
  184:                 pattern = (
  185:                     1.00,
  186:                     1.035,
  187:                     1.00,
  188:                     1.055,
  189:                     1.02,
  190:                 )
  191: 
  192:                 zoom = pattern[
  193:                     (shot.index - 1)
  194:                     % len(pattern)
  195:                 ]
  196: 
  197:                 if zoom != 1.0:
  198:                     clip = clip.resized(
  199:                         zoom
  200:                     )

  202:                 clip = clip.cropped(
  203:                     x_center=(
  204:                         clip.w / 2
  205:                     ),
  206:                     y_center=(
  207:                         clip.h / 2
  208:                     ),
  209:                     width=(
  210:                         LuxuryReferenceTimeline.WIDTH
  211:                     ),
  212:                     height=(
  213:                         LuxuryReferenceTimeline.HEIGHT
  214:                     ),
  215:                 )
  216: 
  217:                 clip = clip.with_duration(
  218:                     actual_duration
  219:                 )
  220: 
  221:                 rendered_clips.append(
  222:                     clip
  223:                 )
  224: 
  225:             if not rendered_clips:
  226:                 raise RuntimeError(
  227:                     "Luxury benchmark produced no clips."
  228:                 )
  229:

  232:                 method="compose",
  233:             )
  234: 
  235:             safe_name = "".join(
  236:                 character
  237:                 if character.isalnum()
  238:                 else "_"
  239:                 for character in title
  240:             )[:70]
  241: 
  242:             output = (
  243:                 self.output_dir
  244:                 / (
  245:                     f"{safe_name}_"

  269:                     ),

  277:                     LuxuryReferenceTimeline.FPS

  307:         *,


--- LuxuryBenchmarkRenderer._spread_select @ line 305 ---
  301:                 except Exception:
  302:                     pass
  303: 
  304:     @staticmethod
  305:     def _spread_select(
  306:         clips: list[IndexedSourceClip],
  307:         *,
  308:         count: int,
  309:     ) -> list[IndexedSourceClip]:
  310: 
  311:         if count <= 0:
  312:             return []
  313: 
  314:         if len(clips) < count:
  315:             raise RuntimeError(
  316:                 "Not enough indexed clips."
  317:             )
  318: 
  319:         if count == 1:
  320:             return [
  321:                 clips[
  322:                     len(clips) // 2
  323:                 ]
  324:             ]
  325: 
  326:         indexes = []
  327: 
  328:         for position in range(count):
  329: 
  330:             ratio = (
  331:                 position
  332:                 / (
  333:                     count - 1
  334:                 )
  335:             )
  336: 
  337:             index = round(
  338:                 ratio
  339:                 * (
  340:                     len(clips) - 1
  341:                 )
  342:             )
  343: 
  344:             indexes.append(
  345:                 index
  346:             )
  347: 
  348:         return [
  349:             clips[index]
  350:             for index in indexes
  351:         ]

   55:     async def render(
   56:         self,
   57:         *,

   61:     ) -> dict:
   62: 
   63:         if not matches:
   64:             raise RuntimeError(
   65:                 "MatchedClipRenderer received no matched clips."
   66:             )
   67: 
   68:         clips = []
   69:         opened_sources = []
   70: 
   71:         try:
   72: 
   73:             for index, match in enumerate(
   74:                 matches
   75:             ):
   76: 
   77:                 source_path = Path(
   78:                     match.source_path
   79:                 )
   80: 
   81:                 if not source_path.exists():
   82:                     continue
   83: 
   84:                 source = VideoFileClip(
   85:                     str(source_path)
   86:                 )
   87: 
   88:                 opened_sources.append(
   89:                     source
   90:                 )
   91: 
   92:                 start = max(
   93:                     0.0,
   94:                     float(

   96:                     ),
   97:                 )
   98:

  100:                     float(
  101:                         match.end_time
  102:                     ),
  103:                     float(
  104:                         source.duration
  105:                     ),
  106:                 )
  107: 
  108:                 if end <= start:
  109:                     continue
  110: 
  111:                 clip = source.subclipped(
  112:                     start,
  113:                     end,
  114:                 )
  115: 
  116:                 ################################################
  117:                 # Vertical 9:16 reframe.
  118:                 ################################################
  119: 
  120:                 scale = max(
  121:                     profile.width / clip.w,
  122:                     profile.height / clip.h,
  123:                 )
  124: 
  125:                 clip = clip.resized(
  126:                     scale
  127:                 )
  128: 
  129:                 ################################################
  130:                 # Light deterministic zoom variation.
  131:                 ################################################
  132: 
  133:                 if (
  134:                     profile.zoom_enabled
  135:                     and index % 3 == 1
  136:                 ):
  137:                     clip = clip.resized(
  138:                         1.035
  139:                     )
  140: 
  141:                 elif (
  142:                     profile.zoom_enabled
  143:                     and index % 3 == 2
  144:                 ):
  145:                     clip = clip.resized(
  146:                         1.06
  147:                     )
  148: 
  149:                 clip = clip.cropped(
  150:                     x_center=clip.w / 2,
  151:                     y_center=clip.h / 2,
  152:                     width=profile.width,
  153:                     height=profile.height,
  154:                 )
  155: 
  156:                 clips.append(
  157:                     clip
  158:                 )

  161:                 raise RuntimeError(
  162:                     "No matched clips were renderable."
  163:                 )
  164:

  166:                 clips,
  167:                 method="compose",
  168:             )
  169:

  171:                 c if c.isalnum()
  172:                 else "_"
  173:                 for c in title
  174:             )[:70]

  176:             output = (
  177:                 self.output_dir

   52:     async def render(
   53:         self,
   54:         *,

   58:     ) -> dict:
   59:

   61:             raise ValueError(
   62:                 "MovieClipRenderer requires a video asset."
   63:             )
   64: 
   65:         source_path = Path(
   66:             asset.file_path
   67:         )

   71:                 source_path
   72:             )
   73: 
   74:         source = VideoFileClip(
   75:             str(source_path)
   76:         )
   77: 
   78:         clips = []
   79:         final = None
   80:         caption = None
   81: 
   82:         try:
   83: 
   84:             if source.duration <= 0:
   85:                 raise RuntimeError(
   86:                     "Movie source has invalid duration."
   87:                 )
   88: 
   89:             target = min(
   90:                 self.TARGET_DURATION,
   91:                 float(source.duration),
   92:             )
   93: 
   94:             ################################################
   95:             # Select a segment away from opening titles
   96:             ################################################
   97: 
   98:             available_start = max(
   99:                 0.0,
  100:                 source.duration - target,
  101:             )
  102: 
  103:             start = min(
  104:                 max(
  105:                     source.duration * 0.15,
  106:                     0.0,
  107:                 ),
  108:                 available_start,
  109:             )
  110: 
  111:             end = start + target
  112: 
  113:             ################################################
  114:             # Split into short visual beats
  115:             ################################################
  116: 
  117:             current = start
  118:             shot_index = 0
  119: 
  120:             while current < end:
  121: 
  122:                 shot_end = min(
  123:                     current
  124:                     + self.SHOT_DURATION,
  125:                     end,
  126:                 )
  127: 
  128:                 clip = source.subclipped(
  129:                     current,
  130:                     shot_end,
  131:                 )
  132: 
  133:                 ################################################
  134:                 # Fill 9:16 vertically.
  135:                 ################################################
  136: 
  137:                 scale = max(
  138:                     self.WIDTH / clip.w,
  139:                     self.HEIGHT / clip.h,
  140:                 )
  141: 
  142:                 clip = clip.resized(
  143:                     scale
  144:                 )
  145: 
  146:                 clip = clip.cropped(
  147:                     x_center=clip.w / 2,
  148:                     y_center=clip.h / 2,
  149:                     width=self.WIDTH,
  150:                     height=self.HEIGHT,
  151:                 )
  152: 
  153:                 ################################################
  154:                 # Alternate framing slightly between cuts.
  155:                 ################################################
  156: 
  157:                 if shot_index % 2 == 1:
  158:                     clip = clip.resized(
  159:                         1.04
  160:                     )
  161: 
  162:                     clip = clip.cropped(
  163:                         x_center=clip.w / 2,
  164:                         y_center=clip.h / 2,
  165:                         width=self.WIDTH,
  166:                         height=self.HEIGHT,
  167:                     )
  168: 
  169:                 clips.append(
  170:                     clip
  171:                 )
  172: 
  173:                 shot_index += 1
  174:                 current = shot_end
  175: 
  176:             if not clips:
  177:                 raise RuntimeError(
  178:                     "Movie renderer produced no shots."
  179:                 )
  180: 
  181:             base = concatenate_videoclips(
  182:                 clips,
  183:                 method="compose",
  184:             )
  185:

  187:             # Hook caption in Shorts-safe zone
  188:             ################################################

  209:                     )
  210:                 )

  213:                         "center",
  214:                         self.CAPTION_Y,
  215:                     )
  216:                 )
  217:             )
  218: 
  219:             final = CompositeVideoClip(
  220:                 [
  221:                     base,
  222:                     caption,
  223:                 ],
  224:                 size=(
  225:                     self.WIDTH,
  226:                     self.HEIGHT,
  227:                 ),
  228:             )
  229: 
  230:             safe_name = "".join(
  231:                 c if c.isalnum()
  232:                 else "_"
  233:                 for c in title
  234:             )[:60]
  235: 
  236:             output = (
  237:                 self.output_dir
  238:                 / f"{safe_name}_movie_short.mp4"
  239:             )
  240: 
  241:             final.write_videofile(
  242:                 str(output),

  244:                 codec="libx264",
  245:                 audio_codec="aac",
  246:                 logger=None,
  247:             )
  248:

   71:     def _cover_image_clip(
   72:         self,
   73:         image_path: Path,
   74:         duration: float,
   75:         zoom: float = 1.0,
   76:     ):
   77:         """
   78:         Preserve source aspect ratio while filling the
   79:         vertical frame. Overscan is centered instead of
   80:         stretching the source image.
   81:         """
   82: 
   83:         clip = ImageClip(str(image_path))
   84: 
   85:         source_width, source_height = clip.size
   86: 
   87:         if source_width <= 0 or source_height <= 0:
   88:             clip.close()
   89:             raise RuntimeError(
   90:                 "Invalid source image dimensions."
   91:             )
   92: 
   93:         cover_scale = max(
   94:             self.WIDTH / float(source_width),
   95:             self.HEIGHT / float(source_height),
   96:         )
   97: 
   98:         scale = cover_scale * max(1.0, float(zoom))
   99: 
  100:         target_width = max(
  101:             self.WIDTH,
  102:             int(round(source_width * scale)),
  103:         )
  104: 
  105:         target_height = max(
  106:             self.HEIGHT,
  107:             int(round(source_height * scale)),
  108:         )
  109: 
  110:         return (
  111:             clip
  112:             .resized((target_width, target_height))
  113:             .with_duration(max(0.05, float(duration)))
  114:             .with_position(("center", "center"))
  115:         )
  116: 
  117:     async def render(
  118:         self,

  265:             # Render

  267: 
  268:             final.write_videofile(
  269:                 str(output),
  270:                 fps=profile.fps,
  271:                 codec="libx264",
  272:                 audio_codec="aac",
  273:                 logger=None,
  274:             )
  275: 
  276:             final.close()
  277:             base.close()
  278: 
  279:             return {
  280:                 "status": "success",
  281:                 "video_path": str(output),
  282:                 "duration": target_duration,
  283:                 "format_name": (
  284:                     profile.format_name
  285:                 ),
  286:                 "benchmark_reference": (
  287:                     profile.benchmark_reference
  288:                 ),

  290:                     shot_clips
  291:                 ),

  293:                     f"{profile.width}"
  294:                     f"x{profile.height}"
  295:                 ),
  296:                 "fps": profile.fps,
  297:             }
  298: 
  299:         finally:
  300: 
  301:             for clip in shot_clips:
  302: 
  303:                 try:
  304:                     clip.close()
  305:                 except Exception:
  306:                     pass
  307: 
  308:             if narration is not None:
  309:

  313:                     pass
  314:

  319:                 except Exception:

--- RichMultiShotRenderer._normalize_video_for_moviepy @ line 322 ---

  321: 
  322:     def _normalize_video_for_moviepy(
  323:         self,
  324:         source_path: Path,
  325:     ) -> Path:
  326:         """
  327:         Normalize third-party video containers before MoviePy reads
  328:         them.
  329: 
  330:         This removes timecode/data streams and provider metadata that
  331:         can confuse MoviePy's FFmpeg parser, while caching the result
  332:         so repeated renders do not transcode the same asset again.
  333:         """
  334: 
  335:         stat = source_path.stat()
  336: 
  337:         identity = (
  338:             f"{source_path.resolve()}|"
  339:             f"{stat.st_size}|"
  340:             f"{stat.st_mtime_ns}"
  341:         )
  342: 
  343:         digest = hashlib.sha1(
  344:             identity.encode(
  345:                 "utf-8"
  346:             )
  347:         ).hexdigest()[:16]
  348: 
  349:         output = (
  350:             self.normalized_video_dir
  351:             / f"{digest}.mp4"
  352:         )
  353: 
  354:         if (
  355:             output.exists()
  356:             and output.stat().st_size > 0
  357:         ):
  358:             return output
  359: 
  360:         command = [
  361:             self.ffmpeg_executable,
  362:             "-y",
  363: 
  364:             "-i",
  365:             str(source_path),
  366: 
  367:             # Keep only the primary video stream.
  368:             "-map",
  369:             "0:v:0",
  370: 
  371:             # Explicitly remove non-video streams.
  372:             "-an",
  373:             "-sn",
  374:             "-dn",
  375: 
  376:             # Remove timecode/provider metadata.
  377:             "-map_metadata",
  378:             "-1",
  379: 
  380:             "-metadata",
  381:             "timecode=",
  382: 
  383:             # Stable MoviePy-friendly encoding.
  384:             "-c:v",
  385:             "libx264",
  386: 
  387:             "-preset",
  388:             "veryfast",
  389: 
  390:             "-crf",
  391:             "18",
  392: 
  393:             "-pix_fmt",
  394:             "yuv420p",
  395: 
  396:             "-movflags",
  397:             "+faststart",
  398: 
  399:             str(output),
  400:         ]
  401: 
  402:         completed = subprocess.run(
  403:             command,
  404:             stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
  405:             stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  406:             text=True,
  407:             check=False,
  408:             timeout=180,
  409:         )
  410: 
  411:         if (
  412:             completed.returncode != 0
  413:             or not output.exists()
  414:             or output.stat().st_size <= 0
  415:         ):
  416:             raise RuntimeError(
  417:                 "FFmpeg video normalization failed for "
  418:                 f"{source_path}: "
  419:                 f"{completed.stderr[-1500:]}"
  420:             )
  421: 
  422:         return output
  423:

  425:         self,
  426:         *,
  427:         asset: MediaAsset,

  420:             )
  421: 
  422:         return output

  424:     def _build_asset_clip(
  425:         self,
  426:         *,
  427:         asset: MediaAsset,
  428:         duration: float,
  429:         profile: RichFormatProfile,
  430:         shot_index: int,
  431:     ):
  432: 
  433:         source_path = Path(
  434:             asset.file_path
  435:         )
  436: 
  437:         if not source_path.exists():
  438:             raise FileNotFoundError(
  439:                 source_path
  440:             )
  441: 
  442:         if asset.asset_type == "video":
  443: 
  444:             normalized_path = (
  445:                 self._normalize_video_for_moviepy(
  446:                     source_path
  447:                 )
  448:             )
  449: 
  450:             source = VideoFileClip(
  451:                 str(normalized_path)
  452:             )
  453: 
  454:             if source.duration <= 0:
  455:                 source.close()
  456: 
  457:                 raise RuntimeError(
  458:                     "Video asset has invalid duration: "
  459:                     f"{source_path}"
  460:                 )
  461: 
  462:             usable_duration = min(
  463:                 duration,
  464:                 source.duration,
  465:             )
  466: 
  467:             ################################################
  468:             # Avoid always starting at frame zero.
  469:             ################################################
  470: 
  471:             max_start = max(
  472:                 0.0,
  473:                 source.duration
  474:                 - usable_duration,
  475:             )
  476: 
  477:             if max_start > 0:
  478: 
  479:                 fraction = (
  480:                     (
  481:                         shot_index * 0.37
  482:                     )
  483:                     % 1.0
  484:                 )
  485: 
  486:                 start = (
  487:                     max_start
  488:                     * fraction
  489:                 )
  490: 
  491:             else:
  492:                 start = 0.0
  493: 
  494:             clip = source.subclipped(
  495:                 start,
  496:                 start + usable_duration,
  497:             )
  498: 
  499:         elif asset.asset_type == "image":
  500: 
  501:             clip = (
  502:                 ImageClip(
  503:                     str(source_path)
  504:                 )
  505:                 .with_duration(
  506:                     duration
  507:                 )
  508:             )
  509: 
  510:         else:
  511: 
  512:             raise ValueError(
  513:                 "Unsupported media type: "
  514:                 f"{asset.asset_type}"
  515:             )
  516: 
  517:         ####################################################
  518:         # 9:16 crop/reframe.
  519:         ####################################################
  520: 
  521:         scale = max(
  522:             profile.width / clip.w,
  523:             profile.height / clip.h,
  524:         )
  525: 
  526:         clip = clip.resized(
  527:             scale
  528:         )
  529: 
  530:         ####################################################
  531:         # Mild zoom variation.
  532:         ####################################################
  533: 
  534:         if (
  535:             profile.zoom_enabled
  536:             and shot_index % 3 == 1
  537:         ):
  538:             clip = clip.resized(
  539:                 1.035
  540:             )
  541: 
  542:         elif (
  543:             profile.zoom_enabled
  544:             and shot_index % 3 == 2
  545:         ):
  546:             clip = clip.resized(
  547:                 1.06
  548:             )
  549: 
  550:         clip = clip.cropped(
  551:             x_center=clip.w / 2,
  552:             y_center=clip.h / 2,
  553:             width=profile.width,
  554:             height=profile.height,
  555:         )
  556: 
  557:         return clip.with_duration(
  558:             duration
  559:         )

   74:     def create_phrase_clips(
   75:         self,
   76:         text: str,
   77:         start_time: float,
   78:         end_time: float,
   79:     ) -> list[TextClip]:
   80: 
   81:         text = text.strip()
   82: 
   83:         if not text:
   84:             raise ValueError(
   85:                 "Subtitle text cannot be empty."
   86:             )
   87: 
   88:         duration = (
   89:             end_time - start_time
   90:         )
   91: 
   92:         if duration <= 0:
   93:             raise ValueError(
   94:                 "Subtitle duration must be positive."
   95:             )
   96: 
   97:         words = text.split()
   98: 
   99:         phrases = [
  100:             words[
  101:                 index:
  102:                 index + self.WORDS_PER_PHRASE
  103:             ]
  104:             for index in range(
  105:                 0,
  106:                 len(words),
  107:                 self.WORDS_PER_PHRASE,
  108:             )
  109:         ]
  110: 
  111:         total_words = len(words)
  112: 
  113:         clips = []
  114: 
  115:         current_time = start_time
  116: 
  117:         for index, phrase_words in enumerate(
  118:             phrases
  119:         ):
  120: 
  121:             phrase_text = " ".join(
  122:                 phrase_words
  123:             )
  124: 
  125:             phrase_duration = (
  126:                 duration
  127:                 * len(phrase_words)
  128:                 / total_words
  129:             )
  130: 
  131:             # Force the final phrase to end exactly
  132:             # at the scene boundary.
  133:             if index == len(phrases) - 1:
  134:                 phrase_end = end_time
  135:             else:
  136:                 phrase_end = (
  137:                     current_time
  138:                     + phrase_duration
  139:                 )
  140: 
  141:             clip = self.create_clip(
  142:                 text=phrase_text,
  143:                 start_time=current_time,
  144:                 end_time=phrase_end,
  145:             )
  146: 
  147:             clips.append(
  148:                 clip
  149:             )
  150: 
  151:             current_time = phrase_end
  152: 
  153:         return clips
  154:

  263: def build_focus_track(
  264:     source,
  265:     *,
  266:     start: float,
  267:     end: float,
  268: ):
  269: 
  270:     if end <= start:
  271: 
  272:         return [
  273:             (
  274:                 0.0,
  275:                 0.5,
  276:             )
  277:         ]
  278: 
  279:     times = np.linspace(
  280:         start,
  281:         end,
  282:         15,
  283:     )
  284: 
  285:     raw = []
  286: 
  287:     for timestamp in times:
  288: 
  289:         frame = source.get_frame(
  290:             float(timestamp)
  291:         ).astype(
  292:             np.uint8
  293:         )
  294: 
  295:         _, focus = composition_score(
  296:             frame
  297:         )
  298: 
  299:         raw.append(
  300:             max(
  301:                 0.12,
  302:                 min(
  303:                     0.88,
  304:                     float(focus),
  305:                 ),
  306:             )
  307:         )
  308: 
  309:     median_values = []
  310: 
  311:     for index in range(
  312:         len(raw)
  313:     ):
  314: 
  315:         left = max(
  316:             0,
  317:             index - 2,
  318:         )
  319: 
  320:         right = min(
  321:             len(raw),
  322:             index + 3,
  323:         )
  324: 
  325:         median_values.append(
  326:             float(
  327:                 np.median(
  328:                     raw[
  329:                         left:right
  330:                     ]
  331:                 )
  332:             )
  333:         )
  334: 
  335:     alpha = 0.36
  336: 
  337:     smooth = [
  338:         median_values[0]
  339:     ]
  340: 
  341:     for value in median_values[1:]:
  342: 
  343:         previous = smooth[-1]
  344: 
  345:         smooth.append(
  346:             previous
  347:             + alpha
  348:             * (
  349:                 value
  350:                 - previous
  351:             )
  352:         )
  353: 
  354:     stable = [
  355:         smooth[0]
  356:     ]
  357: 
  358:     dead_zone = 0.025
  359:     max_step = 0.042
  360: 
  361:     for target in smooth[1:]:
  362: 
  363:         current = stable[-1]
  364:         delta = target - current
  365: 
  366:         if abs(delta) <= dead_zone:
  367:             stable.append(current)
  368:             continue
  369: 
  370:         delta = max(
  371:             -max_step,
  372:             min(
  373:                 max_step,
  374:                 delta,
  375:             ),
  376:         )
  377: 
  378:         stable.append(
  379:             max(
  380:                 0.12,
  381:                 min(
  382:                     0.88,
  383:                     current + delta,
  384:                 ),
  385:             )
  386:         )
  387: 
  388:     return [
  389:         (
  390:             float(timestamp - start),
  391:             float(focus),
  392:         )
  393:         for timestamp, focus
  394:         in zip(
  395:             times,
  396:             stable,
  397:         )
  398:     ]

--- dynamic_crop @ line 401 ---
  397:         )

  400: 
  401: def dynamic_crop(
  402:     clip,
  403:     focus_track,
  404:     *,
  405:     width: int,
  406:     height: int,
  407: ):
  408: 
  409:     track_t = np.array(
  410:         [
  411:             item[0]
  412:             for item
  413:             in focus_track
  414:         ],
  415:         dtype=np.float32,
  416:     )
  417: 
  418:     track_x = np.array(
  419:         [
  420:             item[1]
  421:             for item
  422:             in focus_track
  423:         ],
  424:         dtype=np.float32,
  425:     )
  426: 
  427:     def transform(
  428:         get_frame,
  429:         t,
  430:     ):
  431: 
  432:         frame = np.asarray(
  433:             get_frame(t)
  434:         ).astype(
  435:             np.uint8
  436:         )
  437: 
  438:         h, w = frame.shape[:2]
  439: 
  440:         scale = max(
  441:             width / w,
  442:             height / h,
  443:         )
  444: 
  445:         rw = int(
  446:             np.ceil(
  447:                 w * scale
  448:             )
  449:         )
  450: 
  451:         rh = int(
  452:             np.ceil(
  453:                 h * scale
  454:             )
  455:         )
  456: 
  457:         resized = cv2.resize(
  458:             frame,
  459:             (
  460:                 rw,
  461:                 rh,
  462:             ),
  463:             interpolation=(
  464:                 cv2.INTER_LANCZOS4
  465:             ),
  466:         )
  467: 
  468:         focus = float(
  469:             np.interp(
  470:                 float(t),
  471:                 track_t,
  472:                 track_x,
  473:             )
  474:         )
  475: 
  476:         cx = rw * focus
  477:         half = width / 2
  478: 
  479:         cx = max(
  480:             half,
  481:             min(
  482:                 rw - half,
  483:                 cx,
  484:             ),
  485:         )
  486: 
  487:         x1 = int(
  488:             round(
  489:                 cx - half
  490:             )
  491:         )
  492: 
  493:         x1 = max(
  494:             0,
  495:             min(
  496:                 rw - width,
  497:                 x1,
  498:             ),
  499:         )
  500: 
  501:         y1 = max(
  502:             0,
  503:             int(
  504:                 (
  505:                     rh - height
  506:                 )
  507:                 / 2
  508:             ),
  509:         )
  510: 
  511:         output = resized[
  512:             y1:y1 + height,
  513:             x1:x1 + width,
  514:         ]
  515: 
  516:         if (
  517:             output.shape[0] != height
  518:             or output.shape[1] != width
  519:         ):
  520: 
  521:             output = cv2.resize(
  522:                 output,
  523:                 (
  524:                     width,
  525:                     height,
  526:                 ),
  527:                 interpolation=(
  528:                     cv2.INTER_LANCZOS4
  529:                 ),
  530:             )
  531: 
  532:         return output
  533: 
  534:     return clip.transform(
  535:         transform
  536:     )
